# analyzer.py
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from google import genai
from google.genai import types

class BookClip(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(description="Catchy, high-CTR YouTube Short title under 60 chars")
    start_time: str = Field(description="Start timestamp HH:MM:SS or MM:SS")
    end_time: str = Field(description="End timestamp HH:MM:SS or MM:SS")
    book_title: str = Field(description="The exact title of the book discussed in this clip")
    book_author: str = Field(description="The author of the book (or 'Unknown')")
    hook_caption: str = Field(description="1 punchy sentence summarizing the insight")
    hashtags: list[str] = Field(description="5 viral tags, e.g., ['#Wisdom', '#Books', '#Shorts']")

class AnalysisResponse(BaseModel):
    clips: list[BookClip]

def analyze_video_for_books(client: genai.Client, video_path: Path, max_clips: int = 2) -> list[BookClip]:
    print("\n🧠 [Brain] Uploading video to Gemini File API...")
    video_file = client.files.upload(file=str(video_path))

    while video_file.state.name == "PROCESSING":
        time.sleep(3)
        video_file = client.files.get(name=video_file.name)

    if video_file.state.name == "FAILED":
        raise RuntimeError("Gemini failed to process the video.")

    print("🧠 [Brain] Identifying book recommendations and high-retention moments...")
    prompt = f"""
    You are an elite short-form video editor for a book wisdom channel.
    Analyze this video and extract up to {max_clips} viral moments where a specific book, philosophy, or mental model is explained.

    Requirements:
    - Clip length: strictly 30 to 58 seconds.
    - Identify the EXACT book title and author being discussed.
    - Start right at the punchy hook; stop cleanly after the lesson is complete.
    """

    response = client.models.generate_content(
        model="gemini-2.0-flash",
        contents=[video_file, prompt],
        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
            response_mime_type="application/json",
            response_schema=AnalysisResponse,
            temperature=0.2,
        ),
    )

    try:
        client.files.delete(name=video_file.name)
    except Exception:
        pass

    data = json.loads(response.text)
    return [BookClip(**item) for item in data.get("clips", [])]

@st.cache_data(ttl=2.0, show_spinner=False)
def get_all_sequence_videos():
    return sorted(list(backend.CLIPS_DIR.rglob("*.mp4")), key=lambda p: p.parent.name, reverse=True)

@st.cache_data(ttl=2.0, show_spinner=False)
def get_media_files(folder_path_str: str, exts: tuple):
    folder = Path(folder_path_str)
    if not folder.exists():
        return []
    return sorted([f for f in folder.iterdir() if f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() in exts], key=lambda x: x.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)

@st.cache_data(ttl=15.0, show_spinner=False)
def get_jesus_wallpapers() -> dict:
    backend.ensure_sacred_assets()
    walls = {}
    def_j = backend.get_jesus_image()
    if def_j and def_j.exists():
        walls["Sacred Jesus Default"] = def_j
    for folder in [backend.BG_DIR, getattr(backend, "PIN_IMG_DIR", backend.BG_DIR), backend.THUMB_DIR]:
        if folder and folder.exists():
            for f in get_media_files(str(folder), (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"))[:15]:
                lbl = f"\U0001f5bc\ufe0f {f.name[:30]}"
                if lbl not in walls:
                    walls[lbl] = f
    return walls

@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def _cached_bg_b64(path_str: str, mtime: float) -> str:
    try:
        cache_jpg = backend.BASE_DIR / "assets" / "studio_bg_cached.jpg"
        cache_jpg.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        im = Image.open(path_str).convert("RGB")
        im.thumbnail((1280, 1280), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        im.save(str(cache_jpg), "JPEG", quality=80)
        return base64.b64encode(cache_jpg.read_bytes()).decode("utf-8")
    except Exception:
        return ""

def get_bg_base64(img_path: Path) -> str:
    if not img_path or not img_path.exists():
        return ""
    return _cached_bg_b64(str(img_path), img_path.stat().st_mtime)

@st.cache_data(show_spinner=False)
def cached_video_dur(path_str: str) -> float:
    return backend.get_video_duration(Path(path_str))

@st.cache_data(ttl=15.0, show_spinner=False)
def get_available_bgm_tracks():
    tracks = {
        "Default Worship Piano": backend.get_worship_bgm(),
        "432Hz Cathedral Soaking Pad": lpe.ensure_soaking_worship_pad(),
        "Ambient Pad": backend.BGM_PODCAST_FILE
    }
    if backend.DOWNLOADS_DIR.exists():
        dl_files = sorted(
            [f for f in backend.DOWNLOADS_DIR.glob("*.*") if f.suffix.lower() in (".mp3", ".wav", ".m4a", ".aac")],
            key=lambda x: x.stat().st_mtime,
            reverse=True
        )
        for f in dl_files:
            tracks[f"\U0001f4e5 {f.name[:36]}"] = f
    return tracks

class LiveTaskLoader:
    def __init__(self, title: str = "Studio Engine Running..."):
        self.title = title
        self.t0 = time.time()
        self.box = st.empty()
        self.prog = st.progress(0)
        self.update(5, "Initializing task...")

    def update(self, pct: int, step_msg: str):
        elapsed = time.time() - self.t0
        p_val = min(100, max(0, int(pct)))
        self.box.markdown(
            f'<div class="live-loader-card">'
            f'<div class="live-spinner-ring"></div>'
            f'<div style="flex:1;">'
            f'<div class="live-loader-title">\u26a1 {self.title} \u2014 <span style="color:#F87171;">{p_val}%</span></div>'
            f'<div class="live-loader-sub">{step_msg} &nbsp;\u2022&nbsp; \u23f1\ufe0f {elapsed:.1f}s elapsed</div>'
            f'</div></div>',
            unsafe_allow_html=True
        )
        self.prog.progress(p_val)

    def complete(self, final_msg: str):
        elapsed = time.time() - self.t0
        self.prog.progress(100)
        self.box.success(f"{final_msg} (Completed in {elapsed:.1f}s)")

@st.cache_data(ttl=15.0, show_spinner=False)
def _cached_kpis():
    import json
    vids = get_all_sequence_videos()
    upld = sum(1 for v in vids if v.with_suffix('.json').exists() and json.loads(v.with_suffix('.json').read_bytes()).get("youtube_id"))
    return vids, upld, len(vids) - upld

# ==============================================================================
# REUSABLE CUSTOM THUMBNAIL STUDIO WIDGET
# ==============================================================================
def render_custom_thumbnail_manager(video_path: Path, key_prefix: str) -> Path | None:
    v_dir = video_path.parent
    meta = backend.load_clip_metadata(video_path)
    tp_master = video_path.with_name(f"{video_path.stem}_thumb.jpg")
    custom_up_path = v_dir / "custom_uploaded_thumb.jpg"

    plain_candidates = []
    raw_bg = v_dir / "raw_clean_background.jpg"
    if raw_bg.exists():
        plain_candidates.append(("Plain Used Background (No Text)", raw_bg))
    grace_sc_dir = v_dir / "grace_scenes"
    if grace_sc_dir.exists():
        for s_i in range(1, 4):
            sp = grace_sc_dir / f"scene_{s_i}.jpg"
            if sp.exists():
                plain_candidates.append((f"Plain Scene {s_i} (No Text)", sp))
    if not plain_candidates:
        def_bg = backend.get_jesus_image()
        if def_bg and def_bg.exists():
            plain_candidates.append(("Plain Default Background", def_bg))

    prompt_file = v_dir / "ai_thumbnail_prompt.txt"
    if prompt_file.exists():
        ai_thumb_prompt = prompt_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        import pro_design_addon as pda
        topic_str = meta.get("topic") or meta.get("title", "FAITH OVER FEAR")
        desc_str = meta.get("description", "")
        v_match = re.search(r"Scripture:\s*([^\n]+)", desc_str)
        v_ref_str = v_match.group(1).strip() if v_match else "PSALM 34:18"
        hook_str = re.sub(r"#\S+", "", meta.get("title", "DON'T GIVE UP YET")).strip()[:28]
        ai_thumb_prompt = pda.build_ai_thumbnail_prompt(topic_str, v_ref_str, hook_str)

    with st.expander("🎨 Custom Thumbnail Studio: Download Plain Image • Copy AI Prompt • Upload External", expanded=True):
        st.markdown("##### 1️⃣ Download Plain Background Image (Clean — Zero Text)")
        if plain_candidates:
            dl_cols = st.columns(min(4, len(plain_candidates)))
            for idx_p, (lbl_p, p_path) in enumerate(plain_candidates[:4]):
                with dl_cols[idx_p]:
                    st.image(str(p_path), caption=lbl_p, width="stretch")
                    st.download_button(
                        f"📥 Download {lbl_p[:18]}",
                        data=p_path.read_bytes(),
                        file_name=f"plain_{video_path.stem}_{p_path.name}",
                        mime="image/jpeg",
                        key=f"{key_prefix}_dl_plain_{idx_p}",
                        width="stretch"
                    )

        st.markdown("##### 2️⃣ AI-Analyzed Thumbnail Prompt (Copy for External AI Generators)")
        st.caption("Click the copy icon in the top-right corner to copy this prompt into Google AI Studio / Flow / Midjourney:")
        st.code(ai_thumb_prompt, language="text")

        st.markdown("##### 3️⃣ Upload Your Own Thumbnail (Made Outside)")
        up_ext_thumb = st.file_uploader(
            "Drop your custom thumbnail image (.jpg, .png, .webp) — automatically selected for YouTube upload:",
            type=["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "webp"],
            key=f"{key_prefix}_ext_thumb_up"
        )
        if up_ext_thumb is not None:
            im_ext = Image.open(up_ext_thumb).convert("RGB")
            im_ext.save(str(custom_up_path), "JPEG", quality=96, subsampling=0)
            st.success("✅ Custom thumbnail saved and selected for YouTube upload!")

    thumb_options = {}
    if custom_up_path.exists():
        thumb_options["🖼️ My Uploaded Custom Thumbnail"] = custom_up_path
    if tp_master.exists():
        thumb_options["⭐ Studio Master 9:16 (3D Gold)"] = tp_master
    for v_i in range(1, 4):
        vf_p = v_dir / f"thumbnail_9x16_variant_{v_i}.png"
        if vf_p.exists():
            thumb_options[f"✨ Variant #{v_i}"] = vf_p

    if thumb_options:
        sel_lbl = st.radio(
            "📺 Select Which Thumbnail Uploads to YouTube:",
            list(thumb_options.keys()),
            index=0,
            horizontal=True,
            key=f"{key_prefix}_final_thumb_radio"
        )
        chosen_file = thumb_options[sel_lbl]
        st.image(str(chosen_file), caption=f"Active Cover for YouTube: {sel_lbl}", width=240)
        return chosen_file

    return tp_master if tp_master.exists() else None

                with gs2:

                    gr_stk_path = gr_stk_inv[gr_stk_lbl]

                if st.button("⚡ 2. GENERATE 3-SCENE CINEMATIC SHORT (28–38s)", key="gr_render_3sc_btn", width="stretch"):

                        st.warning("Please click '1. Write 3-Scene Script' above first!")
                    else:

                        chosen_v_id = tone_info["voice"] if use_rec_tone else global_v_obj["voice"]

                            st.rerun()

    # --- STYLE B: 1-SCENE QUICK SHORT ---
    else:
        with col_workflow:
            with st.container(border=True):
                st.markdown("#### 📖 Step 1: Topic, 16 Openers & Full Spoken Script")

                    if st.button("🎲 AI Pick Another Relevant Verse & Explanation for This Topic", key="en_reroll_v", width="stretch"):

                    qs_ref = st.session_state["en_ai_bundle"]["verse_citation"]
                    qs_why_explain = st.session_state["en_ai_bundle"]["why_en"]

                _, qs_v_txt, qs_v_hi, qs_v_rom = lookup_reference_bilingual(qs_ref)

                st.markdown(

                    st.audio(str(mp3_en), format="audio/mp3", autoplay=True)

                q_stk_inv = sticker_studio.get_sticker_inventory()
                qst_c1, qst_c2 = st.columns(2)
                with qst_c1:
                    qs_stk_lbl = st.selectbox("🏷️ Floating Sticker Overlay:", list(q_stk_inv.keys()), index=1 if len(q_stk_inv) > 1 else 0, key="qs_stk_sel")
                    qs_stk_path = q_stk_inv[qs_stk_lbl]
                with qst_c2:
                    qs_stk_pos = st.selectbox("📍 Sticker Position:", ["Bottom Dock (Centered)", "Bottom Right (Floating)", "Top Right Corner"], key="qs_stk_pos")

                if st.button(f"⚡ GENERATE QUICK SHORT ({qs_ref})", key="qs_gen_btn", width="stretch"):
                    qs_loader = LiveTaskLoader("Generating Quick Short")
                    active_maker_voice = dict(global_v_obj, rate=global_rate_str, accent=global_acc, script_mode=SCRIPT_MODES[0])
                    save_active_voice(active_maker_voice)

                    script_obj = backend.BibleShortScript(
                        topic=qs_topic, title=st.session_state.get("qs_synced_title_in", f"God's Promise in {qs_ref} #shorts"),
                        verse_citation=qs_ref, verse_text=qs_v_txt,
                        meaning="Trust God's unshakeable provision today.", prayer="Lord Jesus, release Your breakthrough, Amen.",
                        thumbnail_hook=st.session_state.get("qs_synced_hook", "GOD WILL PROVIDE"),
                        hashtags=["#DailyPrayer", "#BibleVerse", "#Shorts"]
                    )

                    qs_loader.update(30, "🎙️ Synthesizing exact voiceover script...")
                    temp_voice = backend.BASE_DIR / "temp_voice.mp3"
                    backend.generate_voiceover(qs_spoken_script.strip(), temp_voice)

                    qs_loader.update(65, "⚡ Rendering 1080p Short + 3D Gold Thumbnail...")
                    seq_dir = backend.get_next_sequence_folder(backend.clean_filename(f"Quick_{qs_topic}_{qs_ref}"))
                    out_vid = seq_dir / f"{seq_dir.name}.mp4"
                    out_th = seq_dir / f"{seq_dir.name}_thumb.jpg"

                    c_intro_path = (backend.INTRO_DIR / qs_vid) if (qs_vid != "Auto / Newest") else (intro_files[0] if intro_files else backend.prepare_muted_short_main())
                    c_bg_path = (backend.BG_DIR / qs_img) if (qs_img != "Auto / Newest") else active_wall_path

                    ok_q = backend.render_faceless_bible_video(
                        script_obj, temp_voice, out_vid, out_th,
                        custom_intro=c_intro_path, custom_bg=c_bg_path, custom_thumb=c_bg_path,
                        palette_name=global_palette, custom_bgm=global_bgm_file, bgm_volume=global_bgm_vol,
                        video_mode=qs_v_mode, sticker_path=qs_stk_path, sticker_position=qs_stk_pos,
                        lyrics_words_per_card=global_lyrics_words
                    )
                    if temp_voice.exists():
                        temp_voice.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    if ok_q:
                        synced_pkg_now = vr.get_fully_synced_youtube_package(qs_topic, qs_ref, qs_v_txt, qs_opening)
                        final_t = st.session_state.get("qs_synced_title_in") or synced_pkg_now["title"]
                        final_d = st.session_state.get("qs_synced_desc_in") or synced_pkg_now["description"]
                        final_tags_str = st.session_state.get("qs_synced_tags_in") or ", ".join(synced_pkg_now["tags"])
                        final_tags_raw = [t.strip() for t in final_tags_str.split(",") if t.strip()]
                        clean_tags = backend.sanitize_youtube_tags(final_tags_raw)
                        backend.save_clip_metadata(out_vid, final_t, final_d, clean_tags, topic=qs_topic)
                        st.session_state["last_created_video"] = str(out_vid)
                        qs_loader.complete(f"🎉 Created Short: `{out_vid.name}`!")
                        time.sleep(0.4)
                        st.rerun()

    # --- RIGHT COLUMN: UN-CROPPED LIVE MONITOR & DIRECT PUBLISHER ---
    with col_monitor:
        with st.container(border=True):
            st.markdown("#### 🎬 Live Output Monitor & Direct Publisher")
            mon_vid_str = st.session_state.get("last_grace_video") or st.session_state.get("last_created_video")
            if not mon_vid_str or not Path(mon_vid_str).exists():
                mon_vid_str = str(all_videos[0]) if all_videos else None

            if not mon_vid_str:
                st.info("💡 Generate your Short on the left to preview the video and 3D Gold Thumbnails here!")
            else:
                mvp = Path(mon_vid_str)
                m_meta = backend.load_clip_metadata(mvp)
                m_dur = cached_video_dur(str(mvp))

                st.markdown(f"**⏱️ Final Duration:** `{m_dur:.1f}s` &nbsp;|&nbsp; 📁 `{mvp.parent.name}`")
                st.video(str(mvp))

                chosen_cover = render_custom_thumbnail_manager(mvp, f"pub_{mvp.parent.name}")

                st.markdown(f"**🏆 Publish Title:** `{m_meta['title']}`")
                with st.expander("📝 View Description & Backend Tags", expanded=False):
                    st.text_area("Description:", value=m_meta["description"], height=120, key=f"mon_d_{mvp.parent.name}")
                    st.code(", ".join(m_meta.get("tags", [])), language="text")

                if m_meta.get("youtube_id"):
                    st.success(f"🎉 LIVE ON YOUTUBE: https://youtube.com/shorts/{m_meta['youtube_id']}")
                else:
                    if st.button(f"🚀 Publish Video + Selected Thumbnail to '{global_target_ch}'", key=f"pub_btn_{mvp.parent.name}", width="stretch"):
                        live_ed_d = st.session_state.get(f"mon_d_{mvp.parent.name}", m_meta.get("description", ""))
                        backend.save_clip_metadata(mvp, m_meta.get("title", ""), live_ed_d, m_meta.get("tags", []), topic=m_meta.get("topic", ""))
                        with st.spinner(f"Publishing to {global_target_ch}..."):
                            yt_id = backend.upload_video_to_youtube(global_target_ch, mvp, thumbnail_path=chosen_cover)
                            if yt_id:
                                gle.post_pinned_comment_if_possible(global_target_ch, yt_id, mvp)
                                st.success(f"Live on YouTube! https://youtube.com/shorts/{yt_id}")
                                time.sleep(1.0)
                                st.rerun()

                    "प्रभु आपके हर छिपे हुए आंसू को देखते हैं और आपके दर्द को जानते हैं..."

with tab_long:
    st.markdown("### 🕊️ Long-Form Soul Prayer Sanctuary (5 Min • 10 Min • 20 Min • 30 Min • 1 Hour)")
    lf_left, lf_right = st.columns([1.18, 1.0], gap="large")

    with lf_left:
        with st.container(border=True):
            st.markdown("#### 🙏 Step 1: Prayer Duration & Topic")
            lf_c1, lf_c2 = st.columns(2)
            with lf_c1:
                lf_dur = st.selectbox("⏱️ Video Length:", list(lpe.DURATION_CONFIGS.keys()), index=1, key="lf_dur_sel")
            with lf_c2:
                lf_nature = st.selectbox("🌧️ Nature Ambience:", ["None (Pure Music)", "Gentle Night Rain", "Mountain Stream"], index=1, key="lf_nat_sel")

            lf_t_labels = [f"{item['id']}. {item['topic']}" for item in TOP_50_PRAYER_TOPICS]
            lf_chosen_lbl = st.selectbox("Select Topic:", lf_t_labels, key="lf_topic_dd")
            lf_topic = TOP_50_PRAYER_TOPICS[lf_t_labels.index(lf_chosen_lbl)]["topic"]

            if st.button("📜 1. Generate 5-Stage Prayer Script", key="lf_gen_script_btn", width="stretch"):
                with st.spinner(f"Crafting prayer journey for '{lf_topic}' ({lf_dur})..."):
                    st.session_state["lf_active_journey"] = lpe.generate_soul_prayer_journey(lf_topic, lf_dur)

            if "lf_active_journey" not in st.session_state:
                st.info("💡 Click **'1. Generate 5-Stage Prayer Script'** above to begin.")
                lf_journey = None
            else:
                lf_journey = st.session_state["lf_active_journey"]
                vers_list_str = ", ".join([v["ref"] for v in lf_journey["scriptures"]])
                st.markdown(
                    f'<div class="verse-card">'
                    f'<div class="verse-hdr">📖 {len(lf_journey["scriptures"])} Scriptures Loaded for {lf_dur}:</div>'
                    f'<div class="verse-body">{vers_list_str}</div>'
                    f'</div>',
                    unsafe_allow_html=True
                )
                with st.expander("📝 View / Edit 5 Prayer Movements Before Rendering", expanded=False):
                    for stg in lf_journey["stages"]:
                        st.markdown(f"**Stage {stg['stage_num']}: {stg['chapter_title']}**")
                        edited_stg = st.text_area(f"Spoken Prayer (Stage {stg['stage_num']}):", value=stg["full_text"], height=100, key=f"lf_stg_edit_{stg['stage_num']}")
                        stg["full_text"] = edited_stg.strip()
                        stg["paragraphs"] = [p.strip() for p in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", edited_stg) if p.strip()]

                v_col1, v_col2 = st.columns(2)
                intro_vids = get_media_files(str(backend.INTRO_DIR), (".mp4", ".mov", ".webm", ".mkv"))
                bg_imgs = get_media_files(str(backend.BG_DIR), (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"))
                with v_col1:
                    lf_sub_mode = st.selectbox(
                        "📝 Subtitle Style & Render Speed:",
                        [
                            "⚡ Instant 3-Second Render (YouTube CC & .SRT Subtitles)",
                            "🎬 Burned-In Subtitles (Hardsubs — Re-encodes All Frames)"
                        ],
                        index=0,
                        key="lf_sub_mode_sel"
                    )
                    lf_vis_mode = st.selectbox("🎬 16:9 Style:", ["Looping Video Background", "Cinematic Ken Burns Wallpaper"], key="lf_vis_mode")
                    lf_pick_vid = st.selectbox("🎬 Video:", ["Auto / Newest"] + [f.name for f in intro_vids], key="lf_vid_dd")
                with v_col2:
                    lf_pick_img = st.selectbox("🖼️ Wallpaper:", ["Auto / Newest"] + [f.name for f in bg_imgs], key="lf_img_dd")

                if st.button(f"🚀 2. RENDER {lf_dur.upper()} 16:9 VIDEO", key="lf_render_btn", width="stretch"):
                    lf_loader = LiveTaskLoader(f"Producing {lf_dur} Video")
                    out_folder = lpe.get_next_longform_folder(f"{lf_dur}_{lf_topic}")
                    voice_raw_mp3 = out_folder / "meditative_voice.mp3"
                    master_mix_mp3 = out_folder / "master_soaking_audio.mp3"
                    final_16x9_mp4 = out_folder / f"{out_folder.name}.mp4"
                    final_16x9_thumb = out_folder / f"{out_folder.name}_thumb.jpg"

                    lf_loader.update(25, "🎙️ Synthesizing voiceover...")
                    v_res = lpe.synthesize_meditative_voiceover(lf_journey, voice_raw_mp3, voice_name=global_v_obj["voice"], rate_str=global_rate_str, pitch_str="-2Hz")

                    lf_loader.update(55, f"🎵 Mixing {lf_dur} master audio with worship music...")
                    lpe.mix_longform_master_audio(voice_raw_mp3, master_mix_mp3, target_duration_sec=lf_journey["config"]["target_seconds"], bgm_file=global_bgm_file, bgm_volume=global_bgm_vol, nature_style=lf_nature)

                    lf_loader.update(80, "🎬 Rendering Full-HD 1920x1080 (16:9) Video...")
                    chosen_v = (backend.INTRO_DIR / lf_pick_vid) if (lf_pick_vid != "Auto / Newest") else (intro_vids[0] if intro_vids else backend.prepare_muted_short_main())
                    chosen_i = (backend.BG_DIR / lf_pick_img) if (lf_pick_img != "Auto / Newest") else active_wall_path

                    ok_lf = lpe.render_longform_16x9_video(
                        master_mix_mp3, final_16x9_mp4, final_16x9_thumb, lf_journey,
                        bg_image_path=chosen_i, bg_video_path=chosen_v,
                        visual_mode=lf_vis_mode, palette_name=global_palette,
                        subtitle_mode=(lf_sub_mode if 'lf_sub_mode' in locals() else 'Instant Render (YouTube CC / .SRT)'),
                        progress_cb=lambda pct, msg: lf_loader.update(pct, msg)
                    )
                    if ok_lf:
                        lf_title, lf_desc, lf_tags = lpe.build_longform_youtube_metadata(lf_journey, v_res["chapters"])
                        backend.save_clip_metadata(final_16x9_mp4, lf_title, lf_desc, lf_tags, topic=lf_topic)
                        st.session_state["last_longform_video"] = str(final_16x9_mp4)
                        lf_loader.complete(f"🎉 Ready: `{final_16x9_mp4.name}`!")
                        time.sleep(0.4)
                        st.rerun()

    with lf_right:
        with st.container(border=True):
            st.markdown("#### 📺 Monitor & Publisher")
            lf_existing = [f for f in sorted(list(lpe.LONGFORM_DIR.rglob("*.mp4")), key=lambda p: p.parent.name, reverse=True) if "temp_" not in str(f)]
            lf_prev = st.session_state.get("last_longform_video") or (str(lf_existing[0]) if lf_existing else None)
            if not lf_prev:
                st.info("💡 Long-Form videos will preview here.")
            else:
                lf_p = Path(lf_prev)
                st.markdown(f"**⏱️ Length:** `{lpe.format_mm_ss(cached_video_dur(str(lf_p)))}` &nbsp;|&nbsp; 📁 `{lf_p.parent.name}`")
                st.video(str(lf_p))

                lf_srt_f = lf_p.parent / "longform_subtitles.srt"
                if lf_srt_f.exists():
                    st.download_button(
                        "📥 Download Subtitles (.SRT for YouTube CC)",
                        data=lf_srt_f.read_bytes(),
                        file_name=f"{lf_p.stem}_subtitles.srt",
                        mime="application/x-subrip",
                        width="stretch"
                    )

                lf_th = render_custom_thumbnail_manager(lf_p, f"lf_pub_{lf_p.parent.name}")
                if st.button(f"🚀 Publish to '{global_target_ch}'", key="lf_pub_main", width="stretch"):
                    yt_id = backend.upload_video_to_youtube(global_target_ch, lf_p, thumbnail_path=lf_th)
                    if yt_id:
                        st.success(f"Live! https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={yt_id}")

                        sb, work_dir, voice_name=global_v_obj["voice"], voice_rate=global_rate_str,

                        progress_cb=lambda pct, msg: mv_loader.update(pct, msg)
                    )
                    if ok_m:
                        backend.save_clip_metadata(out_mv, sb["story_title"], sb["story_title"], ["BibleStory", "Shorts"], topic=sb["topic"])
                        st.session_state["last_story_movie"] = str(out_mv)

            existing_m = sorted(list(fse.STORY_MOVIES_DIR.rglob("ST*.mp4")), key=lambda p: p.parent.name, reverse=True)
            st_prev = st.session_state.get("last_story_movie") or (str(existing_m[0]) if existing_m else None)
            if not st_prev:
                st.info("💡 Rendered 3-Minute Movies will display here.")

                                backend.save_clip_metadata(out_f, clip.title, clip.hook_caption, clip.hashtags)

    st.markdown("### 📊 Channel Analytics")
    ctl_c1, ctl_c2, ctl_c3 = st.columns([1.5, 1.2, 1.2])
    with ctl_c1:
        stat_ch = st.selectbox("Channel to Audit:", channel_names, key="stats_ch_sel")
    with ctl_c2:
        stat_days = st.selectbox("Period:", [28, 90, 365], index=1, format_func=lambda x: f"Last {x} Days", key="stats_period_sel")
    with ctl_c3:
        st.markdown("<div style='height:26px;'></div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)
        if st.button("🔄 Refresh Analytics", key="refresh_stats_btn", width="stretch"):
            st.session_state[f"full_intel_{stat_ch}_{stat_days}"] = cae.fetch_complete_channel_intelligence(stat_ch, days_back=stat_days)

    cache_k = f"full_intel_{stat_ch}_{stat_days}"
    intel = st.session_state.get(cache_k)
    if intel is None:
        st.info(f"💡 Click **'Refresh Analytics'** above to load live Subscribers, Video Audit, and Geography for **{stat_ch}**.")
    elif not intel.get("ok"):
        st.error(f"⚠️ {intel.get('error', 'Could not load data.')}")
    else:
        ov = intel["overview"]
        tot = intel["totals"]
        vids = intel["videos"]
        live_views = max(ov["lifetime_views"], sum(v["views"] for v in vids))
        wh_mins = tot["watch_time_minutes_period"]
        wh_hrs = round(wh_mins / 60.0, 1)

        r1 = st.columns(4)
        r1[0].metric("👥 Total Subscribers", f"{ov['subscribers']:,}")
        r1[1].metric("👁️ Total Channel Views", f"{live_views:,}", f"{tot['shorts_views']:,} Shorts • {tot['longform_views']:,} Long-Form")
        r1[2].metric("📹 Total Public Videos", f"{max(ov['total_videos'], len(vids)):,}", f"{tot['shorts_count']} Shorts • {tot['longform_count']} Long-Form")
        r1[3].metric("⏱️ Est. Watch Time", f"{wh_hrs:,.1f} hrs", f"{wh_mins:,} mins")

        r2 = st.columns(4)
        r2[0].metric("👍 Total Likes", f"{tot['total_likes']:,}")
        r2[1].metric("💬 Total Comments", f"{tot['total_comments']:,}")
        r2[2].metric("🔥 Engagement Rate", f"{tot['engagement_rate_pct']:.2f}%")
        top_v = max([v["views"] for v in vids], default=0)
        r2[3].metric("🚀 Top Video Views", f"{top_v:,}")

        if vids:
            st.write("")
            df_v = pd.DataFrame([{"Rank": f"#{i+1}", "Type": v["type"], "Title": v["title"][:50], "Views": v["views"], "Likes": v["likes"], "Comments": v["comments"], "Duration": v["duration_str"], "Date": v["published_date"]} for i, v in enumerate(vids)])
            st.dataframe(df_v, hide_index=True, width="stretch")

_BIBLE_MEM_CACHE: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None

def ensure_full_bible_downloaded() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Downloads and caches the complete 66-book, 31,102-verse English KJV Bible JSON."""
    global _BIBLE_MEM_CACHE
    if _BIBLE_MEM_CACHE is not None:
        return _BIBLE_MEM_CACHE

    if not KJV_JSON_FILE.exists() or KJV_JSON_FILE.stat().st_size < 1_000_000:
        url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/thiagobodruk/bible/master/json/en_kjv.json"
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as resp:
                raw_bytes = resp.read()
                # Strip UTF-8 BOM if present
                text_data = raw_bytes.decode("utf-8-sig")
                KJV_JSON_FILE.write_text(text_data, encoding="utf-8")
        except Exception:
            pass

    if KJV_JSON_FILE.exists() and KJV_JSON_FILE.stat().st_size > 100_000:
        try:
            _BIBLE_MEM_CACHE = json.loads(KJV_JSON_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
            return _BIBLE_MEM_CACHE
        except Exception:
            pass
    return []

def get_book_chapters_count(book_eng_name: str) -> int:
    for b_en, _, ch_cnt in BIBLE_66_BOOKS:
        if b_en.strip().lower() == book_eng_name.strip().lower():
            return ch_cnt
    return 1

def get_verses_for_chapter(book_eng_name: str, chapter_num: int) -> Dict[int, str]:
    """Returns {verse_number: verse_text} for any of the 66 books and any chapter."""
    data = ensure_full_bible_downloaded()
    book_idx = next((i for i, (b_en, _, _) in enumerate(BIBLE_66_BOOKS) if b_en.strip().lower() == book_eng_name.strip().lower()), -1)

    if data and 0 <= book_idx < len(data):
        chapters = data[book_idx].get("chapters", [])
        if 1 <= chapter_num <= len(chapters):
            v_list = chapters[chapter_num - 1]
            return {i + 1: re.sub(r"\{([^}]*)\}", r"\1", v_str) for i, v_str in enumerate(v_list)}

    # Online single-chapter fallback if JSON hasn't downloaded yet
    try:
        q = urllib.parse.quote(f"{book_eng_name} {chapter_num}")
        req = urllib.request.Request(f"https://bible-api.com/{q}?translation=kjv", headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=8) as resp:
            j = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
            return {int(v["verse"]): v["text"].strip() for v in j.get("verses", [])}
    except Exception:
        return {1: "The Lord is near to all who call upon Him in truth."}


# Verified Pavitra Baibil (Devanagari Hindi) Verses for instant 100% accurate Hindi reading
VERIFIED_PAVITRA_BAIBIL: Dict[str, Tuple[str, str]] = {
    "Philippians 4:19": (

        "Aur mera Parmeshwar apne us dhan ke anusaar jo mahima sahit Masih Yeshu mein hai, tumhari har ek ghati ko poori karega."

def translate_verse_to_hindi(ref: str, english_verse: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Guarantees pure Devanagari Hindi translation for any Bible verse.
    Never caches or returns English in the Devanagari slot.
    """
    ref_clean = ref.strip()
    if ref_clean in VERIFIED_PAVITRA_BAIBIL:
        return VERIFIED_PAVITRA_BAIBIL[ref_clean]

    cache: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if HINDI_CACHE_FILE.exists():
        try:
            cache = json.loads(HINDI_CACHE_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            cache = {}

    if ref_clean in cache and isinstance(cache[ref_clean], dict):
        cached_dev = cache[ref_clean].get("devanagari", "")
        if re.search(r"[\u0900-\u097F]", cached_dev):
            return cached_dev, cache[ref_clean].get("hinglish", cached_dev)

    devanagari = ""
    hinglish = ""
    try:
        q = urllib.parse.quote(english_verse.strip())
        # Use single dt=t first for 100% reliable Devanagari extraction
        url = f"https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single?client=gtx&sl=en&tl=hi&dt=t&q={q}"
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=8) as resp:
            arr = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
            if arr and arr[0]:
                devanagari = "".join(str(seg[0]) for seg in arr[0] if seg and seg[0]).strip()
    except Exception:
        pass

    if re.search(r"[\u0900-\u097F]", devanagari):
        hinglish = devanagari
        cache[ref_clean] = {"devanagari": devanagari, "hinglish": hinglish}
        try:
            HINDI_CACHE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(cache, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        except Exception:
            pass
        return devanagari, hinglish

    # Fallback if offline
    return "यहोवा अपने सभी पुकारने वालों के समीप रहता है और उन्हें शान्ति देता है।", "Yahowa apne sabhi pukarne walon ke samip rehta hai."



def lookup_reference_bilingual(ref_str: str) -> Tuple[str, str, str, str]:
    """
    Parses any reference like 'Philippians 4:19', 'Psalm 34:18', or 'Philippians 4:6-7'
    and returns (normalized_ref, english_text, hindi_devanagari, romanized_hinglish).
    """
    ref_clean = ref_str.strip()
    m = re.match(r"^((?:[1-3]\s+)?[A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+(\d+):(\d+)(?:-(\d+))?$", ref_clean)
    if not m:
        en_default = "And my God will supply every need of yours according to his riches in glory in Christ Jesus."
        dev, rom = translate_verse_to_hindi("Philippians 4:19", en_default)
        return ("Philippians 4:19", en_default, dev, rom)

    book_raw = m.group(1).strip()
    if book_raw.lower() == "psalm":
        book_lookup = "Psalms"
    else:
        book_lookup = book_raw

    ch_num = int(m.group(2))
    v_start = int(m.group(3))
    v_end = int(m.group(4)) if m.group(4) else v_start

    verses_map = get_verses_for_chapter(book_lookup, ch_num)
    collected = []
    for vn in range(v_start, v_end + 1):
        if vn in verses_map:
            collected.append(verses_map[vn])
    en_text = " ".join(collected) if collected else verses_map.get(v_start, "The Lord is near to all who call upon Him.")

    dev_hi, rom_hi = translate_verse_to_hindi(ref_clean, en_text)
    return (ref_clean, en_text, dev_hi, rom_hi)

def format_reference_for_speech(ref_str: str, is_hindi_speech: bool = False) -> str:
    """
    Converts 'Leviticus 1:1' into:
    - Hindi Speech:   'लैव्यव्यवस्था, अध्याय 1, वचन 1' (Never says '1 bajke 1 minute'!)
    - English Speech: 'Leviticus, chapter 1, verse 1'
    """
    ref_clean = ref_str.strip()
    m = re.match(r"^((?:[1-3]\s+)?[A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+(\d+):(\d+)(?:-(\d+))?$", ref_clean)
    if not m:
        # Strip any colons between numbers so TTS never reads clock time
        if is_hindi_speech:
            return re.sub(r"(\d+):(\d+)", r"अध्याय \1, वचन \2", ref_clean)
        return re.sub(r"(\d+):(\d+)", r"chapter \1, verse \2", ref_clean)

    book_en = m.group(1).strip()
    ch = m.group(2)
    v1 = m.group(3)
    v2 = m.group(4)

    if is_hindi_speech:
        hi_book = HINDI_BOOK_SPOKEN_MAP.get(book_en.lower(), book_en)
        if v2 and v2 != v1:
            return f"{hi_book}, अध्याय {ch}, वचन {v1} से {v2}"
        return f"{hi_book}, अध्याय {ch}, वचन {v1}"
    else:
        if v2 and v2 != v1:
            return f"{book_en}, chapter {ch}, verses {v1} to {v2}"
        return f"{book_en}, chapter {ch}, verse {v1}"


def translate_any_text_to_hindi(en_text: str) -> str:
    """Translates any English sentence (topic, meaning, prayer) into 100% pure Devanagari Hindi."""
    if not en_text or not en_text.strip():
        return ""
    # If already contains Devanagari characters, return as-is
    if re.search(r"[\u0900-\u097F]", en_text):
        return en_text
    cache: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if HINDI_CACHE_FILE.exists():
        try:
            cache = json.loads(HINDI_CACHE_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            cache = {}
    key = f"txt_{en_text.strip()[:80]}"
    if key in cache and isinstance(cache[key], str) and cache[key]:
        return cache[key]
    try:
        q = urllib.parse.quote(en_text.strip())
        url = f"https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single?client=gtx&sl=en&tl=hi&dt=t&q={q}"
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=6) as resp:
            arr = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
            if arr and arr[0]:
                hi_out = "".join(seg[0] for seg in arr[0] if seg and seg[0]).strip()
                if hi_out:
                    cache[key] = hi_out
                    HINDI_CACHE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(cache, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                    return hi_out
    except Exception:
        pass
    return en_text

def ai_pick_relevant_verse_for_topic(topic_str: str, lang: str = "en", api_key: str = "") -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Uses AI (plus anti-repetition tracking per language channel) to pick a fresh,
    relevant Bible verse for `topic_str` along with an explanation, meaning, and prayer.
    `lang` is 'en' (English Maker) or 'hi' (Hindi Maker).
    """
    import os
    import random

    clean_topic = re.sub(r"^\d+\.\s*", "", re.sub(r"\s*—\s*\[.*?\]", "", topic_str)).strip()
    used_file = BASE_DIR / f"used_verses_{lang}.json"
    try:
        used_list = json.loads(used_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if used_file.exists() else []
    except Exception:
        used_list = []

    avoid_str = ", ".join(used_list[-40:]) if used_list else "None"
    seed = random.randint(10000, 999999)

    # Determine category pool for fallback
    cat_name = "Faith"
    for item in TOP_50_PRAYER_TOPICS:
        if item["topic"].lower() in clean_topic.lower() or clean_topic.lower() in item["topic"].lower():
            cat_name = item["cat"]
            break
    pool = CATEGORY_VERSE_POOLS.get(cat_name, CATEGORY_VERSE_POOLS["Faith"])
    unused_pool = [r for r in pool if r not in used_list] or pool
    fallback_ref = random.choice(unused_pool)

    chosen_ref = fallback_ref
    why_explanation_en = f"This scripture directly addresses {clean_topic} by reminding us of God's unshakeable promise and provision."
    meaning_en = f"When you face {clean_topic}, God invites you to cast every burden upon Him. His strength and grace are more than enough to carry you through today."
    prayer_en = f"Lord Jesus, I surrender my struggle with {clean_topic} into Your hands; fill my life with Your peace and breakthrough, Amen."
    hook_en = f"GODS PROMISE FOR {clean_topic.upper()[:16]}"
    title_en = f"God's Promise for {clean_topic[:32]} #shorts"

    key_to_use = api_key or os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "")
    if key_to_use:
        prompt = f"""Random Seed: {seed}
Topic: {clean_topic}
DO NOT pick any of these recently used verses: [{avoid_str}]

Pick 1 powerful, deeply relevant Bible verse for someone praying about '{clean_topic}'.
Respond ONLY in valid JSON with exact keys:
- "verse_citation": Standard Book Chapter:Verse (e.g. "Psalm 147:3" or "Deuteronomy 28:12")
- "why_this_verse": 1 sentence explaining WHY this specific verse is powerful for '{clean_topic}'
- "meaning": 2 comforting, reverent sentences explaining the spiritual meaning of this verse
- "prayer": 1 powerful breakthrough prayer sentence starting with 'Lord...' and ending with 'Amen.'
- "thumbnail_hook": 3-4 words ALL CAPS bold hook
- "title": Under 55 chars catchy YouTube Short title ending with #shorts"""

        for m_name in ["gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-2.0-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"]:
            try:
                url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{m_name}:generateContent?key={key_to_use}"
                payload = {
                    "contents": [{"parts": [{"text": prompt}]}],
                    "generationConfig": {"responseMimeType": "application/json", "temperature": 1.05}
                }
                req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}, data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
                with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=7) as resp:
                    resp_json = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                    raw_t = resp_json["candidates"][0]["content"]["parts"][0]["text"]
                    m = re.search(r"\{.*\}", raw_t, re.DOTALL)
                    if m:
                        raw_t = m.group(0)
                    parsed = json.loads(raw_t)
                    cand_ref = parsed.get("verse_citation", "").strip()
                    if re.match(r"^(?:[1-3]\s+)?[A-Za-z\s]+\s+\d+:\d+", cand_ref):
                        chosen_ref = cand_ref
                        why_explanation_en = parsed.get("why_this_verse") or why_explanation_en
                        meaning_en = parsed.get("meaning") or meaning_en
                        prayer_en = parsed.get("prayer") or prayer_en
                        hook_en = parsed.get("thumbnail_hook") or hook_en
                        title_en = parsed.get("title") or title_en
                        break
            except Exception:
                continue

    # Look up exact canonical English KJV verse and pure Devanagari Hindi verse
    norm_ref, en_verse_text, hi_verse_dev, hi_verse_rom = lookup_reference_bilingual(chosen_ref)

    # Record in language-specific used list so next click gives a different verse
    if norm_ref not in used_list:
        used_list.append(norm_ref)
        try:
            used_file.write_text(json.dumps(used_list[-80:], indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        except Exception:
            pass

    why_hi = translate_any_text_to_hindi(why_explanation_en)
    meaning_hi = translate_any_text_to_hindi(meaning_en)
    prayer_hi = translate_any_text_to_hindi(prayer_en)
    topic_hi = translate_any_text_to_hindi(clean_topic)

    return {
        "topic_en": clean_topic,
        "topic_hi": topic_hi,
        "verse_citation": norm_ref,
        "en_spoken_ref": format_reference_for_speech(norm_ref, is_hindi_speech=False),
        "hi_spoken_ref": format_reference_for_speech(norm_ref, is_hindi_speech=True),
        "en_verse": en_verse_text,
        "hi_verse": hi_verse_dev,
        "rom_verse": hi_verse_rom,
        "why_en": why_explanation_en,
        "why_hi": why_hi,
        "meaning_en": meaning_en,
        "meaning_hi": meaning_hi,
        "prayer_en": prayer_en,
        "prayer_hi": prayer_hi,
        "hook_en": hook_en,
        "title_en": title_en,
        "title_hi": f"{format_reference_for_speech(norm_ref, is_hindi_speech=True)} — {topic_hi[:22]} #shorts",
    }



==========================================
=== FILE: channel_analytics_engine.py ===

import os
import re
import json
import pickle
import collections
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Tuple

from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import InstalledAppFlow
from google.auth.transport.requests import Request
from googleapiclient.discovery import build

import main as backend

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

FULL_ANALYTICS_SCOPES = [
    "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube",
    "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.upload",
    "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.readonly",
    "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/yt-analytics.readonly",
]

COUNTRY_MAP = {
    "IN": "India \U0001f1ee\U0001f1f3",
    "US": "United States \U0001f1fa\U0001f1f8",
    "GB": "United Kingdom \U0001f1ec\U0001f1e7",
    "PH": "Philippines \U0001f1f5\U0001f1ed",
    "CA": "Canada \U0001f1e8\U0001f1e6",
    "AU": "Australia \U0001f1e6\U0001f1fa",
    "NG": "Nigeria \U0001f1f3\U0001f1ec",
    "ZA": "South Africa \U0001f1ff\U0001f1e6",
    "KE": "Kenya \U0001f1f0\U0001f1ea",
    "BR": "Brazil \U0001f1e7\U0001f1f7",
    "DE": "Germany \U0001f1e9\U0001f1ea",
    "GH": "Ghana \U0001f1ec\U0001f1ed",
    "SG": "Singapore \U0001f1f8\U0001f1ec",
    "NZ": "New Zealand \U0001f1f3\U0001f1ff",
    "PK": "Pakistan \U0001f1f5\U0001f1f0",
    "ID": "Indonesia \U0001f1ee\U0001f1e9",
    "MY": "Malaysia \U0001f1f2\U0001f1fe",
    "AE": "UAE \U0001f1e6\U0001f1ea",
    "UG": "Uganda \U0001f1fa\U0001f1ec",
    "MX": "Mexico \U0001f1f2\U0001f1fd",
}

TRAFFIC_SOURCE_MAP = {
    "SHORTS": "\U0001f4f1 YouTube Shorts Feed",
    "YT_SEARCH": "\U0001f50d YouTube Search",
    "BROWSE": "\U0001f3e0 Home / Browse Features",
    "RELATED_VIDEO": "\U0001f449 Suggested Videos",
    "YT_CHANNEL": "\U0001f4fa Channel Page",
    "EXT_URL": "\U0001f310 External Websites / WhatsApp",
    "NOTIFICATION": "\U0001f514 Subscriber Notifications",
    "PLAYLIST": "\U0001f4cb Playlists",
    "SUBSCRIBER": "\U0001f465 Subscriptions Feed",
    "END_SCREEN": "\U0001f3ac End Screens",
    "NO_LINK_OTHER": "\U0001f4ca Direct / Other",
}

def parse_iso_sec(dur_str: str) -> int:
    if not dur_str:
        return 0
    m = re.match(r"PT(?:(\d+)H)?(?:(\d+)M)?(?:(\d+)S)?", dur_str)
    if not m:
        return 0
    return int(m.group(1) or 0) * 3600 + int(m.group(2) or 0) * 60 + int(m.group(3) or 0)

def format_dur_mmss(seconds: int) -> str:
    h, rem = divmod(max(0, int(seconds)), 3600)
    m, s = divmod(rem, 60)
    return f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}" if h > 0 else f"{m:02d}:{s:02d}"

def reauthorize_channel_full_analytics(channel_name: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Opens Google OAuth consent screen with full YouTube Data + YouTube Analytics scopes."""
    secret_f = backend.CLIENT_SECRET_FILE
    if not secret_f.exists():
        return False, "Missing client_secret.json in project root!"
    try:
        flow = InstalledAppFlow.from_client_secrets_file(str(secret_f), FULL_ANALYTICS_SCOPES)
        creds = flow.run_local_server(port=0, prompt="consent")
        yt = build("youtube", "v3", credentials=creds)
        res = yt.channels().list(mine=True, part="snippet").execute()
        if not res.get("items"):
            return False, "No YouTube channel found on the selected Google account."
        actual_title = res["items"][0]["snippet"]["title"]
        safe_name = re.sub(r'[\/*?:"<>| ]', "_", actual_title)
        token_file = backend.CHANNELS_DIR / f"{safe_name}.pickle"
        with open(token_file, "wb") as f:
            pickle.dump(creds, f)
        channels = backend.load_channels()
        channels[actual_title] = str(token_file)
        backend.save_channels(channels)
        return True, actual_title
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)

def _load_creds(channel_name: str):
    channels = backend.load_channels()
    token_path = channels.get(channel_name)
    if not token_path or not Path(token_path).exists():
        return None
    try:
        with open(token_path, "rb") as f:
            creds = pickle.load(f)
        if not creds or not creds.valid:
            if creds and creds.expired and creds.refresh_token:
                creds.refresh(Request())
                with open(token_path, "wb") as f:
                    pickle.dump(creds, f)
            else:
                return None
        return creds
    except Exception:
        return None

def fetch_complete_channel_intelligence(channel_name: str, days_back: int = 90) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Fetches comprehensive channel & video analytics:
    - Channel Overview: Subscribers, Lifetime Views, Total Videos, Handle, Age, Avatar
    - All Uploaded Videos (up to 150 videos, both Shorts and Long-Form)
    - Aggregated Likes, Comments, Engagement Rate %, Views in last 7d / 28d
    - YouTube Analytics v2 Reports:
      1. Daily Views, Watch Minutes & Net Subscribers
      2. Geographical Country Breakdown (Views, Watch Minutes, Avg View %, Subs Gained)
      3. Traffic Sources (Shorts Feed, Search, Browse, Suggested)
      4. Device Types (Mobile, TV, Desktop, Tablet)
      5. Best Posting Hours in IST (UTC+5:30)
    """
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "ok": False,
        "error": None,
        "needs_reauth": False,
        "analytics_api_notice": None,
        "overview": {
            "title": channel_name,
            "handle": "",
            "channel_id": "",
            "published_at": "",
            "channel_age_days": 1,
            "country": "IN",
            "subscribers": 0,
            "hidden_subscribers": False,
            "lifetime_views": 0,
            "total_videos": 0,
        },
        "totals": {
            "analyzed_videos": 0,
            "shorts_count": 0,
            "longform_count": 0,
            "shorts_views": 0,
            "longform_views": 0,
            "total_likes": 0,
            "total_comments": 0,
            "engagement_rate_pct": 0.0,
            "views_last_7d_uploads": 0,
            "views_last_28d_uploads": 0,
            "avg_views_per_short": 0,
            "avg_views_per_longform": 0,
            "watch_time_minutes_period": 0,
            "avg_view_duration_sec": 0,
            "subs_gained_period": 0,
        },
        "videos": [],
        "daily_rows": [],
        "geo_rows": [],
        "traffic_rows": [],
        "device_rows": [],
        "best_hours_ist": [],
    }

    creds = _load_creds(channel_name)
    if not creds:
        result["error"] = f"Channel '{channel_name}' is not authenticated. Click 'Grant Full Analytics Permission' below."
        result["needs_reauth"] = True
        return result

    now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PART 1: YOUTUBE DATA API v3 (CHANNEL OVERVIEW + ALL VIDEOS)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        yt = build("youtube", "v3", credentials=creds)
        ch_res = yt.channels().list(mine=True, part="snippet,statistics,contentDetails").execute()
        if not ch_res.get("items"):
            result["error"] = "No YouTube channel found on this token."
            return result

        ch_item = ch_res["items"][0]
        snip = ch_item.get("snippet", {})
        stats = ch_item.get("statistics", {})
        c_details = ch_item.get("contentDetails", {})

        pub_str = snip.get("publishedAt", "")
        try:
            ch_pub_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(pub_str.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            ch_age = max(1, int((now_utc - ch_pub_dt).total_seconds() // 86400))
        except Exception:
            ch_age = 1

        result["overview"] = {
            "title": snip.get("title", channel_name),
            "handle": snip.get("customUrl", ""),
            "channel_id": ch_item.get("id", ""),
            "published_at": pub_str[:10] if pub_str else "N/A",
            "channel_age_days": ch_age,
            "country": snip.get("country", "IN"),
            "subscribers": int(stats.get("subscriberCount", 0)),
            "hidden_subscribers": bool(stats.get("hiddenSubscriberCount", False)),
            "lifetime_views": int(stats.get("viewCount", 0)),
            "total_videos": int(stats.get("videoCount", 0)),
        }

        uploads_pl = c_details.get("relatedPlaylists", {}).get("uploads", "")
        vid_ids: List[str] = []
        next_page = None

        # Fetch up to 150 recent uploads (3 pages of 50)
        for _ in range(3):
            if not uploads_pl:
                break
            pl_req = yt.playlistItems().list(
                playlistId=uploads_pl,
                part="contentDetails",
                maxResults=50,
                pageToken=next_page
            ).execute()
            for it in pl_req.get("items", []):
                v_id = it.get("contentDetails", {}).get("videoId")
                if v_id:
                    vid_ids.append(v_id)
            next_page = pl_req.get("nextPageToken")
            if not next_page:
                break

        all_vids: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for i in range(0, len(vid_ids), 50):
            batch_ids = vid_ids[i:i+50]
            v_res = yt.videos().list(
                id=",".join(batch_ids),
                part="snippet,statistics,contentDetails"
            ).execute()
            for v in v_res.get("items", []):
                v_snip = v.get("snippet", {})
                v_stat = v.get("statistics", {})
                v_cd = v.get("contentDetails", {})

                dur_s = parse_iso_sec(v_cd.get("duration", ""))
                is_short = (0 < dur_s <= 180)
                v_pub = v_snip.get("publishedAt", "")
                try:
                    v_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(v_pub.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                except Exception:
                    v_dt = now_utc

                age_d = max(0.1, (now_utc - v_dt).total_seconds() / 86400.0)
                views = int(v_stat.get("viewCount", 0))
                likes = int(v_stat.get("likeCount", 0))
                comments = int(v_stat.get("commentCount", 0))
                vpd = round(views / max(1.0, age_d), 1)
                eng_pct = round(((likes + comments) / max(1, views)) * 100.0, 2) if views > 0 else 0.0

                # Convert publish time to IST (UTC+5:30)
                ist_dt = v_dt + timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)

                all_vids.append({
                    "id": v["id"],
                    "title": v_snip.get("title", "Untitled"),
                    "type": "\U0001f4f1 Short" if is_short else "\U0001f4fa Long-Form",
                    "is_short": is_short,
                    "duration_sec": dur_s,
                    "duration_str": format_dur_mmss(dur_s),
                    "views": views,
                    "views_per_day": vpd,
                    "likes": likes,
                    "comments": comments,
                    "engagement_pct": eng_pct,
                    "age_days": round(age_d, 1),
                    "published_date": ist_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
                    "published_hour_ist": ist_dt.hour,
                    "url": f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={v['id']}"
                })

        all_vids.sort(key=lambda x: x["views"], reverse=True)
        result["videos"] = all_vids

        # Compute granular totals
        sh_vids = [x for x in all_vids if x["is_short"]]
        lf_vids = [x for x in all_vids if not x["is_short"]]

        sh_views = sum(x["views"] for x in sh_vids)
        lf_views = sum(x["views"] for x in lf_vids)
        tot_likes = sum(x["likes"] for x in all_vids)
        tot_comments = sum(x["comments"] for x in all_vids)
        tot_v_analyzed = sh_views + lf_views

        v_7d = sum(x["views"] for x in all_vids if x["age_days"] <= 7.0)
        v_28d = sum(x["views"] for x in all_vids if x["age_days"] <= 28.0)

        result["overview"]["lifetime_views"] = max(result["overview"]["lifetime_views"], tot_v_analyzed)
        result["overview"]["total_videos"] = max(result["overview"]["total_videos"], len(all_vids))
        est_watch_mins = int(round(sum(x["views"] * max(12, min(x["duration_sec"], 60)) * 0.78 for x in all_vids) / 60.0))

        result["totals"].update({
            "analyzed_videos": len(all_vids),
            "shorts_count": len(sh_vids),
            "longform_count": len(lf_vids),
            "shorts_views": sh_views,
            "longform_views": lf_views,
            "total_likes": tot_likes,
            "total_comments": tot_comments,
            "engagement_rate_pct": round(((tot_likes + tot_comments) / max(1, tot_v_analyzed)) * 100.0, 2) if tot_v_analyzed > 0 else 0.0,
            "views_last_7d_uploads": v_7d,
            "views_last_28d_uploads": v_28d,
            "avg_views_per_short": int(sh_views // max(1, len(sh_vids))) if sh_vids else 0,
            "avg_views_per_longform": int(lf_views // max(1, len(lf_vids))) if lf_vids else 0,
            "watch_time_minutes_period": est_watch_mins,
        })

        day_groups = collections.defaultdict(lambda: {"Views": 0, "Likes": 0, "Comments": 0, "Videos": 0, "Watch Minutes": 0})
        for x in all_vids:
            dk = x["published_date"]
            day_groups[dk]["Views"] += x["views"]
            day_groups[dk]["Likes"] += x["likes"]
            day_groups[dk]["Comments"] += x["comments"]
            day_groups[dk]["Videos"] += 1
            day_groups[dk]["Watch Minutes"] += int(round((x["views"] * max(12, x["duration_sec"]) * 0.78) / 60.0))
        result["daily_rows"] = [{"Date": k, **v} for k, v in sorted(day_groups.items())]

        # Best Posting Hour in IST
        hour_buckets = collections.defaultdict(list)
        for x in all_vids:
            hour_buckets[x["published_hour_ist"]].append(x["views"])
        best_hours = []
        for hr, v_list in hour_buckets.items():
            ampm = f"{hr % 12 or 12}:00 {'AM' if hr < 12 else 'PM'} IST"
            best_hours.append({
                "hour_ist": ampm,
                "videos_posted": len(v_list),
                "avg_views": int(sum(v_list) // max(1, len(v_list))),
                "total_views": sum(v_list)
            })
        best_hours.sort(key=lambda x: x["avg_views"], reverse=True)
        result["best_hours_ist"] = best_hours
        result["ok"] = True

    except Exception as e_data:
        err_s = str(e_data)
        result["error"] = f"YouTube Data API Error: {err_s[:180]}"
        if "insufficientPermissions" in err_s or "403" in err_s:
            result["needs_reauth"] = True
        return result

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # PART 2: YOUTUBE ANALYTICS API v2 (DAILY, GEOGRAPHY, TRAFFIC, DEVICES)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        yt_ana = build("youtubeAnalytics", "v2", credentials=creds)
        end_str = now_utc.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        start_str = (now_utc - timedelta(days=days_back)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        # A. Daily Views, Watch Time & Subscribers Gained
        try:
            rep_day = yt_ana.reports().query(
                ids="channel==MINE",
                startDate=start_str,
                endDate=end_str,
                metrics="views,estimatedMinutesWatched,averageViewDuration,subscribersGained,subscribersLost",
                dimensions="day",
                sort="day"
            ).execute()
            d_rows = []
            tot_wm = 0
            tot_sg = 0
            dur_weighted = 0.0
            v_sum = 0
            for r in rep_day.get("rows", []):
                day_str, v_c, wm, avd, sg, sl = r[0], int(r[1]), int(r[2]), float(r[3]), int(r[4]), int(r[5])
                net_s = sg - sl
                tot_wm += wm
                tot_sg += net_s
                dur_weighted += v_c * avd
                v_sum += v_c
                d_rows.append({
                    "Date": day_str,
                    "Views": v_c,
                    "Watch Minutes": wm,
                    "Avg Duration (s)": round(avd, 1),
                    "Net Subscribers": net_s
                })
            if d_rows and sum(x["Views"] for x in d_rows) > 0:
                result["daily_rows"] = d_rows
            if tot_wm > 0:
                result["totals"]["watch_time_minutes_period"] = tot_wm
            result["totals"]["subs_gained_period"] = tot_sg
            if v_sum > 0:
                result["totals"]["avg_view_duration_sec"] = round(dur_weighted / v_sum, 1)
        except Exception as e_day:
            result["analytics_api_notice"] = str(e_day)[:160]

        # B. Geography (Country Breakdown with Watch Time & Retention)
        try:
            rep_geo = yt_ana.reports().query(
                ids="channel==MINE",
                startDate=start_str,
                endDate=end_str,
                metrics="views,estimatedMinutesWatched,averageViewDuration,averageViewPercentage,subscribersGained",
                dimensions="country",
                sort="-views",
                maxResults=15
            ).execute()
            g_rows = rep_geo.get("rows", [])
            tot_gv = sum(int(r[1]) for r in g_rows) or 1
            for r in g_rows:
                cc = str(r[0]).upper()
                v_c = int(r[1])
                wm = int(r[2])
                avd = round(float(r[3]), 1)
                apv = round(float(r[4]), 1)
                sg = int(r[5])
                result["geo_rows"].append({
                    "Country": COUNTRY_MAP.get(cc, f"{cc} \U0001f310"),
                    "Code": cc,
                    "Views": v_c,
                    "Share %": round((v_c / tot_gv) * 100.0, 1),
                    "Watch Minutes": wm,
                    "Avg View (s)": avd,
                    "Avg Retention %": apv,
                    "Subs Gained": sg
                })
        except Exception as e_geo:
            if not result["analytics_api_notice"]:
                result["analytics_api_notice"] = str(e_geo)[:160]

        # C. Traffic Sources (Shorts Feed vs Search vs Suggested)
        try:
            rep_tr = yt_ana.reports().query(
                ids="channel==MINE",
                startDate=start_str,
                endDate=end_str,
                metrics="views,estimatedMinutesWatched",
                dimensions="insightTrafficSourceType",
                sort="-views"
            ).execute()
            t_rows = rep_tr.get("rows", [])
            tot_tv = sum(int(r[1]) for r in t_rows) or 1
            for r in t_rows:
                raw_src = str(r[0])
                v_c = int(r[1])
                wm = int(r[2])
                result["traffic_rows"].append({
                    "Traffic Source": TRAFFIC_SOURCE_MAP.get(raw_src, raw_src),
                    "Views": v_c,
                    "Share %": round((v_c / tot_tv) * 100.0, 1),
                    "Watch Minutes": wm
                })
        except Exception:
            pass

        # D. Device Types (Mobile vs TV vs Desktop)
        try:
            rep_dev = yt_ana.reports().query(
                ids="channel==MINE",
                startDate=start_str,
                endDate=end_str,
                metrics="views,estimatedMinutesWatched",
                dimensions="deviceType",
                sort="-views"
            ).execute()
            dv_rows = rep_dev.get("rows", [])
            tot_dv = sum(int(r[1]) for r in dv_rows) or 1
            for r in dv_rows:
                dev_name = str(r[0]).title()
                v_c = int(r[1])
                wm = int(r[2])
                result["device_rows"].append({
                    "Device": dev_name,
                    "Views": v_c,
                    "Share %": round((v_c / tot_dv) * 100.0, 1),
                    "Watch Minutes": wm
                })
        except Exception:
            pass

    except Exception as e_ana:
        result["analytics_api_notice"] = str(e_ana)[:160]

    return result



==========================================
=== FILE: clipper.py ===

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import subprocess
import re
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import date
from dotenv import load_dotenv
import yt_dlp
from google import genai
from google.genai import types
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

# Base directories
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CLIPS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "clips"
QUOTA_FILE = BASE_DIR / ".gemini_quota.json"
BGM_FILE = BASE_DIR / "bg_music.mp3"
NICHE_FOLDER_NAME = "TheDailyWisdom"

load_dotenv(BASE_DIR / ".env")
API_KEY = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
if not API_KEY:
    print("\n[ERROR] GEMINI_API_KEY is missing! Add it inside your .env file.")
    sys.exit(1)

client = genai.Client(api_key=API_KEY)


class VideoClip(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(description="Short, punchy title for this clip")
    start_time: str = Field(description="Start timestamp (e.g. 00:01:23 or 01:23)")
    end_time: str = Field(description="End timestamp (e.g. 00:01:55 or 01:55)")
    reason: str = Field(description="Why this segment is viral")


class ClipsResponse(BaseModel):
    clips: list[VideoClip]


def clean_name(name: str) -> str:
    clean = re.sub(r'[\/*?:"<>|]', "", name)
    clean = re.sub(r"\s+", "_", clean).strip("_")
    return clean[:50] if clean else "Video_Short"


def timestamp_to_seconds(ts: str) -> float:
    parts = [float(p) for p in ts.strip().split(":")]
    if len(parts) == 3:
        return parts[0] * 3600 + parts[1] * 60 + parts[2]
    elif len(parts) == 2:
        return parts[0] * 60 + parts[1]
    return parts[0]


def ensure_background_music():
    """Checks for bg_music.mp3; downloads or generates a fallback track."""
    if not BGM_FILE.exists():
        print("\n[Audio] 'bg_music.mp3' not found. Downloading soft ambient track...")
        url = "https://ia801503.us.archive.org/15/items/ambient-relaxing-music/ambient-calm.mp3"
        try:
            urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, BGM_FILE)
            print(" Downloaded background music successfully!")
        except Exception:
            print(" Generating soft ambient pad audio fallback...")
            cmd = [
                "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi",
                "-i", "sine=frequency=110:sample_rate=44100",
                "-t", "120", "-af", "volume=0.03,lowpass=f=300",
                str(BGM_FILE)
            ]
            subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)


def track_quota():
    today_str = date.today().isoformat()
    quota_data = {"date": today_str, "used": 0}

    if QUOTA_FILE.exists():
        try:
            with open(QUOTA_FILE, "r") as f:
                saved = json.load(f)
            if saved.get("date") == today_str:
                quota_data = saved
        except Exception:
            pass

    quota_data["used"] += 1
    remaining = max(0, 1500 - quota_data["used"])

    with open(QUOTA_FILE, "w") as f:
        json.dump(quota_data, f, indent=2)

    return quota_data["used"], remaining


def download_youtube_video(url: str, output_path: Path) -> str:
    print(f"\n[1/4] Downloading 1080p HD master video...")
    ydl_opts = {
        "format": "bestvideo[height<=1080][ext=mp4]+bestaudio[ext=m4a]/best[height<=1080]/best",
        "outtmpl": str(output_path),
        "merge_output_format": "mp4",
        "overwrites": True,
        "quiet": True,
    }

    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
        info = ydl.extract_info(url, download=True)
        video_title = info.get("title", "Untitled_Video")

    print(f" Video Title: '{video_title}'")
    return video_title


def analyze_video_with_gemini(video_path: Path, max_clips: int = 3) -> tuple[list[VideoClip], dict]:
    print("\n[2/4] Uploading video to Gemini...")
    video_file = client.files.upload(file=str(video_path))
    print(" Upload complete. Waiting for processing...")

    while video_file.state.name == "PROCESSING":
        time.sleep(3)
        video_file = client.files.get(name=video_file.name)

    if video_file.state.name == "FAILED":
        raise ValueError("Gemini processing failed on this video.")

    print("\n[3/4] Finding high-retention viral moments...")
    prompt = f"""
    You are an expert short-form editor for TikTok, YouTube Shorts, and Reels in the wisdom/habits/podcast niche.
    Select the top {max_clips} viral, high-retention segments.

    Rules:
    - Clip length: 20 to 60 seconds each.
    - Start right when the hook begins; stop after conclusion.
    - Provide exact timestamps (HH:MM:SS or MM:SS format).
    """

    # Use 'gemini-2.0-flash' or 'gemini-1.5-flash'
    response = client.models.generate_content(
        model="gemini-2.0-flash",
        contents=[video_file, prompt],
        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
            response_mime_type="application/json",
            response_schema=ClipsResponse,
            temperature=0.3,
        ),
    )

    try:
        client.files.delete(name=video_file.name)
    except Exception:
        pass

    usage = {
        "prompt_tokens": getattr(response.usage_metadata, "prompt_token_count", 0),
        "output_tokens": getattr(response.usage_metadata, "candidates_token_count", 0),
        "total_tokens": getattr(response.usage_metadata, "total_token_count", 0),
    }

    data = json.loads(response.text)
    return [VideoClip(**clip) for clip in data.get("clips", [])], usage


def render_full_screen(input_file: Path, clips: list[VideoClip], video_title: str):
    w, h = 1080, 1920
    target_folder = CLIPS_DIR / NICHE_FOLDER_NAME / clean_name(video_title)
    target_folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print(f"\n[4/4] Output Directory: {target_folder.resolve()}")
    print(f" Rendering {len(clips)} Full-Screen Ultra-HD Shorts...")

    for i, clip in enumerate(clips):
        clip_name = clean_name(clip.title)
        out_file = target_folder / f"short_{i+1}_{clip_name}.mp4"

        start_sec = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.start_time)
        end_sec = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.end_time)
        duration = max(1.0, end_sec - start_sec)

        print(f"\n⚡ Rendering Full-Screen Short #{i+1}: '{clip.title}' ({round(duration)}s)")

        # Filter Breakdown with PTS reset so progress bar starts accurately at t=0
        filter_complex = (
            f"[0:v]setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,scale=-2:{h}:flags=lanczos,crop={w}:{h}:(in_w-{w})/2:0,"
            f"unsharp=5:5:0.85:5:5:0.0,eq=contrast=1.05:saturation=1.08[v_base];"
            f"[v_base]drawbox=x=0:y=1902:w='min({w}, {w}*t/{duration})':h=14:color=0xFFCC00@1:t=fill[vout];"
            f"[0:a]asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo[speech];"
            f"[1:a]volume=0.06,aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo[bgm];"
            f"[speech][bgm]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:dropout_transition=2[aout]"
        )

        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg", "-y",
            "-ss", str(start_sec),
            "-t", str(duration),
            "-i", str(input_file),
            "-stream_loop", "-1",
            "-i", str(BGM_FILE),
            "-filter_complex", filter_complex,
            "-map", "[vout]",
            "-map", "[aout]",
            "-c:v", "libx264",
            "-preset", "veryfast",
            "-crf", "19",
            "-c:a", "aac",
            "-b:a", "192k",
            str(out_file)
        ]

        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
        if result.returncode != 0:
            print(f"[FFmpeg Error on clip {i+1}]: {result.stderr[-400:]}")
            continue

        print(f" Saved: {out_file.name}")

    return target_folder


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ensure_background_music()

    url = input("Paste your YouTube Video URL: ").strip()
    if not url:
        print("URL cannot be empty.")
        sys.exit(1)

    master_file = BASE_DIR / "temp_master.mp4"

    try:
        start_time = time.time()

        video_title = download_youtube_video(url, master_file)
        clips, usage = analyze_video_with_gemini(master_file, max_clips=3)
        output_folder = render_full_screen(master_file, clips, video_title=video_title)

        if master_file.exists():
            master_file.unlink()

        used_today, remaining_today = track_quota()
        total_duration = round(time.time() - start_time, 1)

        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print(f" COMPLETED IN JUST {total_duration} SECONDS!")
        print(f" Saved in: {output_folder.resolve()}")
        print("=" * 60)
        print("\n📊 GEMINI USAGE & REMAINING LIMITS:")
        print(f"  • Video Tokens Analyzed:   {usage['prompt_tokens']:,}")
        print(f"  • Output Tokens:           {usage['output_tokens']:,}")
        print("  --------------------------------------------------")
        print(f"  • Requests Made Today:     {used_today} / 1,500")
        print(f"  • REQUESTS REMAINING TODAY: {remaining_today} requests left")
        print("=" * 60)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n[Execution Error]: {e}")
