import os
import re
import csv
import io
import json
import time
import base64
import shutil
import asyncio
import urllib.request
import urllib.parse
import subprocess
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont, ImageEnhance, ImageFilter
import edge_tts

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ENV_FILE = BASE_DIR / ".env"
load_dotenv(ENV_FILE)

STORY_MOVIES_DIR = BASE_DIR / "story_movies"
STORY_MOVIES_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Cache the working image model so we never waste time retrying failed endpoints on every scene
_WORKING_IMG_MODEL: Optional[str] = None

def get_active_gemini_key() -> str:
    load_dotenv(ENV_FILE, override=True)
    return os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "").strip()

def save_gemini_key_to_env(new_key: str) -> bool:
    clean_k = new_key.strip()
    if not clean_k:
        return False
    os.environ["GEMINI_API_KEY"] = clean_k
    lines = []
    if ENV_FILE.exists():
        lines = ENV_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    found = False
    new_lines = []
    for ln in lines:
        if ln.strip().startswith("GEMINI_API_KEY="):
            new_lines.append(f"GEMINI_API_KEY={clean_k}")
            found = True
        else:
            new_lines.append(ln)
    if not found:
        new_lines.append(f"GEMINI_API_KEY={clean_k}")
    ENV_FILE.write_text("\n".join(new_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return True

def test_gemini_key_capabilities(api_key: str = "") -> Dict[str, str]:
    key = (api_key or get_active_gemini_key()).strip()
    if not key:
        return {
            "text_brain": "No API Key Found",
            "imagen_api": "Using HD Super-Sharp Fallback",
            "veo_video_api": "Using Local Motion / Flow Bridge"
        }
    report = {
        "text_brain": "Failed",
        "imagen_api": "Flux HD + 2K Lanczos Enhancer Ready",
        "veo_video_api": "Local 3D Motion + Flow Bridge Ready"
    }
    try:
        from google import genai
        client = genai.Client(api_key=key)
        for bm in ["gemini-3.7-flash", "gemini-3.6-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"]:
            try:
                res = client.models.generate_content(model=bm, contents="Say OK")
                if res and res.text:
                    report["text_brain"] = f"Connected ({bm})"
                    break
            except Exception as e_b:
                report["text_brain"] = f"Error on {bm}: {str(e_b)[:50]}"
        avail = [m.name.lower() for m in client.models.list()]
        img_m = [x for x in avail if "banana" in x or "image" in x or "imagen" in x]
        vid_m = [x for x in avail if "omni" in x or "veo" in x]
        if img_m:
            report["imagen_api"] = f"Visible on Key: {', '.join(img_m[:2])}"
        if vid_m:
            report["veo_video_api"] = f"Visible on Key: {', '.join(vid_m[:2])}"
    except Exception as e:
        report["text_brain"] = f"Error: {str(e)[:60]}"
    return report

def master_sharp_hd_image(img_path: Path, target_w: int, target_h: int):
    if not img_path.exists():
        return
    try:
        im = Image.open(img_path).convert("RGB")
        ir = im.width / max(1, im.height)
        tr = target_w / max(1, target_h)
        nw, nh = (int(target_h * ir), target_h) if ir > tr else (target_w, int(target_w / max(0.01, ir)))
        im = im.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        left = (nw - target_w) // 2
        top = (nh - target_h) // 2
        im = im.crop((left, top, left + target_w, top + target_h))
        im = im.filter(ImageFilter.UnsharpMask(radius=1.6, percent=135, threshold=2))
        im = ImageEnhance.Contrast(im).enhance(1.08)
        im = ImageEnhance.Color(im).enhance(1.06)
        im = ImageEnhance.Sharpness(im).enhance(1.25)
        im.save(str(img_path), "JPEG", quality=96, subsampling=0)
    except Exception:
        pass

PRESET_STORY_TOPICS: List[str] = [

def _extract_json(text: str) -> dict | None:
    if not text:
        return None
    s = text.find("{")
    e = text.rfind("}")
    if s != -1 and e != -1 and e > s:
        try:
            return json.loads(text[s:e+1])
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None

def analyze_and_create_story_board(
    story_topic: str,
    aspect_ratio: str = "16:9 Horizontal (1920x1080)",
    num_scenes: int = 12,
    log_cb=None
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    clean_topic = re.sub(r"^\d+\.\s*", "", story_topic).strip()
    key = get_active_gemini_key()
    orient_str = "vertical 9:16 portrait (1080x1920)" if "9:16" in aspect_ratio else "horizontal 16:9 widescreen (1920x1080)"

    if log_cb:
        log_cb(f"Analyzing story topic '{clean_topic}' ({num_scenes} scenes, {orient_str})...")

    prompt = (
        f"You are an elite Christian Film Director and Google Flow (Gemini Omni 1.2 Flash & Nano Banana 2.1) Prompt Architect.\n"
        f"Create a 3-MINUTE (180-second) Biblical/Faith Short Story Movie broken into EXACTLY {num_scenes} scenes (15s per scene).\n"
        f"Story Topic: {clean_topic}\n"
        f"Aspect Ratio: {orient_str}\n\n"
        f"Respond ONLY in valid JSON with keys: story_title, anchor_verse_ref, anchor_verse_text, character_dna, camera_style_lock, thumbnail_hook, "
        f"and 'scenes' (list of {num_scenes} objects with scene_num, act, narration [28-35 words], flow_image_prompt [full descriptive sentence including character_dna], flow_video_prompt [15s camera + subject motion], caption)."
    )

    ai_data = None
    if key:
        try:
            from google import genai
            from google.genai import types
            client = genai.Client(api_key=key)
            for m_id in ["gemini-3.7-flash", "gemini-3.6-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"]:
                try:
                    if log_cb:
                        log_cb(f"Requesting 12-scene storyboard from Google model '{m_id}'...")
                    res = client.models.generate_content(
                        model=m_id,
                        contents=prompt,
                        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(response_mime_type="application/json", temperature=0.72)
                    )
                    if res and res.text:
                        ai_data = _extract_json(res.text)
                        if ai_data and ai_data.get("scenes"):
                            if log_cb:
                                log_cb(f"SUCCESS: '{m_id}' generated {len(ai_data['scenes'])} scenes!")
                            break
                except Exception as e_m:
                    if log_cb:
                        log_cb(f"Model '{m_id}' notice: {str(e_m)[:90]}")
                    continue
        except Exception as e_Outer:
            if log_cb:
                log_cb(f"Gemini SDK error: {str(e_Outer)[:90]}")

    if not ai_data or not ai_data.get("scenes"):
        if log_cb:
            log_cb("Using built-in high-retention 12-scene storyboard template...")
        char_dna = "A 33-year-old Middle-Eastern man with warm expressive brown eyes, a neatly trimmed dark beard, and shoulder-length wavy dark-brown hair, wearing a textured cream-white handwoven linen tunic with an earth-brown wool shawl."
        cam_lock = f"Photorealistic {orient_str} cinematic frame on Arri Alexa 65, 50mm anamorphic lens at f/1.8, golden-hour volumetric rays, razor-sharp 8K photorealism."
        fallback_scenes = []
        acts = [
            ("Act 1: The Hook", "In the quiet stillness of the valley, a heavy burden pressed down until there seemed to be no way forward and no strength left to stand."),
            ("Act 1: The Hook", "Every human door had closed, and the whispers of doubt grew louder in the shadows of the night, testing the very foundation of faith."),
            ("Act 1: The Hook", "Yet even in the darkest hour, heaven was watching closely, preparing a miracle that no human eye could yet see."),
            ("Act 2: The Breaking Point", "Falling to his knees on the cold stone floor, he lifted trembling hands toward heaven and whispered a prayer from the depths of his soul."),
            ("Act 2: The Breaking Point", "He surrendered every fear, every broken plan, and every tear into the hands of the Almighty, choosing to trust when nothing made sense."),
            ("Act 3: The Divine Encounter", "Suddenly, a warm golden light broke through the darkness, filling the room with an unshakeable peace that surpassed all understanding."),
            ("Act 3: The Divine Encounter", "The voice of the Lord spoke softly to his spirit: Fear not, for I am with you; I will never leave you nor forsake you."),
            ("Act 3: The Divine Encounter", "Strength returned to his weary bones as he rose to his feet, knowing the battle belonged not to man, but to the living God."),
            ("Act 4: The Climax", "When the moment of testing arrived, what looked like an impossible giant began to crumble before the power of unwavering faith."),
            ("Act 4: The Climax", "Doors that had been locked for years swung wide open, and God poured out a blessing so abundant there was not room enough to contain it."),
            ("Act 5: The Moral & Scripture", "Those who had witnessed the struggle stood in awe, realizing that when God fights for you, no weapon formed against you shall prosper."),
            ("Act 5: The Moral & Scripture", "Whatever giant you are facing today, do not give up. Place your trust in Jesus Christ, for your story is about to turn into a testimony. Amen.")
        ]
        for idx, (act_name, narr) in enumerate(acts[:num_scenes], start=1):
            fallback_scenes.append({
                "scene_num": idx,
                "act": act_name,
                "narration": narr,
                "flow_image_prompt": f"{cam_lock} {char_dna} In this scene: {narr} Crisp focus on the eyes, natural lighting, zero text.",
                "flow_video_prompt": f"One continuous 15-second slow cinematic dolly push-in shot at 24fps. {char_dna} Subtle breathing and eye movement as wind stirs the linen tunic and golden light rays shift.",
                "caption": f"Scene {idx}: {act_name.split(':')[-1].strip()}"
            })
        ai_data = {
            "story_title": f"{clean_topic[:55]} | 3-Minute Faith Story",
            "anchor_verse_ref": "Isaiah 41:10",
            "anchor_verse_text": "Fear thou not; for I am with thee: be not dismayed; for I am thy God: I will strengthen thee.",
            "character_dna": char_dna,
            "camera_style_lock": cam_lock,
            "thumbnail_hook": "GOD FOUGHT THE BATTLE",
            "scenes": fallback_scenes
        }

    return {
        "topic": clean_topic,
        "aspect_ratio": aspect_ratio,
        "story_title": ai_data.get("story_title", clean_topic),
        "anchor_verse_ref": ai_data.get("anchor_verse_ref", "Isaiah 41:10"),
        "anchor_verse_text": ai_data.get("anchor_verse_text", ""),
        "character_dna": ai_data.get("character_dna", ""),
        "camera_style_lock": ai_data.get("camera_style_lock", ""),
        "thumbnail_hook": ai_data.get("thumbnail_hook", "GOD NEVER FAILS"),
        "scenes": ai_data.get("scenes", [])
    }

def generate_scene_image(
    prompt: str,
    out_img_path: Path,
    aspect_ratio: str = "16:9 Horizontal",
    seed: int = 42,
    image_model_pref: str = "Auto",
    log_cb=None
) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    global _WORKING_IMG_MODEL
    out_img_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    is_vert = "9:16" in aspect_ratio
    w, h = (1080, 1920) if is_vert else (1920, 1080)
    orient_hint = "Vertical 9:16 portrait composition." if is_vert else "Horizontal 16:9 widescreen cinematic composition."
    full_img_prompt = f"Generate a photorealistic, ultra-sharp 4K cinematic photograph ({orient_hint}): {prompt}"

    key = get_active_gemini_key()
    if key and "Flux Only" not in image_model_pref and _WORKING_IMG_MODEL != "FLUX_FALLBACK":
        try:
            from google import genai
            client = genai.Client(api_key=key)
            candidates = [_WORKING_IMG_MODEL] if _WORKING_IMG_MODEL else [
                "gemini-2.0-flash-preview-image-generation",
                "gemini-2.5-flash-image"
            ]
            for bm in candidates:
                if not bm:
                    continue
                try:
                    res = client.models.generate_content(model=bm, contents=[full_img_prompt])
                    for cand in getattr(res, "candidates", []) or []:
                        for part in getattr(cand.content, "parts", []) or []:
                            inl = getattr(part, "inline_data", None)
                            if inl and getattr(inl, "data", None):
                                raw_b = base64.b64decode(inl.data) if isinstance(inl.data, str) else inl.data
                                if len(raw_b) > 5000:
                                    out_img_path.write_bytes(raw_b)
                                    master_sharp_hd_image(out_img_path, w, h)
                                    _WORKING_IMG_MODEL = bm
                                    return True, f"Google {bm}"
                except Exception as e_img:
                    if log_cb and not _WORKING_IMG_MODEL:
                        log_cb(f"Google Image API ({bm}): {str(e_img)[:75]} -> Switching to Fast Flux HD")
                    continue
            if not _WORKING_IMG_MODEL:
                _WORKING_IMG_MODEL = "FLUX_FALLBACK"
        except Exception:
            _WORKING_IMG_MODEL = "FLUX_FALLBACK"

    # Fast High-Bitrate Flux HD + 2K Lanczos Mastering
    try:
        clean_p = urllib.parse.quote(
            f"masterpiece, award-winning 8k RAW cinema photograph, Arri Alexa 65, razor sharp focus, {prompt[:750]}"
        )
        flux_url = f"https://image.pollinations.ai/prompt/{clean_p}?width={w}&height={h}&seed={seed}&model=flux&enhance=true&nologo=true"
        req = urllib.request.Request(flux_url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=22) as resp:
            data = resp.read()
            if data and len(data) > 8000:
                out_img_path.write_bytes(data)
                master_sharp_hd_image(out_img_path, w, h)
                return True, "Flux HD + 2K Lanczos Super-Sharp"
    except Exception as e_flx:
        if log_cb:
            log_cb(f"Flux image note: {str(e_flx)[:70]} -> Using local Jesus HD wallpaper")

    # Fallback: Copy an existing high-res Jesus image from custom_media/backgrounds so it is NEVER a blank box
    bg_dir = BASE_DIR / "custom_media" / "backgrounds"
    existing_bgs = sorted(list(bg_dir.glob("*.jpg")) + list(bg_dir.glob("*.png"))) if bg_dir.exists() else []
    if existing_bgs:
        chosen_fb = existing_bgs[seed % len(existing_bgs)]
        shutil.copy(chosen_fb, out_img_path)
        master_sharp_hd_image(out_img_path, w, h)
        return True, f"Studio HD Vault ({chosen_fb.name})"

    im = Image.new("RGB", (w, h), (10, 15, 28))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.rounded_rectangle([40, 40, w - 40, h - 40], radius=30, outline=(225, 185, 65), width=4)
    im.save(str(out_img_path), "JPEG", quality=96, subsampling=0)
    return True, "Local HD Canvas"

def generate_all_scene_images_parallel(
    storyboard: Dict[str, Any],
    scenes_dir: Path,
    image_model_pref: str = "Auto",
    progress_cb=None,
    log_cb=None
) -> int:
    """Generates scene images in parallel (4 workers) so 12 scenes finish in ~20s with live logs!"""
    scenes = storyboard.get("scenes", [])
    ar = storyboard.get("aspect_ratio", "16:9")
    total = max(1, len(scenes))
    completed = 0

    def _worker(sc_obj):
        s_num = sc_obj["scene_num"]
        t0 = time.time()
        out_p = scenes_dir / f"scene_{s_num:02d}.jpg"
        ok, eng = generate_scene_image(
            sc_obj["flow_image_prompt"], out_p, ar, seed=400 + s_num, image_model_pref=image_model_pref, log_cb=None
        )
        dt = time.time() - t0
        return s_num, ok, eng, dt

    if log_cb:
        log_cb(f"Starting parallel HD image generation for {total} scenes (4 concurrent workers)...")

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
        futures = {pool.submit(_worker, sc): sc["scene_num"] for sc in scenes}
        for fut in as_completed(futures):
            s_num, ok, eng, dt = fut.result()
            completed += 1
            pct = int(completed / total * 100)
            msg = f"Scene {s_num:02d}/{total} HD Image Ready in {dt:.1f}s ({eng})"
            if log_cb:
                log_cb(f"[OK] {msg}")
            if progress_cb:
                progress_cb(pct, msg)

    return completed

def render_local_3d_motion_clip(
    sc_img: Path,
    out_mp4: Path,
    duration_sec: float = 8.0,
    aspect_ratio: str = "16:9 Horizontal",
    scene_num: int = 1
) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Renders a smooth 1080p 30fps camera-motion MP4 clip locally from the scene's HD image in ~2.5s!"""
    is_vert = "9:16" in aspect_ratio
    w, h = (1080, 1920) if is_vert else (1920, 1080)
    if not sc_img.exists():
        return False, f"Scene {scene_num} image missing"

    sw, sh = int(w * 1.15), int(h * 1.15)
    if scene_num % 2 == 1:
        crop_expr = f"crop={w}:{h}:'(in_w-out_w)/2':'(in_h-out_h)*(0.15+0.70*t/{duration_sec:.2f})'"
    else:
        crop_expr = f"crop={w}:{h}:'(in_w-out_w)*(0.15+0.70*t/{duration_sec:.2f})':'(in_h-out_h)/2'"

    vf_chain = f"scale={sw}:{sh}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase:flags=lanczos,{crop_expr},fps=30,unsharp=5:5:0.9:5:5:0.0,format=yuv420p,setsar=1"
    cmd = [
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-threads", "0",
        "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{duration_sec:.2f}", "-i", str(sc_img.absolute()),
        "-vf", vf_chain,
        "-an", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "18",
        str(out_mp4.absolute())
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    if res.returncode == 0 and out_mp4.exists() and out_mp4.stat().st_size > 5000:
        return True, f"Rendered 1080p 30fps Motion Clip ({duration_sec:.0f}s)"
    return False, f"FFmpeg error: {res.stderr[-160:]}"

def generate_scene_video_omni(
    video_prompt: str,
    out_mp4_path: Path,
    ref_image_path: Path | None = None,
    aspect_ratio: str = "16:9 Horizontal",
    duration_sec: int = 10,
    resolution: str = "1080p",
    scene_num: int = 1,
    allow_local_fallback: bool = True,
    log_cb=None
) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """
    1. Tries Google's Video API (Omni / Veo) and logs the exact response or error immediately.
    2. If API returns 429/404 (Free-Tier key without Paid Video Billing), automatically renders
       a 1080p 30fps 3D Camera Motion video clip from the Scene's HD Image so it NEVER fails!
    """
    key = get_active_gemini_key()
    ar_str = "9:16" if "9:16" in aspect_ratio else "16:9"
    dur_str = f"{min(15, max(5, int(duration_sec)))}s"

    if key:
        try:
            url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/interactions?key={key}"
            payload = {
                "model": "models/gemini-omni-1.2-flash",
                "input": video_prompt,
                "response_format": {
                    "aspect_ratio": ar_str,
                    "resolution": resolution,
                    "duration": dur_str
                }
            }
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                url,
                headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "x-goog-api-key": key},
                data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=25) as resp:
                r_data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                vid_b64 = r_data.get("output_video", {}).get("data")
                if not vid_b64:
                    for o_it in r_data.get("outputs", []):
                        if o_it.get("type") == "video" and o_it.get("data"):
                            vid_b64 = o_it["data"]
                            break
                if vid_b64:
                    out_mp4_path.write_bytes(base64.b64decode(vid_b64))
                    return True, "Generated via Gemini Omni 1.2 Flash API"
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as he:
            err_body = ""
            try:
                err_body = he.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")[:140]
            except Exception:
                pass
            api_err_msg = f"Google Omni API HTTP {he.code}: {err_body or he.reason}"
            if log_cb:
                log_cb(f"[API Notice] Scene {scene_num}: {api_err_msg}")
            if not allow_local_fallback:
                return False, api_err_msg
        except Exception as e_gen:
            if log_cb:
                log_cb(f"[API Notice] Scene {scene_num}: {str(e_gen)[:100]}")
            if not allow_local_fallback:
                return False, str(e_gen)[:100]

    # Automatic Local 1080p 3D Camera Motion Fallback so the user ALWAYS gets a playable MP4 clip
    if allow_local_fallback and ref_image_path and ref_image_path.exists():
        if log_cb:
            log_cb(f"Scene {scene_num}: Rendering instant 1080p 30fps 3D Camera Motion clip from Scene Image...")
        return render_local_3d_motion_clip(ref_image_path, out_mp4_path, duration_sec=float(duration_sec), aspect_ratio=aspect_ratio, scene_num=scene_num)

    return False, "No video generated (Generate Scene Images first or enable local motion fallback)."

def build_google_flow_csv_bytes(storyboard: Dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
    out = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(out)
    writer.writerow(["Scene", "Act", "Model", "Duration", "Aspect_Ratio", "Image_Ingredient_Prompt", "Omni_Video_Motion_Prompt", "Spoken_Narration"])
    ar = "9:16" if "9:16" in storyboard.get("aspect_ratio", "") else "16:9"
    for sc in storyboard.get("scenes", []):
        writer.writerow([
            f"Scene_{sc['scene_num']:02d}",
            sc.get("act", ""),
            "gemini-omni-1.2-flash",
            "15s",
            ar,
            sc.get("flow_image_prompt", ""),
            sc.get("flow_video_prompt", ""),
            sc.get("narration", "")
        ])
    return out.getvalue().encode("utf-8")

def get_media_dur(p: Path) -> float:
    try:
        cmd = ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1", str(p.absolute())]
        r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=8, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        return float(r.stdout.strip())
    except Exception:
        return 0.0

def get_next_story_folder(title: str) -> Path:
    clean = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9]+", "_", title or "").strip("_")[:35] or "story_movie"
    nums = []
    for d in STORY_MOVIES_DIR.iterdir():
        if d.is_dir():
            m = re.match(r"^ST(\d{3})_", d.name)
            if m:
                nums.append(int(m.group(1)))
    next_n = (max(nums) + 1) if nums else 1
    folder = STORY_MOVIES_DIR / f"ST{next_n:03d}_{clean}"
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return folder

def create_scene_subtitle_overlay_png(out_png: Path, text_line1: str, text_line2: str, act_label: str, w: int, h: int):
    """
    Renders a crystal-clear transparent RGBA subtitle & act badge overlay using Pillow.
    100% immune to FFmpeg drawtext apostrophe/quote crashes!
    """
    ov = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)

    is_vert = h > w
    f_sz = 40 if is_vert else 36
    f_act_sz = 24 if is_vert else 22

    font_main = ImageFont.load_default()
    font_act = ImageFont.load_default()
    for fp in ["C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeuib.ttf", "C:/Windows/Fonts/arialbd.ttf", "C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf"]:
        if Path(fp).exists():
            try:
                font_main = ImageFont.truetype(fp, f_sz)
                font_act = ImageFont.truetype(fp, f_act_sz)
                break
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Bottom gradient bar for readability
    bar_h = 200 if is_vert else 150
    for y in range(h - bar_h, h):
        alpha = int(215 * ((y - (h - bar_h)) / float(bar_h)))
        draw.line([(0, y), (w, y)], fill=(5, 8, 16, alpha))

    # Top-left subtle Act pill
    if act_label:
        ab = draw.textbbox((0, 0), act_label.upper(), font=font_act)
        aw, ah = ab[2] - ab[0] + 36, ab[3] - ab[1] + 18
        draw.rounded_rectangle([36, 36, 36 + aw, 36 + ah], radius=ah // 2, fill=(8, 13, 26, 200), outline=(225, 185, 65, 200), width=2)
        draw.text((54 - ab[0], 45 - ab[1]), act_label.upper(), font=font_act, fill=(255, 240, 175, 255))

    y1 = h - (155 if is_vert else 115)
    y2 = y1 + (52 if is_vert else 46)

    for ln_txt, y_pos, col in [(text_line1, y1, (255, 255, 255, 255)), (text_line2, y2, (255, 215, 0, 255))]:
        if not ln_txt:
            continue
        bb = draw.textbbox((0, 0), ln_txt, font=font_main, stroke_width=4)
        tw = bb[2] - bb[0]
        tx = (w - tw) // 2 - bb[0]
        draw.text((tx, y_pos), ln_txt, font=font_main, fill=col, stroke_width=4, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 245))

    ov.save(str(out_png), "PNG")

def render_complete_3min_story_movie(
    storyboard: Dict[str, Any],
    work_folder: Path,
    voice_name: str = "en-US-ChristopherNeural",
    voice_rate: str = "-6%",
    bgm_file: Path | None = None,
    bgm_vol: float = 0.16,
    palette_name: str = "Gold Classic",
    progress_cb=None,
    log_cb=None
) -> Tuple[bool, Path, Path]:
    import pro_design_addon as pda
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    is_vert = "9:16" in storyboard.get("aspect_ratio", "")
    w, h = (1080, 1920) if is_vert else (1920, 1080)

    scenes_dir = work_folder / "scenes"
    scenes_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Step A: Ensure all scene images exist first (parallel if missing)
    scenes_list = storyboard.get("scenes", [])
    total_sc = max(1, len(scenes_list))
    missing_imgs = [
        sc for sc in scenes_list
        if not (scenes_dir / f"scene_{sc['scene_num']:02d}.jpg").exists()
        and not (scenes_dir / f"scene_{sc['scene_num']:02d}_flow.mp4").exists()
    ]
    if missing_imgs:
        if log_cb:
            log_cb(f"Generating {len(missing_imgs)} missing scene image(s) in parallel first...")
        generate_all_scene_images_parallel(storyboard, scenes_dir, progress_cb=progress_cb, log_cb=log_cb)

    sil_mp3 = scenes_dir / "pause_1_2s.mp3"
    subprocess.run([
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-t", "1.20", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-q:a", "2", str(sil_mp3.absolute())
    ], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    async def _tts(txt: str, out_p: Path):
        clean_t = re.sub(r"(\d+):(\d+)", r"chapter \1 verse \2", txt)
        comm = edge_tts.Communicate(clean_t, voice=voice_name, rate=voice_rate, pitch="-2Hz")
        await comm.save(str(out_p))

    rendered_scene_mp4s: List[Path] = []

    for idx, sc in enumerate(scenes_list, start=1):
        t_sc0 = time.time()
        pct_now = int((idx - 1) / total_sc * 78)
        if progress_cb:
            progress_cb(pct_now, f"Mastering Scene {idx}/{total_sc} ({sc.get('act', '')})...")
        if log_cb:
            log_cb(f"Scene {idx:02d}/{total_sc}: Synthesizing voiceover & mastering 1080p video...")

        raw_v = scenes_dir / f"sc_{idx:02d}_raw.mp3"
        padded_v = scenes_dir / f"sc_{idx:02d}_voice.mp3"
        try:
            asyncio.run(_tts(sc["narration"], raw_v))
        except RuntimeError:
            loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
            loop.run_until_complete(_tts(sc["narration"], raw_v))
            loop.close()

        l_txt = scenes_dir / f"sc_{idx:02d}_aud_list.txt"
        l_txt.write_text(f"file '{raw_v.absolute().as_posix()}'\nfile '{sil_mp3.absolute().as_posix()}'\n", encoding="utf-8")
        subprocess.run([
            "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", str(l_txt.absolute()),
            "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", str(padded_v.absolute())
        ], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

        sc_dur = max(4.0, get_media_dur(padded_v))

        flow_clip = scenes_dir / f"scene_{idx:02d}_flow.mp4"
        sc_img = scenes_dir / f"scene_{idx:02d}.jpg"
        if sc_img.exists():
            master_sharp_hd_image(sc_img, w, h)

        # Build crash-proof Pillow PNG subtitle overlay for this scene
        words = sc.get("narration", "").strip().split()
        mid = len(words) // 2
        line_a = " ".join(words[:mid])[:68]
        line_b = " ".join(words[mid:])[:68]
        sub_png = scenes_dir / f"sc_{idx:02d}_sub.png"
        create_scene_subtitle_overlay_png(sub_png, line_a, line_b, sc.get("act", ""), w, h)

        sc_out_mp4 = scenes_dir / f"scene_{idx:02d}_rendered.mp4"
        sharp_chain = "unsharp=5:5:0.85:5:5:0.0,eq=contrast=1.04:saturation=1.05"

        if flow_clip.exists() and flow_clip.stat().st_size > 5000:
            cmd_sc = [
                "ffmpeg", "-y", "-threads", "0",
                "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{sc_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(flow_clip.absolute()),
                "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{sc_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(sub_png.absolute()),
                "-i", str(padded_v.absolute()),
                "-filter_complex",
                f"[0:v]scale={w}:{h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase:flags=lanczos,crop={w}:{h},fps=24,{sharp_chain},format=yuv420p,setsar=1[bg];"
                f"[bg][1:v]overlay=0:0:format=auto,format=yuv420p[vout]",
                "-map", "[vout]", "-map", "2:a:0",
                "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "18",
                "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-t", f"{sc_dur:.2f}",
                str(sc_out_mp4.absolute())
            ]
        else:
            sw, sh = int(w * 1.14), int(h * 1.14)
            if idx % 2 == 1:
                crop_expr = f"crop={w}:{h}:'(in_w-out_w)/2':'(in_h-out_h)*(0.15+0.70*t/{sc_dur:.2f})'"
            else:
                crop_expr = f"crop={w}:{h}:'(in_w-out_w)*(0.15+0.70*t/{sc_dur:.2f})':'(in_h-out_h)/2'"

            cmd_sc = [
                "ffmpeg", "-y", "-threads", "0",
                "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{sc_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(sc_img.absolute()),
                "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{sc_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(sub_png.absolute()),
                "-i", str(padded_v.absolute()),
                "-filter_complex",
                f"[0:v]scale={sw}:{sh}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase:flags=lanczos,{crop_expr},fps=24,{sharp_chain},format=yuv420p,setsar=1[bg];"
                f"[bg][1:v]overlay=0:0:format=auto,format=yuv420p[vout]",
                "-map", "[vout]", "-map", "2:a:0",
                "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "18",
                "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-t", f"{sc_dur:.2f}",
                str(sc_out_mp4.absolute())
            ]

        res_sc = subprocess.run(cmd_sc, cwd=str(BASE_DIR), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if res_sc.returncode == 0 and sc_out_mp4.exists() and sc_out_mp4.stat().st_size > 5000:
            rendered_scene_mp4s.append(sc_out_mp4)
            if log_cb:
                log_cb(f"[OK] Scene {idx:02d} rendered ({sc_dur:.1f}s clip in {time.time() - t_sc0:.1f}s)")
        else:
            if log_cb:
                log_cb(f"[ERROR] Scene {idx:02d} FFmpeg error: {res_sc.stderr[-220:]}")

    if not rendered_scene_mp4s:
        return False, work_folder, work_folder

    if progress_cb:
        progress_cb(85, "Merging all 1080p scenes & mixing cinematic soundtrack...")
    if log_cb:
        log_cb(f"Concatenating {len(rendered_scene_mp4s)} scenes & mixing background music...")

    concat_vid_txt = scenes_dir / "all_scenes.txt"
    concat_vid_txt.write_text("\n".join([f"file '{p.absolute().as_posix()}'" for p in rendered_scene_mp4s]), encoding="utf-8")
    merged_no_bgm = scenes_dir / "merged_no_bgm.mp4"
    subprocess.run([
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", str(concat_vid_txt.absolute()),
        "-c", "copy", str(merged_no_bgm.absolute())
    ], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

    total_movie_dur = max(10.0, get_media_dur(merged_no_bgm))
    final_movie_mp4 = work_folder / f"{work_folder.name}.mp4"
    final_thumb_jpg = work_folder / f"{work_folder.name}_thumb.jpg"

    if bgm_file and Path(bgm_file).exists():
        fade_out_st = max(1.0, total_movie_dur - 5.0)
        subprocess.run([
            "ffmpeg", "-y",
            "-i", str(merged_no_bgm.absolute()),
            "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{total_movie_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(Path(bgm_file).absolute()),
            "-filter_complex",
            f"[0:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume=1.0[v];"
            f"[1:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume={bgm_vol:.2f},afade=t=out:st={fade_out_st:.1f}:d=5[bgm];"
            f"[v][bgm]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:normalize=0[aout]",
            "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "[aout]",
            "-c:v", "copy", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k",
            str(final_movie_mp4.absolute())
        ], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    else:
        shutil.copy(merged_no_bgm, final_movie_mp4)

    first_img = scenes_dir / "scene_01.jpg"
    if first_img.exists():
        try:
            dummy = SimpleNamespace(
                topic=storyboard.get("topic", "FAITH STORY")[:28],
                verse_citation=storyboard.get("anchor_verse_ref", "ISAIAH 41:10"),
                thumbnail_hook=storyboard.get("thumbnail_hook", "GOD FOUGHT FOR HIM"),
                title=storyboard.get("story_title", "Faith Story")
            )
            thumbs = pda.render_pro_thumbnails(first_img, dummy, final_thumb_jpg, palette_name=palette_name)
            if not is_vert and "16x9" in thumbs and Path(thumbs["16x9"]).exists():
                Image.open(thumbs["16x9"]).convert("RGB").save(str(final_thumb_jpg), "JPEG", quality=98, subsampling=0)
        except Exception:
            shutil.copy(first_img, final_thumb_jpg)

    if progress_cb:
        progress_cb(100, "3-Minute 1080p HD Story Movie Complete!")
    if log_cb:
        log_cb(f"MOVIE READY: {final_movie_mp4.name} ({total_movie_dur:.1f}s)")

    return final_movie_mp4.exists(), final_movie_mp4, final_thumb_jpg

import os

import time

import main as backend
import flow_story_engine as fse
import sticker_studio

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
load_dotenv(BASE_DIR / ".env")

ARCHETYPE_STATE_FILE = BASE_DIR / "last_archetype.json"
AFFILIATE_CONFIG_FILE = BASE_DIR / "affiliate_links.json"

ARCHETYPES: Dict[str, str] = {
    "Auto-Rotate (Never Repeat Last Archetype)": "AUTO",
    "Archetype A: Biblical Backstory (Historical Moment)": (
        "ARCHETYPE A (Biblical Backstory): Open with the raw historical moment a biblical figure wrote the verse "
        "(e.g., David in the cave of Adullam, Paul in chains, Hezekiah weeping at the wall), explain the verse, and close with a short prayer."
    ),
    "Archetype B: Original Word Insight (Hebrew/Greek Meaning)": (
        "ARCHETYPE B (Original Word Insight): Reveal the deeper Hebrew or Greek meaning of a key word in the scripture "
        "(e.g., Shalom, Hesed, Merimnao) providing educational commentary, then apply it to the viewer's life in prayer."
    ),
    "Archetype C: 3 AM Heart-to-Heart (Empathetic Scenario)": (
        "ARCHETYPE C (3 AM Heart-to-Heart): Start with an empathetic, specific real-life emotional scenario "
        "(e.g., staring at the ceiling at 2 AM worried about tomorrow), anchor it in Scripture, and pray for peace."
    ),
    "Archetype D: Immediate First-Person Prayer (Starts at 0:00)": (
        "ARCHETYPE D (Immediate First-Person Prayer): Start at 0:00 directly inside an intimate, poetic prayer woven with Scripture, "
        "speaking in first-person ('Lord, right now my mind is racing...')."
    ),
    "Archetype E: Perspective Shift (Reframe + 1 Action Step)": (
        "ARCHETYPE E (Perspective Shift): Open with a bold spiritual truth that reframes a hardship, share the Bible verse, "
        "and give 1 practical spiritual action step for the day."
    ),
    "Archetype F: Psalm of Protection & Pastoral Blessing": (
        "ARCHETYPE F (Psalm of Protection/Blessing): Spoken as a rhythmic, comforting pastoral blessing over the viewer's family, home, and mind."
    ),
}

VOICE_TONE_MAP: Dict[str, Dict[str, str]] = {
    "warm_mentor_male": {"voice": "en-US-AndrewNeural", "label": "Andrew (Warm Mentor Male)"},
    "gentle_comfort_female": {"voice": "en-US-AvaNeural", "label": "Ava (Gentle Comfort Female)"},
    "deep_pastoral_male": {"voice": "en-US-ChristopherNeural", "label": "Christopher (Deep Pastoral Male)"},
}

BANNED_OPENINGS = [
    "dealing with", "if you are struggling with", "if you're struggling with",
    "hear god's promise", "hear gods promise", "stop scrolling"
]

BANNED_NON_CHRISTIAN_TAGS = {
    "#dua", "#islamicreminder", "#allah", "#quran", "#manifestation",
    "#lawofattraction", "#universe", "#karma", "#zodiac", "#tarot"
}

DEFAULT_AFFILIATES = [
    {"heading": "📖 Featured Study Bible (ESV):", "url": "https://amzn.to/your-study-bible-link"},
    {"heading": "🙏 Daily Prayer & Gratitude Journal:", "url": "https://amzn.to/your-prayer-journal-link"},
    {"heading": "🕊️ Christian Devotional for Peace:", "url": "https://amzn.to/your-devotional-link"},
]

def load_affiliate_links() -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    if AFFILIATE_CONFIG_FILE.exists():
        try:
            data = json.loads(AFFILIATE_CONFIG_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) == 3:
                return data
        except Exception:
            pass
    return DEFAULT_AFFILIATES

def save_affiliate_links(links: List[Dict[str, str]]):
    AFFILIATE_CONFIG_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(links, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

def pick_next_archetype(selected_key: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    keys = [k for k in ARCHETYPES.keys() if k != "Auto-Rotate (Never Repeat Last Archetype)"]
    if selected_key in ARCHETYPES and ARCHETYPES[selected_key] != "AUTO":
        ARCHETYPE_STATE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps({"last": selected_key}), encoding="utf-8")
        return selected_key, ARCHETYPES[selected_key]

    last_used = ""
    if ARCHETYPE_STATE_FILE.exists():
        try:
            last_used = json.loads(ARCHETYPE_STATE_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("last", "")
        except Exception:
            pass

    idx = (keys.index(last_used) + 1) % len(keys) if last_used in keys else 0
    chosen_k = keys[idx]
    ARCHETYPE_STATE_FILE.write_text(json.dumps({"last": chosen_k}), encoding="utf-8")
    return chosen_k, ARCHETYPES[chosen_k]

def _sanitize_opening(text: str) -> str:
    t = text.strip()
    low = t.lower()
    for banned in BANNED_OPENINGS:
        if low.startswith(banned):
            # Strip the banned opening clause up to the first period or comma
            parts = re.split(r"[.,!?]", t, maxsplit=1)
            if len(parts) > 1 and len(parts[1].strip()) > 10:
                return parts[1].strip()
    return t

def _clean_christian_hashtags(desc: str) -> str:
    # Remove any [HASHTAGS] or [/HASHTAGS] or HTML comments
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\[/?HASHTAGS\]", "", desc)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"<!--[\s\S]*?-->", "", cleaned)
    for bad_tag in BANNED_NON_CHRISTIAN_TAGS:
        cleaned = re.sub(re.escape(bad_tag), "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    return cleaned.strip()

def generate_grace_3scene_script(
    topic: str,
    archetype_choice: str = "Auto-Rotate (Never Repeat Last Archetype)",
    target_pacing: str = "Viral 18-24s (High Retention)"
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Generates a deeply authentic, non-repetitive 28-to-38s 3-Scene YouTube Short script
    following the exact 'Grace of the Lord' (@GraceAndDailyPrayer) specification.
    """
    arch_name, arch_instruction = pick_next_archetype(archetype_choice)
    aff_links = load_affiliate_links()
    aff_block = "\n\n".join([f"{item['heading']}\n{item['url']}" for item in aff_links])

    sys_prompt = """You are the Creative Director and Theological Scriptwriter for "Grace of the Lord" (@GraceAndDailyPrayer), a Christian devotional and prayer channel.
Your goal is to generate deeply authentic, non-repetitive, high-retention 28-to-38-second YouTube Shorts scripts that pass YouTube's human review for Original & Authentic Content.

STRICT ANTI-REPETITION RULES (CRITICAL FOR MONETIZATION):
1. BANNED PHRASES: NEVER start a script with "Dealing with...", "If you are struggling with...", "Hear God's promise", or "Stop scrolling".
2. ARCHETYPE TO USE FOR THIS SCRIPT:
""" + arch_instruction + """

3. COMPLIANCE & BRAND SAFETY:
- Never make literal medical cure claims or literal financial advice guarantees. Frame financial topics around "wisdom, stewardship, and freedom from money anxiety" and health topics around "spiritual comfort, strength, and peace."
- Use ONLY Christian/Biblical hashtags. NEVER include tags from other religions (no #dua, #islamicreminder, #manifestation).

4. 3-SCENE VISUAL STORYBOARD (Total spoken words across all 3 scenes: 44 to 55 words so total audio is 18 to 24 seconds (maximum Shorts retention)):
- Break the video into 3 distinct visual scenes (Scene 1: 0-10s Hook & Context, Scene 2: 10-22s Scripture & Editorial Commentary, Scene 3: 22-35s Anointed Prayer & Natural CTA) that visually progress from "The Human Struggle/Setting" -> "The Spiritual Turning Point" -> "Peace & Breakthrough".
- Do not just prompt for a static portrait of Jesus in every scene; include cinematic biblical landscapes, symbolic imagery (e.g., a warm oil lamp in a storm, olive groves at dawn, open ancient parchment scrolls), and warm biblical scenes."""

    user_prompt = f"""Topic / Life Theme: {topic}
Required Archetype: {arch_name}

Include these 3 affiliate items inside 'clean_youtube_description':
{aff_block}

Respond ONLY with a valid JSON object matching this exact schema (no markdown fences):
{{
  "archetype_used": "{arch_name}",
  "recommended_voice_tone": "deep_pastoral_male",
  "youtube_title": "Max 55 chars, high curiosity/emotion, 1 emoji and #shorts",
  "spoken_script_segments": [
    {{
      "scene_number": 1,
      "Role": "Hook & Context (0-10s)",
      "spoken_text": "13-17 words for TTS to read (short, punchy hook). Natural pauses (...) and punctuation. NO banned openings.",
      "on_screen_header": "SHORT 3-4 WORD UPPERCASE THEME",
      "image_prompt": "Detailed cinematic prompt for AI image generator, warm golden hour lighting, vertical 9:16 aspect ratio, no text in image.",
      "camera_motion": "zoom_in_slow"
    }},
    {{
      "scene_number": 2,
      "Role": "Scripture & Editorial Commentary (10-22s)",
      "spoken_text": "16-20 words for TTS reading the verse and 1-sentence core meaning.",
      "bible_verse_reference": "e.g., Psalm 34:18 (ESV)",
      "bible_verse_quote_short": "Short 1-sentence quote for on-screen display (Max 90 chars).",
      "image_prompt": "Detailed cinematic prompt showing transformation/hope, vertical 9:16, no text.",
      "camera_motion": "pan_up_slow"
    }},
    {{
      "scene_number": 3,
      "Role": "Anointed Prayer & Natural CTA (22-35s)",
      "spoken_text": "14-18 words heartfelt prayer ending in Amen + short call to action.",
      "image_prompt": "Detailed cinematic prompt of peace, sanctuary, or light breaking through clouds, vertical 9:16, no text.",
      "camera_motion": "zoom_out_slow"
    }}
  ],
  "clean_youtube_description": "Full ready-to-publish description including a 2-sentence devotional summary, the Bible verse, the 3 Amazon affiliate links with headings, the Amazon Associate disclaimer, and 8 strictly Christian hashtags at the very bottom. NO bracket tags like [HASHTAGS].",
  "pinned_comment_text": "A warm, engaging question + prayer agreement prompt to post as the pinned comment."
}}"""

    ai_json = None
    key = fse.get_active_gemini_key()
    if key:
        try:
            from google import genai
            from google.genai import types
            client = genai.Client(api_key=key)
            for m_id in ["gemini-3.8-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite", "gemini-3.7-flash", "gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-2.0-flash"]:
                try:
                    res = client.models.generate_content(
                        model=m_id,
                        contents=f"{sys_prompt}\n\n{user_prompt}",
                        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(response_mime_type="application/json", temperature=0.8)
                    )
                    if res and res.text:
                        s = res.text.find("{")
                        e = res.text.rfind("}")
                        if s != -1 and e != -1:
                            cand = json.loads(res.text[s:e+1])
                            if cand.get("spoken_script_segments") and len(cand["spoken_script_segments"]) >= 3:
                                ai_json = cand
                                break
                except Exception:
                    continue
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not ai_json:
        # Authentic Archetype Fallback so it is never repetitive even offline
        ai_json = {
            "archetype_used": arch_name,
            "recommended_voice_tone": "deep_pastoral_male",
            "youtube_title": f"When Night Feels Heavy, Remember This 🙏 #shorts",
            "spoken_script_segments": [
                {
                    "scene_number": 1,
                    "Role": "Hook & Context (0-10s)",
                    "spoken_text": f"Long before King David wore a crown, he sat alone in the cold shadows of a cave... wondering if his story was over.",
                    "on_screen_header": "LIGHT IN THE VALLEY",
                    "image_prompt": "Cinematic vertical 9:16 photograph of a flickering clay oil lamp resting on a stone table inside a quiet ancient cavern at dusk, warm golden rim light, shallow depth of field, Arri Alexa 65, no text.",
                    "camera_motion": "zoom_in_slow"
                },
                {
                    "scene_number": 2,
                    "Role": "Scripture & Editorial Commentary (10-22s)",
                    "spoken_text": "In that quiet place, he wrote Psalm 34, verse 18... The Lord is near to the brokenhearted. In Hebrew, the word 'near' means leaning in close to listen.",
                    "bible_verse_reference": "Psalm 34:18 (ESV)",
                    "bible_verse_quote_short": "The Lord is near to the brokenhearted and saves the crushed in spirit.",
                    "image_prompt": "Cinematic vertical 9:16 photograph of an ancient open Hebrew scroll beside an olive branch on weathered wood as warm morning sunbeams break through a window, golden dust motes, no text.",
                    "camera_motion": "pan_up_slow"
                },
                {
                    "scene_number": 3,
                    "Role": "Anointed Prayer & Natural CTA (22-35s)",
                    "spoken_text": "Father, wrap Your peace around every weary mind tonight and renew their strength for tomorrow. In Jesus' name, Amen. Share a prayer request below so we can pray with you.",
                    "image_prompt": "Cinematic vertical 9:16 photograph of ancient olive groves on a peaceful hillside overlooking a calm sea at sunrise, volumetric golden light rays parting soft clouds, no text.",
                    "camera_motion": "zoom_out_slow"
                }
            ],
            "clean_youtube_description": (
                f"Even in the quietest, heaviest seasons of life, God is leaning in close to hear your heart. "
                f"May this reflection on Psalm 34:18 bring deep spiritual comfort and unshakeable peace to your home tonight.\n\n"
                f"📖 Scripture: Psalm 34:18 (ESV)\n"
                f"\"The Lord is near to the brokenhearted and saves the crushed in spirit.\"\n\n"
                f"{aff_block}\n\n"
                f"Disclaimer: As an Amazon Associate, I earn from qualifying purchases at no extra cost to you.\n\n"
                f"#ChristianShorts #DailyPrayer #BibleVerse #Psalm34 #JesusChrist #FaithOverFear #GodsPeace #BiblicalWisdom"
            ),
            "pinned_comment_text": "How can our community pray for you or your family this week? Leave a word below and let's stand in faith together! 🙏"
        }

    # Sanitize openings & description
    segs = ai_json.get("spoken_script_segments", [])
    if segs:
        segs[0]["spoken_text"] = _sanitize_opening(segs[0].get("spoken_text", ""))
        for s_item in segs:
            if "bible_verse_quote_short" in s_item and s_item["bible_verse_quote_short"]:
                s_item["bible_verse_quote_short"] = s_item["bible_verse_quote_short"][:90]

    ai_json["clean_youtube_description"] = _clean_christian_hashtags(ai_json.get("clean_youtube_description", ""))
    return ai_json

def _sec_to_ass(sec: float) -> str:
    sec = max(0.0, float(sec))
    h = int(sec // 3600)
    m = int((sec % 3600) // 60)
    s = sec % 60
    return f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:05.2f}"

def render_grace_3scene_short(
    grace_pkg: Dict[str, Any],
    voice_id: str = "en-US-ChristopherNeural",
    voice_rate: str = "-4%",
    lyrics_words_per_card: int = 4,
    bgm_file: Path | None = None,
    bgm_vol: float = 0.18,
    palette_name: str = "Gold Classic",
    sticker_path: Path | None = None,
    sticker_position: str = "Bottom Dock (Centered)",
    sticker_timing: str = "Throughout Entire Video",
    progress_cb=None
) -> Tuple[bool, Path, Path]:
    """
    1. Synthesizes each of the 3 scenes' spoken_text and measures exact scene transition timestamps.
    2. Generates the 3 distinct 9:16 HD scene images in parallel from their image_prompts.
    3. Applies Scene 1 (zoom_in_slow), Scene 2 (pan_up_slow), and Scene 3 (zoom_out_slow),
       overlays the on_screen_header, bible_verse_reference, short verse quote, calm 2-line ASS lyrics,
       and renders the 28-to-38s Short + 3D Gold Thumbnail + clean metadata + pinned_comment.txt!
    """
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import pro_design_addon as pda

    segs = grace_pkg["spoken_script_segments"]
    s1, s2, s3 = segs[0], segs[1], segs[2]

    header_str = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9\s&\-]", "", s1.get("on_screen_header", "GRACE OF THE LORD")).upper().strip()[:28]
    verse_ref = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9\s:\-()]", "", s2.get("bible_verse_reference", "Psalm 34:18")).strip()[:28]
    verse_short = s2.get("bible_verse_quote_short", "")[:90]

    clean_label = backend.clean_filename(f"Grace_{header_str}_{verse_ref}")
    seq_dir = backend.get_next_sequence_folder(clean_label)
    scenes_dir = seq_dir / "grace_scenes"
    scenes_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    out_video = seq_dir / f"{clean_label}.mp4"
    out_thumb = seq_dir / f"{clean_label}_thumb.jpg"

    if progress_cb:
        progress_cb(15, "🎨 Generating 3 Distinct 9:16 HD Scene Images in Parallel (Struggle ➔ Turning Point ➔ Peace)...")

    img_paths = [scenes_dir / f"scene_{i}.jpg" for i in range(1, 4)]

    def _gen_img(idx_zero: int):
        p_txt = segs[idx_zero].get("image_prompt", "Cinematic biblical golden hour landscape, vertical 9:16")
        fse.generate_scene_image(p_txt, img_paths[idx_zero], aspect_ratio="9:16 Vertical", seed=700 + idx_zero)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:
        list(pool.map(_gen_img, [0, 1, 2]))

    if progress_cb:
        progress_cb(45, f"🎙️ Synthesizing 3-Scene Anointed Voiceover ({voice_rate} speed)...")

    # Synthesize each scene's audio so we know exact scene durations, and capture word timings
    scene_mp3s = [scenes_dir / f"sc_{i}.mp3" for i in range(1, 4)]
    sil_mp3 = scenes_dir / "pause_0_5s.mp3"
    subprocess.run([
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
        "-t", "0.55", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-q:a", "2", str(sil_mp3.absolute())
    ], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    all_word_timings = []
    scene_durs = []
    time_offset = 0.0

    for idx_s, seg in enumerate(segs):
        raw_t = seg.get("spoken_text", "").strip()
        clean_tts = re.sub(r"(\d+):(\d+)", r"chapter \1 verse \2", raw_t)
        target_mp3 = scene_mp3s[idx_s]

        async def _make_sc(txt_in: str, mp3_out: Path, t_off: float):
            comm = edge_tts.Communicate(txt_in, voice=voice_id, rate=voice_rate, boundary="WordBoundary")
            with open(mp3_out, "wb") as f_v:
                async for ch in comm.stream():
                    if ch["type"] == "audio":
                        f_v.write(ch["data"])
                    elif ch["type"] in ("WordBoundary", "SentenceBoundary"):
                        st_s = t_off + (ch["offset"] / 10_000_000.0)
                        du_s = ch["duration"] / 10_000_000.0
                        w_txt = ch["text"].strip()
                        if len(w_txt.split()) <= 1:
                            all_word_timings.append({"text": w_txt, "start": round(st_s, 3), "end": round(st_s + du_s, 3)})
                        else:
                            w_list = w_txt.split()
                            step_d = max(0.1, du_s) / len(w_list)
                            for w_i, w_tok in enumerate(w_list):
                                all_word_timings.append({
                                    "text": w_tok,
                                    "start": round(st_s + w_i * step_d, 3),
                                    "end": round(st_s + (w_i + 1) * step_d, 3)
                                })

        try:
            asyncio.run(_make_sc(clean_tts, target_mp3, time_offset))
        except RuntimeError:
            loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
            loop.run_until_complete(_make_sc(clean_tts, target_mp3, time_offset))
            loop.close()

        sc_d = max(3.0, backend.get_video_duration(target_mp3))
        scene_durs.append(sc_d + 0.55)
        time_offset += sc_d + 0.55

    # Concatenate the 3 scene voiceovers with 0.55s natural pauses
    aud_concat_txt = scenes_dir / "voice_concat.txt"
    aud_lines = []
    for smp3 in scene_mp3s:
        aud_lines.append(f"file '{smp3.absolute().as_posix()}'")
        aud_lines.append(f"file '{sil_mp3.absolute().as_posix()}'")
    aud_concat_txt.write_text("\n".join(aud_lines), encoding="utf-8")

    full_voice_mp3 = scenes_dir / "full_voice.mp3"
    subprocess.run([
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", str(aud_concat_txt.absolute()),
        "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", str(full_voice_mp3.absolute())
    ], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    total_dur = max(12.0, sum(scene_durs))
    d1, d2, d3 = scene_durs[0], scene_durs[1], scene_durs[2]

    if progress_cb:
        progress_cb(70, f"🎬 Rendering 3-Scene Storyboard ({total_dur:.1f}s: zoom_in_slow ➔ pan_up_slow ➔ zoom_out_slow)...")

    # Build calm 2-line ASS subtitles
    max_w_card = max(1, int(lyrics_words_per_card))
    max_c_card = max_w_card * 7 + 4
    phrases = []
    cur_w = []
    c_st = 0.0
    for i, w_obj in enumerate(all_word_timings):
        if not cur_w:
            c_st = w_obj["start"]
        cur_w.append(w_obj["text"])
        t_str = " ".join(cur_w)
        nxt_len = len(t_str) + (len(all_word_timings[i + 1]["text"]) + 1 if i + 1 < len(all_word_timings) else 0)
        is_punct = bool(re.search(r"[.;!?]", w_obj["text"])) or (len(cur_w) >= 3 and "," in w_obj["text"])
        if nxt_len > max_c_card or len(cur_w) >= max_w_card or is_punct or i == len(all_word_timings) - 1:
            c_en = all_word_timings[i + 1]["start"] if i + 1 < len(all_word_timings) else (w_obj["end"] + 0.2)
            safe_t = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9\s:\-',.!?]", "", t_str).strip()
            if safe_t:
                phrases.append({"text": safe_t, "start": round(c_st, 3), "end": round(c_en, 3)})
            cur_w = []

    ass_path = BASE_DIR / "temp_grace_subs.ass"
    ass_lines = [
        "[Script Info]", "ScriptType: v4.00+", "PlayResX: 1080", "PlayResY: 1920", "",
        "[V4+ Styles]",
        "Format: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding",
        "Style: SubPop,Nirmala UI,90,&H20FFFFFF,&H0000FFFF,&H90000000,&H90000000,-1,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,5,4,5,70,70,0,1",
        "", "[Events]", "Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text"
    ]
    for p in phrases:
        t_s = p["text"]
        w_p = t_s.split()
        if len(t_s) > 16 and len(w_p) >= 3:
            m_w = len(w_p) // 2
            l1, l2 = " ".join(w_p[:m_w]), " ".join(w_p[m_w:])
            f_sz = 86 if max(len(l1), len(l2)) <= 13 else max(60, int(960 / (max(len(l1), len(l2)) * 0.58)))
            disp = f"{l1}\\N{l2}"
        else:
            f_sz = 92 if len(t_s) <= 11 else max(62, int(960 / (len(t_s) * 0.58)))
            disp = t_s
        ass_lines.append(f"Dialogue: 0,{_sec_to_ass(p['start'])},{_sec_to_ass(max(p['start']+0.12, p['end']))},SubPop,,0,0,0,,{{\\fs{f_sz}}}{disp}")

    ass_path.write_text("\n".join(ass_lines), encoding="utf-8")

    # Build top header + Scene 2 Verse Quote Overlay PNG with Pillow (zero drawtext escaping bugs!)
    hdr_png = scenes_dir / "header_overlay.png"
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
    ov = Image.new("RGBA", (1080, 1920), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    dr = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)
    for y in range(340):
        dr.line([(0, y), (1080, y)], fill=(5, 8, 16, int(210 * (1.0 - y / 340.0))))
    for y in range(1580, 1920):
        dr.line([(0, y), (1080, y)], fill=(5, 8, 16, int(220 * ((y - 1580) / 340.0))))

    f_hdr = pda.get_black_font(38)
    f_ref = pda.get_black_font(44)
    f_ch = pda.get_bold_font(26)

    # Channel Brand Pill
    ch_txt = "GRACE OF THE LORD"
    cb = dr.textbbox((0, 0), ch_txt, font=f_ch)
    dr.text(((1080 - (cb[2] - cb[0])) // 2, 130), ch_txt, font=f_ch, fill=(235, 185, 55, 230), stroke_width=2, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 240))

    # Theme Header
    hb = dr.textbbox((0, 0), header_str, font=f_hdr)
    dr.text(((1080 - (hb[2] - hb[0])) // 2, 175), header_str, font=f_hdr, fill=(255, 245, 185, 255), stroke_width=4, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 250))

    # Verse Reference
    rb = dr.textbbox((0, 0), verse_ref.upper(), font=f_ref)
    dr.text(((1080 - (rb[2] - rb[0])) // 2, 232), verse_ref.upper(), font=f_ref, fill=(255, 255, 255, 255), stroke_width=4, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 250))

    ov.save(str(hdr_png), "PNG")

    bgm_audio = bgm_file if (bgm_file and Path(bgm_file).exists()) else backend.get_worship_bgm()

    # 3-Scene Distinct Camera Motions:
    # Scene 1: zoom_in_slow
    # Scene 2: pan_up_slow
    # Scene 3: zoom_out_slow
    filter_complex = (
        f"[0:v]scale=1260:2240:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase:flags=lanczos,"
        f"crop=1080:1920:'(in_w-out_w)/2':'(in_h-out_h)*(0.5-0.35*t/{d1:.2f})',fps=24,format=yuv420p,setsar=1,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[v1];"
        f"[1:v]scale=1260:2240:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase:flags=lanczos,"
        f"crop=1080:1920:'(in_w-out_w)/2':'(in_h-out_h)*(0.85-0.70*t/{d2:.2f})',fps=24,format=yuv420p,setsar=1,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[v2];"
        f"[2:v]scale=1260:2240:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase:flags=lanczos,"
        f"crop=1080:1920:'(in_w-out_w)*(0.2+0.6*t/{d3:.2f})':'(in_h-out_h)/2',fps=24,format=yuv420p,setsar=1,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[v3];"
        f"[v1][v2][v3]concat=n=3:v=1:a=0,unsharp=5:5:0.85:5:5:0.0,eq=brightness=-0.05:contrast=1.12:saturation=1.10[bg];"
        f"[bg][3:v]overlay=0:0:format=auto,subtitles='temp_grace_subs.ass'[vout];"
        f"[4:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume=1.0[voice];"
        f"[5:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume={bgm_vol:.2f}[bgm];"
        f"[voice][bgm]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:normalize=0[aout]"
    )

    inputs = [
        "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{d1:.2f}", "-i", str(img_paths[0].absolute()),
        "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{d2:.2f}", "-i", str(img_paths[1].absolute()),
        "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{d3:.2f}", "-i", str(img_paths[2].absolute()),
        "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(hdr_png.absolute()),
        "-i", str(full_voice_mp3.absolute()),
        "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(Path(bgm_audio).absolute())
    ]

    stk_inputs, stk_filter = sticker_studio.build_ffmpeg_sticker_filter(
        sticker_path, total_dur, position=sticker_position, timing=sticker_timing
    )
    if stk_inputs:
        inputs.extend(stk_inputs)
        filter_complex = filter_complex.replace("[vout];", f"[vout];[6:v]{stk_filter};")
        final_v_map = "[vout_stk]"
    else:
        final_v_map = "[vout]"

    cmd = [
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-threads", "0",
        *inputs,
        "-filter_complex", filter_complex,
        "-map", final_v_map, "-map", "[aout]",
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "18", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k",
        "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}",
        str(out_video.absolute())
    ]

    subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(BASE_DIR), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    if ass_path.exists():
        ass_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)

    # Render 3D Gold Thumbnail + 3 Variants using Scene 2 (Turning Point) or Scene 3 image
    thumb_bg = img_paths[2] if img_paths[2].exists() else img_paths[0]
    dummy_script = SimpleNamespace(
        topic=header_str,
        verse_citation=re.sub(r"\s*\(.*?\)", "", verse_ref),
        thumbnail_hook=header_str,
        title=grace_pkg.get("youtube_title", header_str)
    )
    pda.render_pro_thumbnails(thumb_bg, dummy_script, out_thumb, palette_name=palette_name)

    # Save metadata, clean description & pinned_comment.txt
    win_title = grace_pkg.get("youtube_title", f"{header_str.title()} 🙏 #shorts")[:95]
    clean_desc = _clean_christian_hashtags(grace_pkg.get("clean_youtube_description", ""))
    pinned_txt = grace_pkg.get("pinned_comment_text", "How can we pray for you today? Comment AMEN below! 🙏")
    (seq_dir / "pinned_comment.txt").write_text(pinned_txt, encoding="utf-8")
    (seq_dir / "grace_script_spec.json").write_text(json.dumps(grace_pkg, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    tags = backend.sanitize_youtube_tags([
        header_str.title(), re.sub(r"\s*\(.*?\)", "", verse_ref),
        "Grace Of The Lord", "Christian Shorts", "Daily Prayer", "Bible Verse",
        "Jesus Christ", "Faith Over Fear", "Spiritual Comfort", "Biblical Wisdom"
    ])
    backend.save_clip_metadata(out_video, win_title, clean_desc, tags, topic=header_str)

    if progress_cb:
        progress_cb(100, f"🎉 3-Scene 'Grace of the Lord' Short Ready ({total_dur:.1f}s)!")

    return out_video.exists(), out_video, out_thumb

def post_pinned_comment_if_possible(channel_name: str, video_id: str, video_path: Path):
    """Posts the saved pinned_comment.txt as the top comment on the uploaded YouTube Short."""
    pin_f = video_path.parent / "pinned_comment.txt"
    if not pin_f.exists() or not video_id:
        return False
    comment_txt = pin_f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if not comment_txt:
        return False
    try:
        yt = backend.get_authenticated_youtube(channel_name)
        if not yt:
            return False
        body = {
            "snippet": {
                "videoId": video_id,
                "topLevelComment": {
                    "snippet": {
                        "textOriginal": comment_txt
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        yt.commentThreads().insert(part="snippet", body=body).execute()
        print(f"   💬 Posted top prayer comment on video {video_id}!")
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"   [Comment Note] {e}")
        return False

=== FILE: longform_prayer_engine.py ===

def clean_ascii(text: str) -> str:
    if not text:
        return ""
    t = text.replace("’", "'").replace("‘", "'").replace("“", '"').replace("”", '"').replace("—", " - ").replace("…", "...")
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", re.sub(r"[^\x20-\x7E]", "", t)).strip()


def _sec_to_srt_time(sec: float) -> str:
    """Converts seconds into standard SRT timestamp format: HH:MM:SS,mmm"""
    sec = max(0.0, float(sec))
    h = int(sec // 3600)
    m = int((sec % 3600) // 60)
    s = int(sec % 60)
    ms = int(round((sec - int(sec)) * 1000))
    if ms >= 1000:
        s += 1
        ms -= 1000
    return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d},{ms:03d}"



def detect_hardware_video_encoder() -> tuple:

    """Auto-detects NVIDIA NVENC, Intel QSV, or AMD AMF for 8x faster rendering."""

    # 1. Test NVIDIA NVENC

    try:

        t = subprocess.run(

            ["ffmpeg", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=s=64x64:d=0.1", "-c:v", "h264_nvenc", "-f", "null", "-"],

            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=3

        )

        if t.returncode == 0:

            return "h264_nvenc", ["-preset", "p1", "-cq", "24"]

    except Exception:

        pass



    # 2. Test Intel QuickSync (QSV)

    try:

        t = subprocess.run(

            ["ffmpeg", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=s=64x64:d=0.1", "-c:v", "h264_qsv", "-f", "null", "-"],

            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=3

        )

        if t.returncode == 0:

            return "h264_qsv", ["-preset", "veryfast", "-global_quality", "25"]

    except Exception:

        pass



    # 3. CPU Fallback (Tuned for maximum throughput)

    return "libx264", ["-preset", "ultrafast", "-tune", "fastdecode", "-crf", "22"]

def select_scriptures_for_journey(topic: str, count: int) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:

    t_low = topic.lower()

    chosen_pool = TOPIC_SCRIPTURE_JOURNEYS["anxiety"]

    for k, pool in TOPIC_SCRIPTURE_JOURNEYS.items():

        if k in t_low:

            chosen_pool = pool

            break



    results = []

    for ref in chosen_pool[:count]:

        norm_ref, en_txt, _, _ = bdb.lookup_reference_bilingual(ref)

        spoken_ref = bdb.format_reference_for_speech(norm_ref, is_hindi_speech=False)

        results.append({

            "ref": norm_ref,

            "spoken_ref": spoken_ref,

            "text": en_txt

        })

    return results

# 2. 5-STAGE SOUL-PRAYER AI & CURATED SCRIPT GENERATOR
