def _call_gemini_stage(prompt: str) -> str:

    key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")

    if not key:

        return ""

    try:

        from google import genai

        from google.genai import types

        client = genai.Client(api_key=key)

        for m_id in ["gemini-3.7-flash", "gemini-3.6-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"]:

            try:

                res = client.models.generate_content(

                    model=m_id,

                    contents=prompt,

                    config=types.GenerateContentConfig(temperature=0.75)

                )

                if res and res.text and len(res.text.strip()) > 60:

                    cleaned = re.sub(r"[*#_`~>]", "", res.text).strip()

                    return cleaned

            except Exception:

                continue

    except Exception:

        pass

    return ""



def generate_soul_prayer_journey(topic: str, duration_label: str = "10 Minutes") -> Dict[str, Any]:

    cfg = DURATION_CONFIGS.get(duration_label, DURATION_CONFIGS["10 Minutes"])

    target_w = cfg["words_per_stage"]

    scriptures = select_scriptures_for_journey(topic, cfg["scriptures_count"])



    s_stage1 = scriptures[0:1]

    s_stage2 = scriptures[1:max(2, len(scriptures)//2)]

    s_stage3 = scriptures[max(2, len(scriptures)//2):max(3, (len(scriptures)*3)//4)]

    s_stage4 = scriptures[max(3, (len(scriptures)*3)//4):]

    if not s_stage4 and scriptures:

        s_stage4 = [scriptures[-1]]



    stages_spec = [

        {

            "stage_num": 1,

            "chapter_title": f"Entering God's Sanctuary & Releasing {topic}",

            "verses": s_stage1,

            "role": (

                f"Stage 1: The Sanctuary Call. Gently welcome the listener who is exhausted and burdened by {topic}. "

                f"Invite them to take a slow deep breath, drop their shoulders, and enter God's quiet presence. "

                f"Include the exact words of {s_stage1[0]['spoken_ref']}: '{s_stage1[0]['text']}'. "

                f"Explain softly how God sees every hidden tear and silent battle."

            ),

            "fallback": (

                f"Take a slow, deep breath right now. Let your shoulders drop, and quiet your heart before the Lord. "

                f"If you have come to this moment carrying the heavy weight of {topic}, know that you are not here by accident. "

                f"God sees the silent battles you have been fighting when no one else is looking. He hears the prayers you whisper in the dark. "

                f"Listen to the comforting promise of God's Word in {s_stage1[0]['spoken_ref']}. "

                f"{s_stage1[0]['text']} "

                f"Right now, you do not have to pretend to be strong. You do not have to figure everything out on your own. "

                f"Come into His sanctuary and lay every anxious thought at the feet of Jesus."

            )

        },

        {

            "stage_num": 2,

            "chapter_title": "The Unshakeable Foundation of God's Word",

            "verses": s_stage2,

            "role": (

                f"Stage 2: Deep Scripture Reflection & Teaching on {topic}. "

                f"Read and weave in these exact scriptures: " +

                "; ".join([f"{v['spoken_ref']}: '{v['text']}'" for v in s_stage2]) + ". "

                f"Give a deep, soul-stirring pastoral explanation of what these verses mean for someone struggling with {topic}."

            ),

            "fallback": (

                "Let the eternal truth of Scripture wash over your mind and spirit. " +

                " ".join([

                    f"In {v['spoken_ref']}, the Word of the Lord declares: {v['text']} "

                    f"Pause and let that promise sink deep into your soul. When human strength runs dry, God's covenant remains unshaken. "

                    for v in s_stage2

                ]) +

                f"Every circumstance connected to {topic} is subject to the authority of Jesus Christ. "

                f"He is not distant from your pain; He is walking right beside you through the fire, turning your testing into a testimony."

            )

        },

        {

            "stage_num": 3,

            "chapter_title": f"Deep Soul Surrender & Prayer for {topic}",

            "verses": s_stage3,

            "role": (

                f"Stage 3: Praying from the Soul. Pray a slow, deeply emotional, fervent prayer directly to God on behalf of the listener regarding {topic}. "

                f"Include the scripture: " + "; ".join([f"{v['spoken_ref']}: '{v['text']}'" for v in s_stage3]) + ". "

                f"Pray against fear, heaviness, and delay, and ask the Holy Spirit to fill their room right now."

            ),

            "fallback": (

                f"Heavenly Father, we come before Your throne of grace right now in the mighty name of Jesus. "

                f"Lord, You see your child who is listening to my voice. You know the exact weight of {topic} that has been pressing upon their heart. " +

                " ".join([f"Your Word promises in {v['spoken_ref']}: {v['text']} " for v in s_stage3]) +

                f"Right now, Lord, we surrender every fear, every disappointment, and every heavy burden into Your nail-scarred hands. "

                f"Where there has been anxiety, release Your supernatural peace that surpasses all understanding. "

                f"Where doors have been closed, open pathways of divine favor and miraculous breakthrough. "

                f"We rebuke every spirit of heaviness, doubt, and discouragement in Jesus' name, and we invite the Holy Spirit to fill this room with light and restoration."

            )

        },

        {

            "stage_num": 4,

            "chapter_title": "Spoken Covenant Promises & Biblical Affirmations",

            "verses": s_stage4,

            "role": (

                f"Stage 4: Covenant Promises & Prophetic Biblical Affirmations over the listener's life and family regarding {topic}. "

                f"Weave in: " + "; ".join([f"{v['spoken_ref']}: '{v['text']}'" for v in s_stage4]) + ". "

                f"Speak peace, provision, healing, and victory directly over their home, mind, and future."

            ),

            "fallback": (

                "Receive these covenant promises over your life, your home, and your family today. " +

                " ".join([

                    f"Hold fast to {v['spoken_ref']}: {v['text']} "

                    for v in s_stage4

                ]) +

                f"I speak the peace of Christ over your mind and your sleep tonight. "

                f"You will not be overwhelmed by {topic}. You will not be defeated by this season. "

                f"The Lord is going before you to make every crooked place straight. "

                f"His favor surrounds you like a shield, and His goodness and mercy shall follow you all the days of your life."

            )

        },

        {

            "stage_num": 5,

            "chapter_title": "Peaceful Benediction & Resting in His Presence",

            "verses": s_stage1,

            "role": (

                f"Stage 5: Final Benediction & Resting in God's Peace. "

                f"Bring the prayer to a gentle, deeply comforting close. Seal every request in the name of Jesus. "

                f"Encourage the listener to rest, breathe, and trust that God is working while they rest. End with 'In Jesus' mighty name, Amen.'"

            ),

            "fallback": (

                f"Now, rest in the assurance that your prayer has been heard in heaven. "

                f"You do not need to carry the worry of {topic} any longer, because it is safely in the Father's hands. "

                f"May the Lord bless you and keep you. May the Lord make His face shine upon you and be gracious to you. "

                f"May the Lord lift up His countenance upon you, protect your loved ones, and give you deep, unshakeable peace tonight and always. "

                f"In Jesus' mighty and precious name we pray, Amen."

            )

        }

    ]



    compiled_stages = []

    for stg in stages_spec:

        prompt = (

            f"You are a deeply compassionate, spirit-filled Christian pastor leading a slow, meditative prayer video on '{topic}'. "

            f"Write {stg['role']} "

            f"Target length: approximately {target_w} words. "

            f"Tone: Unhurried, deeply emotional, praying from the soul, comforting, reverent. "

            f"Formatting: Write ONLY the spoken words in clear paragraphs separated by blank lines. "

            f"Do NOT include stage titles, narrator directions, [brackets], or markdown symbols."

        )

        ai_text = _call_gemini_stage(prompt)

        spoken_text = ai_text if len(ai_text.split()) >= 50 else stg["fallback"]



        paras = [p.strip() for p in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", spoken_text) if p.strip()]

        if len(paras) == 1 and len(spoken_text) > 350:

            sents = re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", spoken_text)

            paras = [" ".join(sents[i:i+3]) for i in range(0, len(sents), 3)]



        compiled_stages.append({

            "stage_num": stg["stage_num"],

            "chapter_title": stg["chapter_title"],

            "verses": stg["verses"],

            "paragraphs": paras,

            "full_text": "\n\n".join(paras)

        })



    return {

        "topic": topic,

        "duration_label": duration_label,

        "config": cfg,

        "scriptures": scriptures,

        "stages": compiled_stages

    }



# ==============================================================================

# 3. MEDITATIVE VOICEOVER SYNTHESIZER WITH REFLECTIVE SILENCE & TIMESTAMPS

# ==============================================================================

def get_audio_duration(audio_path: Path) -> float:

    try:

        cmd = [

            "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration",

            "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1", str(audio_path.absolute())

        ]

        res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

        return float(res.stdout.strip())

    except Exception:

        return 0.0



def _create_silence_mp3(out_path: Path, seconds: float):

    cmd = [

        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",

        "-t", f"{seconds:.2f}", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-q:a", "4", str(out_path.absolute())

    ]

    subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)



def format_mm_ss(seconds: float) -> str:

    sec = int(max(0, seconds))

    h, rem = divmod(sec, 3600)

    m, s = divmod(rem, 60)

    if h > 0:

        return f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"

    return f"{m:02d}:{s:02d}"



def _sec_to_ass_time_lf(sec: float) -> str:

    sec = max(0.0, float(sec))

    h = int(sec // 3600)

    m = int((sec % 3600) // 60)

    s = sec % 60

    return f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:05.2f}"



def synthesize_meditative_voiceover(

    journey_pkg: Dict[str, Any],

    out_voice_mp3: Path,

    voice_name: str = "en-US-ChristopherNeural",

    rate_str: str = "-10%",

    pitch_str: str = "-2Hz"

) -> Dict[str, Any]:

    """

    Synthesizes pastoral voiceover, tracks millisecond word timings,

    inserts reflective pauses, and builds a synchronized longform_subtitles.ass file.

    """

    work_dir = out_voice_mp3.parent / "temp_meditative_chunks"

    work_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)



    cfg = journey_pkg["config"]

    para_pause = float(cfg.get("pause_between_paras_sec", 2.5))

    stage_pause = float(cfg.get("pause_between_stages_sec", 4.0))



    sil_para_mp3 = work_dir / "sil_para.mp3"

    sil_stage_mp3 = work_dir / "sil_stage.mp3"

    _create_silence_mp3(sil_para_mp3, para_pause)

    _create_silence_mp3(sil_stage_mp3, stage_pause)



    concat_files: List[Path] = []

    chapters_log: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    all_word_timings: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    current_time_sec = 0.0



    async def _tts_chunk_stream(txt: str, target_mp3: Path, t_offset: float):

        clean_txt = re.sub(r"(\d+):(\d+)", r"chapter \1 verse \2", txt)

        comm = edge_tts.Communicate(clean_txt, voice=voice_name, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str, boundary="WordBoundary")

        with open(target_mp3, "wb") as f_out:

            async for chunk in comm.stream():

                if chunk["type"] == "audio":

                    f_out.write(chunk["data"])

                elif chunk["type"] in ("WordBoundary", "SentenceBoundary"):

                    st_s = t_offset + (chunk["offset"] / 10_000_000.0)

                    du_s = chunk["duration"] / 10_000_000.0

                    w_txt = chunk["text"].strip()

                    if len(w_txt.split()) <= 1:

                        all_word_timings.append({"text": w_txt, "start": round(st_s, 3), "end": round(st_s + du_s, 3)})

                    else:

                        w_list = w_txt.split()

                        step_d = max(0.1, du_s) / len(w_list)

                        for w_i, w_tok in enumerate(w_list):

                            all_word_timings.append({

                                "text": w_tok,

                                "start": round(st_s + w_i * step_d, 3),

                                "end": round(st_s + (w_i + 1) * step_d, 3)

                            })



    for stg in journey_pkg["stages"]:

        stg_idx = stg["stage_num"]

        chapters_log.append({

            "timestamp": format_mm_ss(current_time_sec),

            "start_sec": round(current_time_sec, 2),

            "title": stg["chapter_title"],

            "verses": [v["ref"] for v in stg["verses"]]

        })



        for p_idx, para_text in enumerate(stg["paragraphs"]):

            chunk_mp3 = work_dir / f"s{stg_idx}_p{p_idx}.mp3"

            try:

                asyncio.run(_tts_chunk_stream(para_text, chunk_mp3, current_time_sec))

            except RuntimeError:

                loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()

                loop.run_until_complete(_tts_chunk_stream(para_text, chunk_mp3, current_time_sec))

                loop.close()



            if chunk_mp3.exists() and chunk_mp3.stat().st_size > 500:

                dur = get_audio_duration(chunk_mp3)

                concat_files.append(chunk_mp3)

                current_time_sec += dur

                concat_files.append(sil_para_mp3)

                current_time_sec += para_pause



        concat_files.append(sil_stage_mp3)

        current_time_sec += stage_pause



    # Concat raw voice pieces

    list_txt = work_dir / "concat_list.txt"

    lines = [f"file '{f.absolute().as_posix()}'" for f in concat_files]

    list_txt.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")



    cmd_merge = [

        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0",

        "-i", str(list_txt.absolute()),

        "-ar", "44100", "-ac", "2", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k",

        str(out_voice_mp3.absolute())

    ]

    subprocess.run(cmd_merge, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    total_voice_dur = get_audio_duration(out_voice_mp3)



    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    # BUILD SYNCHRONIZED 16:9 CALM SUBTITLE PHRASES (.ASS)

    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------

    phrases = []

    cur_w = []

    c_st = 0.0

    for i, w_obj in enumerate(all_word_timings):

        if not cur_w:

            c_st = w_obj["start"]

        cur_w.append(w_obj["text"])

        t_str = " ".join(cur_w)

        nxt_len = len(t_str) + (len(all_word_timings[i + 1]["text"]) + 1 if i + 1 < len(all_word_timings) else 0)

        is_strong_punct = bool(re.search(r"[.;!?]", w_obj["text"])) or (len(cur_w) >= 4 and "," in w_obj["text"])

        

        # 5 to 7 words per card (ideal for slow horizontal 16:9 video)

        if nxt_len > 44 or len(cur_w) >= 6 or is_strong_punct or i == len(all_word_timings) - 1:

            c_en = all_word_timings[i + 1]["start"] if i + 1 < len(all_word_timings) else (w_obj["end"] + 0.25)

            safe_t = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9\s:\-',.!?]", "", t_str).strip()

            if safe_t:

                phrases.append({"text": safe_t, "start": round(c_st, 3), "end": round(c_en, 3)})

            cur_w = []



    ass_path = out_voice_mp3.parent / "longform_subtitles.ass"

    ass_lines = [

        "[Script Info]", "ScriptType: v4.00+", "PlayResX: 1920", "PlayResY: 1080", "",

        "[V4+ Styles]",

        "Format: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding",

        "Style: LongFormSubs,Segoe UI,54,&H00FFFFFF,&H0000D4FF,&H90000000,&H90000000,-1,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,1,3.5,2,2,140,140,82,1",

        "", "[Events]", "Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text"

    ]

    for p in phrases:

        t_s = p["text"]

        w_p = t_s.split()

        if len(t_s) > 28 and len(w_p) >= 4:

            mid_w = len(w_p) // 2

            l1, l2 = " ".join(w_p[:mid_w]), " ".join(w_p[mid_w:])

            disp = f"{l1}\\N{l2}"

        else:

            disp = t_s

        st_t = _sec_to_ass_time_lf(p["start"])

        en_t = _sec_to_ass_time_lf(max(p["start"] + 0.15, p["end"]))

        ass_lines.append(f"Dialogue: 0,{st_t},{en_t},LongFormSubs,,0,0,0,,{{\\fs54}}{disp}")



    ass_path.write_text("\n".join(ass_lines), encoding="utf-8")
    srt_path = out_voice_mp3.parent / "longform_subtitles.srt"
    srt_blocks = []
    for idx_s, p in enumerate(phrases, start=1):
        st_srt = _sec_to_srt_time(p["start"])
        en_srt = _sec_to_srt_time(max(p["start"] + 0.15, p["end"]))
        srt_blocks.append(f"{idx_s}\n{st_srt} --> {en_srt}\n{p['text']}\n")
    srt_path.write_text("\n".join(srt_blocks), encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"   [Subtitles] Generated {len(phrases)} synchronized subtitle cards -> {ass_path.name}")



    return {

        "voice_mp3": out_voice_mp3,

        "voice_duration_sec": round(total_voice_dur, 2),

        "chapters": chapters_log,

        "subtitles_ass": ass_path

    }



# ==============================================================================

# 4. STEP 2: WORSHIP SOAKING MUSIC & NATURE AMBIENCE MIXER (EXACT DURATION)

# ==============================================================================

def ensure_soaking_worship_pad() -> Path:

    """Generates a warm 432Hz Cathedral Soaking Pad if no custom worship MP3 is chosen."""

    pad_file = AMBIENCE_DIR / "432hz_cathedral_soaking_pad.mp3"

    if not pad_file.exists() or pad_file.stat().st_size < 10000:

        cmd = [

            "ffmpeg", "-y",

            "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=144:sample_rate=44100",

            "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=216:sample_rate=44100",

            "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=288:sample_rate=44100",

            "-filter_complex",

            "amix=inputs=3:normalize=0,lowpass=f=380,aecho=0.8:0.88:60:0.4,volume=0.22",

            "-t", "180", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", str(pad_file.absolute())

        ]

        subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    return pad_file



def ensure_nature_ambience(style: str = "Gentle Night Rain") -> Path | None:

    """Synthesizes soothing rain or mountain stream audio for deep prayer/sleep."""

    if not style or "None" in style:

        return None

    if "Stream" in style:

        out_f = AMBIENCE_DIR / "gentle_mountain_stream.mp3"

        filter_str = "anoisesrc=d=120:c=brown:r=44100:a=0.12,bandpass=f=450:width_type=h:w=350,volume=0.35"

    else:

        out_f = AMBIENCE_DIR / "gentle_night_rain.mp3"

        filter_str = "anoisesrc=d=120:c=pink:r=44100:a=0.10,lowpass=f=1200,highpass=f=200,volume=0.30"



    if not out_f.exists() or out_f.stat().st_size < 10000:

        cmd = [

            "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", filter_str,

            "-t", "120", "-ac", "2", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", str(out_f.absolute())

        ]

        subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    return out_f



def mix_longform_master_audio(

    voice_mp3: Path,

    out_master_mp3: Path,

    target_duration_sec: int,

    bgm_file: Path | None = None,

    bgm_volume: float = 0.18,

    nature_style: str = "None (Pure Worship Music)"

) -> float:

    """

    Mixes the slow meditative voiceover with looped worship BGM and optional

    nature ambience, stretching or looping gracefully so the final audio matches

    the exact target duration (5m, 10m, 20m, 30m, or 1 Hour).

    """

    if not bgm_file or not bgm_file.exists():

        bgm_file = ensure_soaking_worship_pad()



    nature_file = ensure_nature_ambience(nature_style)

    voice_dur = get_audio_duration(voice_mp3)

    final_dur = max(int(voice_dur) + 10, int(target_duration_sec))

    fade_out_start = max(1, final_dur - 6)



    # If target duration is much longer than a single prayer pass (e.g. 30m or 1 Hour),

    # add a peaceful 45s soaking instrumental interlude and loop the prayer gently

    padded_voice = voice_mp3.parent / "padded_voice_loop.mp3"

    interlude_sil = voice_mp3.parent / "soaking_interlude.mp3"

    _create_silence_mp3(interlude_sil, 45.0)



    list_v = voice_mp3.parent / "voice_cycle.txt"

    cycles_needed = max(1, int(final_dur // max(1.0, (voice_dur + 45.0))) + 1)

    v_lines = []

    for _ in range(cycles_needed):

        v_lines.append(f"file '{voice_mp3.absolute().as_posix()}'")

        v_lines.append(f"file '{interlude_sil.absolute().as_posix()}'")

    list_v.write_text("\n".join(v_lines), encoding="utf-8")



    subprocess.run([

        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", str(list_v.absolute()),

        "-t", f"{final_dur}", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", str(padded_voice.absolute())

    ], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)



    if nature_file and nature_file.exists():

        cmd_mix = [

            "ffmpeg", "-y",

            "-i", str(padded_voice.absolute()),

            "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{final_dur}", "-i", str(bgm_file.absolute()),

            "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{final_dur}", "-i", str(nature_file.absolute()),

            "-filter_complex",

            f"[0:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume=1.0[v];"

            f"[1:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume={bgm_volume:.2f}[bgm];"

            f"[2:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume=0.06[nat];"

            f"[v][bgm][nat]amix=inputs=3:duration=longest:normalize=0,"

            f"afade=t=in:st=0:d=3,afade=t=out:st={fade_out_start}:d=6[aout]",

            "-map", "[aout]", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", "-t", f"{final_dur}",

            str(out_master_mp3.absolute())

        ]

    else:

        cmd_mix = [

            "ffmpeg", "-y",

            "-i", str(padded_voice.absolute()),

            "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{final_dur}", "-i", str(bgm_file.absolute()),

            "-filter_complex",

            f"[0:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume=1.0[v];"

            f"[1:a]aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo,volume={bgm_volume:.2f}[bgm];"

            f"[v][bgm]amix=inputs=2:duration=longest:normalize=0,"

            f"afade=t=in:st=0:d=3,afade=t=out:st={fade_out_start}:d=6[aout]",

            "-map", "[aout]", "-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "192k", "-t", f"{final_dur}",

            str(out_master_mp3.absolute())

        ]



    subprocess.run(cmd_mix, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    return get_audio_duration(out_master_mp3)







# ==============================================================================

# 5. STEP 3: ULTRA-FAST 16:9 (1920x1080) LONG-FORM VIDEO & THUMBNAIL RENDERER

# ==============================================================================

def get_next_longform_folder(label: str) -> Path:

    clean = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9]+", "_", label or "").strip("_")[:35] or "longform_prayer"

    nums = []

    for d in LONGFORM_DIR.iterdir():

        if d.is_dir():

            m = re.match(r"^LF(\d{3})_", d.name)

            if m:

                nums.append(int(m.group(1)))

    next_n = (max(nums) + 1) if nums else 1

    folder = LONGFORM_DIR / f"LF{next_n:03d}_{clean}"

    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    return folder



def create_16x9_stage_overlay_png(

    out_png: Path,

    topic: str,

    scriptures: List[Dict[str, str]],

    w: int = 1920,

    h: int = 1080

):

    """Creates 1920x1080 Double Gold Frame and top sanctuary banner, leaving bottom clean for live subs."""

    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont



    ov = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), (0, 0, 0, 0))

    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)



    # Top and bottom cinematic dark vignette bands

    for y in range(200):

        alpha = int(210 * (1.0 - y / 200.0))

        draw.line([(0, y), (w, y)], fill=(5, 8, 16, alpha))

    for y in range(h - 260, h):

        alpha = int(220 * ((y - (h - 260)) / 260.0))

        draw.line([(0, y), (w, y)], fill=(5, 8, 16, alpha))



    # Double Gold 16:9 Rounded Border

    gold = (225, 185, 65, 235)

    gold_soft = (225, 185, 65, 130)

    draw.rounded_rectangle([28, 28, w - 28, h - 28], radius=26, outline=gold, width=4)

    draw.rounded_rectangle([40, 40, w - 40, h - 40], radius=20, outline=gold_soft, width=2)



    def _fnt(sz: int, black: bool = False):

        cands = [

            Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/ariblk.ttf") if black else Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeuib.ttf"),

            Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/arialbd.ttf"),

            Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf")

        ]

        for p in cands:

            if p.exists():

                try: return ImageFont.truetype(str(p), sz)

                except Exception: pass

        return ImageFont.load_default()



    f_top = _fnt(28, black=True)



    # Top Sanctuary Pill Badge

    top_txt = f"DEEP SOUL PRAYER  •  {topic.upper()[:42]}"

    tb = draw.textbbox((0, 0), top_txt, font=f_top)

    tw, th = tb[2] - tb[0] + 60, tb[3] - tb[1] + 24

    bx = (w - tw) // 2

    by = 54

    draw.rounded_rectangle([bx, by, bx + tw, by + th], radius=th // 2, fill=(8, 12, 24, 230), outline=gold, width=2)

    draw.text(((w - (tb[2] - tb[0])) // 2 - tb[0], by + 11 - tb[1]), top_txt, font=f_top, fill=(255, 242, 175, 255))



    ov.save(str(out_png), "PNG")



def _make_lf_thumb(bg_image_path, journey_pkg, out_thumb_jpg, palette_name):
    import pro_design_addon as pda
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from PIL import Image
    try:
        first_ref = journey_pkg["scriptures"][0]["ref"] if journey_pkg.get("scriptures") else "PSALM 91:1"
        dummy_script = SimpleNamespace(
            topic=journey_pkg.get("topic", "DEEP PRAYER"),
            verse_citation=first_ref,
            thumbnail_hook=f"PRAY THIS OVER {journey_pkg.get('topic', 'PRAYER').upper()[:18]}",
            title=f"Deep Prayer for {journey_pkg.get('topic', 'Peace')}"
        )
        thumbs = pda.render_pro_thumbnails(bg_image_path, dummy_script, out_thumb_jpg, palette_name=palette_name)
        if "16x9" in thumbs and Path(thumbs["16x9"]).exists():
            im16 = Image.open(thumbs["16x9"]).convert("RGB")
            im16.save(str(out_thumb_jpg), "JPEG", quality=95)
    except Exception:
        pass

def render_longform_16x9_video(
    master_audio_mp3: Path,
    out_video_mp4: Path,
    out_thumb_jpg: Path,
    journey_pkg: Dict[str, Any],
    bg_image_path: Path,
    bg_video_path: Path | None = None,
    visual_mode: str = "Looping Video Background (Fastest)",
    palette_name: str = "Gold Classic",
    subtitle_mode: str = "Instant Render (YouTube CC / .SRT)",
    progress_cb=None,
    **kwargs
) -> bool:
    """
    TURBO LONG-FORM 16:9 RENDER ENGINE:
    - If subtitle_mode is Instant Render: stream-copies (-c:v copy) in ~3 seconds!
    - If subtitle_mode is Burned-In: encodes with GPU/CPU hardware acceleration.
    """
    import pro_design_addon as pda
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from PIL import Image

    w, h = 1920, 1080
    total_dur = get_audio_duration(master_audio_mp3)
    if total_dur <= 1.0:
        total_dur = float(journey_pkg.get("config", {}).get("target_seconds", 300))

    work_dir = out_video_mp4.parent / "temp_lf_render"
    work_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. Overlay frame (Top banner & borders)
    overlay_png = work_dir / "lf_overlay_16x9.png"
    create_16x9_stage_overlay_png(overlay_png, journey_pkg.get("topic", "Prayer"), journey_pkg.get("scriptures", []), w=w, h=h)

    # 2. Check for subtitles file
    ass_path = out_video_mp4.parent / "longform_subtitles.ass"
    if not ass_path.exists():
        ass_path = master_audio_mp3.parent / "longform_subtitles.ass"

    # Pre-bake background with Pillow (takes 0.05s)
    prebaked_bg_jpg = work_dir / "prebaked_bg_1080p.jpg"
    try:
        raw_bg = Image.open(bg_image_path).convert("RGB")
        ir = raw_bg.width / max(1, raw_bg.height)
        tr = w / h
        nw, nh = (int(h * ir), h) if ir > tr else (w, int(w / max(0.01, ir)))
        raw_bg = raw_bg.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        raw_bg = raw_bg.crop(((nw - w) // 2, (nh - h) // 2, (nw - w) // 2 + w, (nh - h) // 2 + h)).convert("RGBA")
        ov_im = Image.open(overlay_png).convert("RGBA")
        prebaked = Image.alpha_composite(raw_bg, ov_im).convert("RGB")
        prebaked.save(str(prebaked_bg_jpg), "JPEG", quality=94)
    except Exception:
        shutil.copy(bg_image_path, prebaked_bg_jpg)

    is_instant_mode = ("Instant" in str(subtitle_mode) or "CC" in str(subtitle_mode) or "SRT" in str(subtitle_mode))

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # BRANCH A: INSTANT 3-SECOND RENDER (Stream-Copy -c:v copy + .SRT)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    if is_instant_mode:
        print(f"   ⚡ [Instant Render] Stream-copying 1080p video across {total_dur:.1f}s audio (~3s total)...")
        if progress_cb:
            progress_cb(85, "⚡ Pre-baking loop & stream-copying in ~3s...")

        loop_segment = work_dir / "instant_base_loop.mp4"
        if "Video" in visual_mode and bg_video_path and bg_video_path.exists():
            cmd_base = [
                "ffmpeg", "-y", "-threads", "0",
                "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", "16", "-i", str(bg_video_path.absolute()),
                "-loop", "1", "-t", "16", "-i", str(overlay_png.absolute()),
                "-filter_complex",
                f"[0:v]scale={w}:{h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={w}:{h},fps=24,format=yuv420p,setsar=1,eq=brightness=-0.07:contrast=1.10[v0];"
                f"[v0][1:v]overlay=0:0:format=auto,format=yuv420p[vout]",
                "-map", "[vout]", "-an", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "19",
                str(loop_segment.absolute())
            ]
        else:
            cmd_base = [
                "ffmpeg", "-y", "-threads", "0",
                "-loop", "1", "-t", "16", "-i", str(prebaked_bg_jpg.absolute()),
                "-vf", f"scale={w}:{h},fps=24,format=yuv420p",
                "-an", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "19",
                str(loop_segment.absolute())
            ]
        subprocess.run(cmd_base, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

        if loop_segment.exists() and loop_segment.stat().st_size > 5000:
            if progress_cb:
                progress_cb(92, "⚡ Muxing master video & audio...")
            cmd_instant = [
                "ffmpeg", "-y",
                "-stream_loop", "-1", "-i", str(loop_segment.absolute()),
                "-i", str(master_audio_mp3.absolute()),
                "-map", "0:v:0", "-map", "1:a:0",
                "-c:v", "copy", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k",
                "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}",
                str(out_video_mp4.absolute())
            ]
            r_inst = subprocess.run(cmd_instant, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            if r_inst.returncode == 0 and out_video_mp4.exists():
                print("   🎉 Instant 1080p Render complete in ~3 seconds!")
                _make_lf_thumb(bg_image_path, journey_pkg, out_thumb_jpg, palette_name)
                return True

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # BRANCH B: HARD-SUBS BURNING (Re-encodes with subtitles on every frame)
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    sub_filter_str = f"subtitles='{ass_path.absolute().as_posix()}'" if ass_path.exists() else "null"
    enc_name, enc_opts = detect_hardware_video_encoder()

    if "Video" in visual_mode and bg_video_path and bg_video_path.exists():
        filter_complex = (
            f"[0:v]scale={w}:{h}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop={w}:{h},fps=24,format=yuv420p,setsar=1,eq=brightness=-0.07:contrast=1.10[v0];"
            f"[v0][1:v]overlay=0:0:format=auto,{sub_filter_str}[vout]"
        )
        inputs = [
            "-stream_loop", "-1", "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(bg_video_path.absolute()),
            "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(overlay_png.absolute()),
            "-i", str(master_audio_mp3.absolute()),
            "-filter_complex", filter_complex,
            "-map", "[vout]", "-map", "2:a:0"
        ]
    else:
        filter_complex = f"[0:v]scale={w}:{h},fps=24,format=yuv420p,{sub_filter_str}[vout]"
        inputs = [
            "-loop", "1", "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}", "-i", str(prebaked_bg_jpg.absolute()),
            "-i", str(master_audio_mp3.absolute()),
            "-filter_complex", filter_complex,
            "-map", "[vout]", "-map", "1:a:0"
        ]

    cmd = [
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-threads", "0",
        *inputs,
        "-c:v", enc_name, *enc_opts,
        "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k",
        "-t", f"{total_dur:.2f}",
        "-progress", "pipe:1",
        str(out_video_mp4.absolute())
    ]

    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, universal_newlines=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    last_ui_update = 0.0

    if proc.stdout:
        for line in proc.stdout:
            if "out_time_us=" in line:
                try:
                    us_val = int(line.strip().split("=")[1])
                    curr_sec = us_val / 1000000.0
                    now_t = time.time()
                    if (now_t - last_ui_update) >= 1.2:
                        last_ui_update = now_t
                        pct = min(99, int(80 + (curr_sec / max(1.0, total_dur)) * 19))
                        if progress_cb:
                            progress_cb(pct, f"🎬 Rendering 1080p Video: {format_mm_ss(curr_sec)} / {format_mm_ss(total_dur)} ({curr_sec/total_dur*100:.0f}%)")
                except Exception:
                    pass
    proc.wait()

    _make_lf_thumb(bg_image_path, journey_pkg, out_thumb_jpg, palette_name)
    return proc.returncode == 0 and out_video_mp4.exists()

def build_longform_youtube_metadata(
    journey_pkg: Dict[str, Any],
    chapters: List[Dict[str, Any]]
) -> Tuple[str, str, List[str]]:
    """
    Builds an AI-optimized high-CTR Long-Form Title, Chapter-Timestamped Description
    with 3 Amazon Affiliate Links, and 380-character packed SEO tags.
    """
    import grace_of_lord_engine as gle
    import viral_researcher as vr

    topic = clean_ascii(journey_pkg.get("topic", "Peace")).strip()
    dur_lbl = journey_pkg.get("duration_label", "10 Minutes")
    scriptures = journey_pkg.get("scriptures", [])
    first_ref = scriptures[0]["ref"] if scriptures else "Psalm 91"
    all_refs = [v["ref"] for v in scriptures]

    # 1. AI-POWERED LONG-FORM TITLE (Search + Browse Optimized)
    ai_title = ""
    prompt = f"""Generate 1 high-CTR YouTube video title for a {dur_lbl} long-form Christian prayer & scripture soaking video.
Topic: {topic}
Key Scriptures: {', '.join(all_refs[:4])}
Rules:
- Title must be between 65 and 95 characters.
- Must feel deep, comforting, and soul-stirring (NOT a short).
- Examples of winning titles:
  • \"When You Feel Overwhelmed & Tired | {dur_lbl} Deep Peace Prayer & Scripture (Listen Before Bed)\"
  • \"FALL ASLEEP IN GOD'S PEACE | {dur_lbl} Powerful Sleep Scriptures & Deep Prayer ({first_ref})\"
  • \"PRAY THIS OVER YOUR HOME & FAMILY | {dur_lbl} Protection & Breakthrough Prayer\"

Respond with ONLY the title string, no quotes."""

    try:
        from viral_researcher import call_gemini_fast
        t_res = call_gemini_fast(prompt, system="Output only the title string. No markdown.")
        if t_res:
            cand = t_res.get("title") if isinstance(t_res, dict) else str(t_res).strip()
            if cand and 45 <= len(cand) <= 98:
                ai_title = cand
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not ai_title:
        ai_title = f"When You Need God's Breakthrough | {dur_lbl} Deep Prayer & Scripture ({first_ref})"[:98]

    # 2. CHAPTER TIMESTAMPS
    ch_lines = []
    has_zero = False
    for ch in chapters:
        ts = ch.get("timestamp", "00:00")
        if ts.startswith("00:00") or ts.startswith("0:00"):
            has_zero = True
        ch_lines.append(f"{ts} - {ch.get('title', 'Prayer Movement')}")

    if not has_zero:
        ch_lines.insert(0, "00:00 - Entering God's Sanctuary & Releasing Burdens")

    chapter_block = "\n".join(ch_lines)

    # 3. VERSE SUMMARY INDEX
    v_lines = []
    for v in scriptures[:8]:
        v_lines.append(f"• {v['ref']}: \"{v['text'][:115]}...\"")
    verse_block = "\n".join(v_lines)

    # 4. LOAD 3 AMAZON AFFILIATE LINKS
    aff_links = gle.load_affiliate_links()
    aff_block = "\n\n".join([f"{item['heading']}\n{item['url']}" for item in aff_links])

    # 5. DYNAMIC CHRISTIAN HASHTAGS
    t_clean_slug = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9]', '', topic.title())[:18]
    hashtags = [
        f"#{t_clean_slug}", "#ChristianPrayer", "#DeepPrayer", "#SoakingPrayer",
        "#SleepPrayer", "#BibleVerses", "#JesusChrist", "#FaithOverFear",
        "#PeaceOfGod", "#PrayerForStrength", "#DailyDevotional", "#BiblicalPeace"
    ]
    ht_line = " ".join(hashtags)

    # 6. ASSEMBLE MONETIZED LONG-FORM DESCRIPTION
    desc = f"""🙏 {topic.upper()} — {dur_lbl.upper()} DEEP SOUL PRAYER & SCRIPTURE SOAKING ({first_ref}) ❤️

Take a slow, deep breath and enter into God's quiet sanctuary. If your heart has been carrying the silent weight of {topic}, let this {dur_lbl.lower()} prayer and scripture soaking journey bring supernatural peace, divine strength, and unshakeable hope into your home.

⏱️ CHAPTER TIMESTAMPS:
{chapter_block}

📖 SCRIPTURES PRAYED IN THIS VIDEO:
{verse_block}

----------------------------------------
💬 Let us agree in prayer! Leave your prayer request in the comments below or comment \"AMEN\" to seal this prayer over your family.
🔔 Subscribe to Grace of the Lord (@GraceAndDailyPrayer) for daily soul prayers, sleep Psalms, and biblical breakthrough.

----------------------------------------
{aff_block}

Disclaimer: As an Amazon Associate, I earn from qualifying purchases at no extra cost to you.

----------------------------------------
{ht_line}
""".strip()

    # 7. PACK 380 CHARACTERS OF TARGETED LONG-FORM SEARCH TAGS
    raw_tag_candidates = [
        f"{topic} Prayer",
        f"Prayer for {topic}",
        f"{dur_lbl} Christian Prayer",
        "Deep Soul Prayer",
        "Soaking Prayer",
        "Christian Meditation",
        "Bible Verses For Sleep",
        "Sleep In Peace",
        "Peace Of God",
        "Jesus Christ",
        "Powerful Prayer",
        "Spiritual Warfare Prayer",
        "Grace Of The Lord",
        "Daily Devotional",
        "Biblical Wisdom"
    ] + [v.replace(":", " ") for v in all_refs[:6]] + hashtags

    packed_tags = vr.pack_tags_to_limit(topic, hashtags, raw_tag_candidates, max_chars=380)

    return ai_title, desc, packed_tags

def sanitize_youtube_tags(raw_tags: list, max_total_chars: int = 380) -> list[str]:

    cleaned_tags = []

    seen = set()

    total_len = 0



    flat_list = []

    for item in raw_tags:

        if isinstance(item, str):

            for part in item.split(","):

                flat_list.append(part)

        elif isinstance(item, list):

            for part in item:

                flat_list.append(str(part))



    for t in flat_list:

        clean = re.sub(r"[<>,#\"'\\`\r\n\t&!?:;]", "", str(t)).strip()

        clean = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", clean)

        if 2 <= len(clean) <= 35 and clean.lower() not in seen:

            cost = len(clean) + (2 if " " in clean else 0) + 1

            if total_len + cost <= max_total_chars:

                cleaned_tags.append(clean)

                seen.add(clean.lower())

                total_len += cost



    return cleaned_tags[:20]

def build_viral_devotional_description(topic: str, ref_str: str, verse_text: str, reflection: str, prayer: str) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:

    """

    Builds a deeply emotional, high-retention long-form devotional description

    with high-converting calls-to-action and targeted viral hashtags.

    """

    topic_clean = clean_ascii(topic).strip()

    topic_lower = topic_clean.lower()



    # Determine custom topic hashtags

    matched_tags = []

    for k, tags in TOPIC_HASHTAG_MAP.items():

        if k in topic_lower:

            matched_tags = tags

            break

    if not matched_tags:

        clean_word = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9]', '', topic_clean.title())

        matched_tags = [f"#{clean_word}", "#PrayThisFirst", "#GodHearsYou", "#NeverGiveUp"]



    # Core universal viral Christian hashtags

    universal_tags = [

        "#PrayThisFirst", "#PowerfulPrayer", "#JesusChrist", "#DailyPrayer",

        "#GodIsWithYou", "#FaithOverFear", "#ChristianShorts", "#PrayerWorks",

        "#HopeInGod", "#YouTubeShorts"

    ]



    all_hts = []

    seen = set()

    for ht in (matched_tags + universal_tags):

        ht_norm = ht.lower()

        if ht_norm not in seen:

            all_hts.append(ht)

            seen.add(ht_norm)

        if len(all_hts) >= 13:

            break



    ht_line = " ".join(all_hts)



    desc = f"""🙏 DON'T GIVE UP! PRAY THIS FIRST! GOD HEARS YOUR CRY! ❤️



WAIT! Before you lose hope or let your struggles overwhelm your heart, take a deep breath and pray with me.



Are you tired of pretending everything is okay? Are you silently fighting battles nobody knows about? Have you been praying for a breakthrough while wondering when things will finally get better?



God sees every tear you hide, hears every prayer you whisper, and knows every burden you carry.



❤️ You don't have to carry this alone. Bring your worries, fears, disappointments, and struggles to Jesus.



📖 Scripture: {ref_str}

\"{verse_text.strip()}\"



💡 Reflection:

{reflection.strip()}



🙏 Surrender Prayer:

{prayer.strip()}



When life feels impossible, remember that nothing is impossible with God. When human strength runs out, His supernatural grace is more than enough!



DON'T GIVE UP JUST YET! YOUR STORY IS NOT OVER, YOUR PRAYERS STILL MATTER, AND GOD HAS NOT FORGOTTEN YOU!



👇 If you are trusting God through this season, comment \"AMEN\"!

❤️ Share this prayer with someone who is silently struggling and needs a reminder that God is near.



May the Lord bless you, protect your family, strengthen your faith, and fill your heart with everlasting peace.

IN JESUS' MIGHTY NAME, AMEN! 🙏



[HASHTAGS]

{ht_line}

[/HASHTAGS]

""".strip()



    return desc, all_hts

    "US": "United States 🇺🇸", "IN": "India 🇮🇳", "GB": "United Kingdom 🇬🇧",

    "PH": "Philippines 🇵🇭", "CA": "Canada 🇨🇦", "AU": "Australia 🇦🇺",

def get_channel_overview_stats(channel_name: str) -> dict:

    """Fetches exact subscriber count, lifetime channel views, and total videos."""

    youtube = get_authenticated_youtube(channel_name)

    if not youtube:

        return {}

    try:

        res = youtube.channels().list(mine=True, part="snippet,statistics").execute()

        track_yt_quota(1)

        if not res.get("items"):

            return {}

        item = res["items"][0]

        stats = item.get("statistics", {})

        snip = item.get("snippet", {})

        return {

            "title": snip.get("title", channel_name),

            "subscribers": int(stats.get("subscriberCount", 0)),

            "total_views": int(stats.get("viewCount", 0)),

            "video_count": int(stats.get("videoCount", 0)),

            "hidden_subscribers": stats.get("hiddenSubscriberCount", False)

        }

    except Exception as e:

        print(f"   [Channel Stats Note] {e}")

        return {}



def get_channel_geo_analytics(channel_name: str) -> tuple[list[dict], str | None]:

    """Fetches real-time geographical country views via YouTube Analytics API."""

    channels = load_channels()

    token_path = channels.get(channel_name)

    if not token_path or not Path(token_path).exists():

        return [], "Channel not authenticated"



    try:

        with open(token_path, "rb") as f:

            credentials = pickle.load(f)



        if not credentials or not credentials.valid:

            if credentials and credentials.expired and credentials.refresh_token:

                credentials.refresh(Request())

            else:

                return [], "Credentials expired"



        # Build YouTube Analytics API v2 service

        yt_analytics = build("youtubeAnalytics", "v2", credentials=credentials)

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        end_date = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        start_date = (now - timedelta(days=90)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")



        # Query top countries by views

        response = yt_analytics.reports().query(

            ids="channel==MINE",

            startDate=start_date,

            endDate=end_date,

            metrics="views",

            dimensions="country",

            sort="-views",

            maxResults=10

        ).execute()



        rows = response.get("rows", [])

        if not rows:

            return [], "No geographical data available yet (takes 24-48h for YouTube to process new views)."



        total_geo_views = sum(r[1] for r in rows) or 1

        geo_list = []

        for r in rows:

            country_code = str(r[0]).upper()

            country_name = COUNTRY_FLAGS.get(country_code, f"{country_code} 🌐")

            views = int(r[1])

            pct = round((views / total_geo_views) * 100, 1)

            geo_list.append({

                "country_code": country_code,

                "country": country_name,

                "views": views,

                "percentage": pct

            })



        return geo_list, None



    except Exception as e:

        err_msg = str(e)

        if "accessNotConfigured" in err_msg or "disabled" in err_msg or "has not used" in err_msg:

            return [], "YouTube Analytics API is not enabled in your Google Cloud Project. Enable it at: console.cloud.google.com"

        return [], f"Analytics Notice: {err_msg[:90]}"





def get_video_duration(video_path: Path) -> float:

    """Returns exact float duration in seconds using ffprobe."""

    try:

        cmd = ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration", "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1", str(video_path.absolute())]

        res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=8, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

        return round(float(res.stdout.strip()), 1)

    except Exception:

        return 0.0



def download_youtube_audio_direct(url: str) -> Path | None:

    """Downloads 192kbps MP3 without interactive prompt."""

    try:

        out_tmpl = str(DOWNLOADS_DIR / '%(title)s.%(ext)s')

        ydl_opts = {

            'format': 'bestaudio/best',

            'outtmpl': out_tmpl,

            'postprocessors': [{'key': 'FFmpegExtractAudio', 'preferredcodec': 'mp3', 'preferredquality': '192'}],

            'overwrites': True,

            'quiet': True,

        }

        with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:

            info = ydl.extract_info(url.strip(), download=True)

            fname = ydl.prepare_filename(info)

            mp3_path = Path(fname).with_suffix(".mp3")

            return mp3_path if mp3_path.exists() else None

    except Exception:

        return None



def download_youtube_video_direct(url: str) -> Path | None:

    """Downloads 1080p MP4 without interactive prompt."""

    try:

        out_tmpl = str(DOWNLOADS_DIR / '%(title)s.%(ext)s')

        ydl_opts = {

            'format': 'bestvideo[height<=1080][ext=mp4]+bestaudio[ext=m4a]/best[height<=1080]/best',

            'outtmpl': out_tmpl,

            'merge_output_format': 'mp4',

            'overwrites': True,

            'quiet': True,

        }

        with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:

            info = ydl.extract_info(url.strip(), download=True)

            fname = ydl.prepare_filename(info)

            mp4_path = Path(fname)

            if not mp4_path.exists():

                mp4_path = mp4_path.with_suffix(".mp4")

            return mp4_path if mp4_path.exists() else None

    except Exception:

        return None

def mute_any_video(input_vid: Path) -> Path:

    """Strips audio and normalizes video stream so FFmpeg concat never fails with -22."""

    if not input_vid or not input_vid.exists():

        return input_vid

    muted_dir = INTRO_DIR / "muted_cache"

    muted_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    safe_stem = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_]+", "_", input_vid.stem).strip("_")[:30]

    muted_out = muted_dir / f"muted_{safe_stem}.mp4"

    if not muted_out.exists() or muted_out.stat().st_size < 1000:

        cmd = [

            "ffmpeg", "-y", "-i", str(input_vid),

            "-t", "12", "-an",

            "-vf", "scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=1080:1920,fps=24,format=yuv420p,setsar=1",

            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "20",

            str(muted_out)

        ]

        subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    return muted_out if muted_out.exists() else input_vid



def choose_media_in_cli(topic: str) -> tuple[Path | None, Path | None, Path | None]:

    """Shows AI suggestions, exact save/rename instructions, and lets user pick files in CLI."""

    sug_intro, sug_bg, sug_thumb = ("Heavenly golden clouds moving", "Jesus in white robes with open arms", "High-contrast portrait of Jesus with golden glow")

    for k, val in TOPIC_VISUAL_SUGGESTIONS.items():

        if k.lower() in topic.lower():

            sug_intro, sug_bg, sug_thumb = val

            break



    safe_tag = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "", topic.title())[:12] or "Miracle"



    print("\n" + "=" * 68)

    print(f"🎨 AI VISUAL SUGGESTIONS & FILE GUIDE FOR: '{topic}'")

    print("=" * 68)

    print("💡 WHAT VISUALS TO USE FOR MAXIMUM VIEWS:")

    print(f"   • 10s Intro Video : {sug_intro}")

    print(f"   • Main Background : {sug_bg}")

    print(f"   • Thumbnail Image : {sug_thumb}")

    print("-" * 68)

    print("📂 WHERE TO SAVE & WHAT TO RENAME YOUR FILES (CLEAR INSTRUCTIONS):")

    print(f"   1. INTRO VIDEO (0-10s):")

    print(f"      👉 Save inside : {INTRO_DIR.resolve()}")

    print(f"      👉 Rename to   : Intro_{safe_tag}.mp4   (Audio will be muted automatically!)")

    print(f"   2. MAIN BACKGROUND IMAGE (10s-End):")

    print(f"      👉 Save inside : {BG_DIR.resolve()}")

    print(f"      👉 Rename to   : BG_{safe_tag}.jpg")

    print(f"   3. THUMBNAIL BACKGROUND IMAGE:")

    print(f"      👉 Save inside : {THUMB_DIR.resolve()}")

    print(f"      👉 Rename to   : Thumb_{safe_tag}.jpg")

    print("=" * 68)



    input("👉 (Optional) Drop your files into the folders above now, then press [ENTER] to select...")



    def _pick_from_folder(folder: Path, label: str, exts: tuple, fallback: Path | None) -> Path | None:

        files = sorted([f for f in folder.iterdir() if f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() in exts], key=lambda x: x.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)

        if not files:

            print(f"   • No custom {label} in folder yet -> Using default.")

            return fallback

        print(f"\n📁 Select {label}:")

        print(f"   [Enter] Auto-use newest: {files[0].name}")

        for idx, f in enumerate(files):

            print(f"   [{idx + 1}] {f.name}")

        ch = input(f"Choose (1-{len(files)}) or press Enter: ").strip()

        if ch.isdigit() and 1 <= int(ch) <= len(files):

            return files[int(ch) - 1]

        return files[0]



    chosen_intro = _pick_from_folder(INTRO_DIR, "10s Intro Video", (".mp4", ".mov", ".mkv", ".webm"), get_short_main_video())

    chosen_bg = _pick_from_folder(BG_DIR, "Main Background Image", (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"), get_jesus_image())

    chosen_thumb = _pick_from_folder(THUMB_DIR, "Thumbnail Image", (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"), chosen_bg)



    if chosen_intro:

        chosen_intro = mute_any_video(chosen_intro)



    return chosen_intro, chosen_bg, chosen_thumb





# --- DYNAMIC ASSET LOCATORS ---

def get_file_by_name(base_name: str, extensions: list[str]) -> Path | None:

    for ext in extensions:

        p = BASE_DIR / f"{base_name}{ext}"

        if p.exists():

            return p

    matches = list(BASE_DIR.glob(f"{base_name}*"))

    return matches[0] if matches else None



def get_worship_bgm() -> Path:

    sample = get_file_by_name("worship_sample", [".mp3", ".wav", ".m4a", ".mp4", ""])

    if sample and sample.exists():

        return sample

    return BASE_DIR / "soft_worship_piano.mp3"



def get_jesus_image() -> Path:

    img = get_file_by_name("Jesus_Lord", [".jpg", ".png", ".jpeg", ".webp", ""])

    if img and img.exists():

        return img

    fb = BASE_DIR / "jesus_white_robes.jpg"

    return fb



def get_short_main_video() -> Path | None:

    vid = get_file_by_name("Short_Main", [".mp4", ".mov", ".mkv", ".webm", ""])

    return vid

# --- 1. ASSET PREPARATION & MUTING ---

def prepare_muted_short_main() -> Path | None:

    """Mutes Short_Main video and saves it as Short_Main_muted.mp4."""

    raw_video = get_short_main_video()

    if not raw_video or not raw_video.exists():

        return None



    muted_file = BASE_DIR / "Short_Main_muted.mp4"

    if not muted_file.exists() or muted_file.stat().st_size < 1000:

        print("  • Muting 'Short_Main' and saving as 'Short_Main_muted.mp4'...")

        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-i", str(raw_video), "-an", "-c:v", "copy", str(muted_file)]

        subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)



    return muted_file if muted_file.exists() else raw_video



def ensure_sacred_assets():

    """Ensures fallback assets exist if user media is not placed."""

    jesus_file = get_jesus_image()

    if not jesus_file.exists() or jesus_file.stat().st_size < 10000:

        headers = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"}

        try:

            req = urllib.request.Request("https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544830237-7d5d28b12f66?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1080&q=80", headers=headers)

            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=6) as resp, open(jesus_file, "wb") as f:

                f.write(resp.read())

        except Exception:

            cmd_gen = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "color=c=0x070B14:s=1080x1920:d=1", "-vf", "drawbox=x=80:y=200:w=920:h=1520:color=0xD4AF37@0.25:t=fill", "-vframes", "1", str(jesus_file)]

            subprocess.run(cmd_gen, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)



    bgm_file = get_worship_bgm()

    if not bgm_file.exists() or bgm_file.stat().st_size < 10000:

        cmd_audio = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=130.81:sample_rate=44100", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=164.81:sample_rate=44100", "-filter_complex", "amix=inputs=2:normalize=0,volume=0.25,lowpass=f=450", "-t", "120", str(bgm_file)]

        subprocess.run(cmd_audio, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    if not BGM_PODCAST_FILE.exists() or BGM_PODCAST_FILE.stat().st_size < 10000:

        try:

            shutil.copy(bgm_file, BGM_PODCAST_FILE)

        except Exception:

            pass



# --- 2. MULTI-CHANNEL SYSTEM ---

def load_channels() -> dict:

    if CHANNELS_CONFIG.exists():

        try:

            with open(CHANNELS_CONFIG, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:

                channels = json.load(f)

            if channels:

                return channels

        except Exception:

            pass

    default_pkl = CHANNELS_DIR / "Grace_of_the_Lord.pickle"

    return {"Grace of the Lord (@GraceAndDailyPrayer)": str(default_pkl)}



def save_channels(data: dict):

    with open(CHANNELS_CONFIG, "w") as f:

        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)



def connect_new_channel():

    if not CLIENT_SECRET_FILE.exists():

        print("❌ Missing client_secret.json in project root directory!")

        return None



    print("\n🌐 Opening Google login in your web browser...")

    flow = InstalledAppFlow.from_client_secrets_file(str(CLIENT_SECRET_FILE), YOUTUBE_SCOPES)

    credentials = flow.run_local_server(port=0, prompt="consent")

    youtube = build("youtube", "v3", credentials=credentials)

    res = youtube.channels().list(mine=True, part="snippet").execute()



    if not res.get("items"):

        return None



    channel_name = res["items"][0]["snippet"]["title"]

    safe_name = re.sub(r'[\/*?:"<>| ]', "_", channel_name)

    token_file = CHANNELS_DIR / f"{safe_name}.pickle"



    with open(token_file, "wb") as f:

        pickle.dump(credentials, f)



    channels = load_channels()

    channels[channel_name] = str(token_file)

    save_channels(channels)

    print(f"✅ Successfully connected: '{channel_name}'!")

    return channel_name



def get_authenticated_youtube(channel_name: str):

    channels = load_channels()

    token_path = channels.get(channel_name)

    if not token_path or not Path(token_path).exists():

        return None



    with open(token_path, "rb") as f:

        credentials = pickle.load(f)



    if not credentials or not credentials.valid:

        if credentials and credentials.expired and credentials.refresh_token:

            credentials.refresh(Request())

            with open(token_path, "wb") as f:

                pickle.dump(credentials, f)

        else:

            return None



    return build("youtube", "v3", credentials=credentials)



# --- 3. METADATA & UPLOADER ---

def clean_filename(text: str) -> str:

    clean = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9]+", "_", text or "")

    return clean.strip("_")[:35] or "media_item"



def timestamp_to_seconds(ts: str) -> float:

    parts = [float(p) for p in ts.strip().split(":")]

    if len(parts) == 3:

        return parts[0] * 3600 + parts[1] * 60 + parts[2]

    elif len(parts) == 2:

        return parts[0] * 60 + parts[1]

    return parts[0]



def get_next_sequence_folder(label: str) -> Path:

    nums = []

    for d in CLIPS_DIR.iterdir():

        if d.is_dir():

            m = re.match(r"^(\d{3})_", d.name)

            if m:

                nums.append(int(m.group(1)))

    next_n = (max(nums) + 1) if nums else 1

    folder = CLIPS_DIR / f"{next_n:03d}_{clean_filename(label)}"

    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    return folder

# --- A. HELPER UTILITIES ---



def parse_iso_duration(duration_str: str) -> int:

    """Parses ISO 8601 duration (e.g. PT45S, PT1M02S) into total seconds."""

    if not duration_str:

        return 0

    m = re.match(r"PT(?:(\d+)H)?(?:(\d+)M)?(?:(\d+)S)?", duration_str)

    if not m:

        return 0

    h = int(m.group(1) or 0)

    mins = int(m.group(2) or 0)

    secs = int(m.group(3) or 0)

    return h * 3600 + mins * 60 + secs





def track_yt_quota(units_spent: int) -> int:

    """Tracks estimated daily YouTube Data API quota units (10,000 free units/day)."""

    today_str = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    data = {"date": today_str, "used": 0}

    if YT_QUOTA_FILE.exists():

        try:

            with open(YT_QUOTA_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:

                saved = json.load(f)

            if saved.get("date") == today_str:

                data = saved

        except Exception:

            pass



    data["used"] += units_spent

    try:

        with open(YT_QUOTA_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:

            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

    except Exception:

        pass



    remaining = max(0, 10000 - data["used"])

    print(f"   [API Quota] ~{data['used']:,} used today (~{remaining:,} units remaining)")

    return remaining





def iter_llm_json(prompt: str, system: str = "", temperature: float = 0.7, timeout: int = 8) -> dict | list | None:

    """Direct REST API caller for Gemini with timeout protection and JSON normalization."""

    if not API_KEY:

        return None



    candidate_models = ["gemini-3.5-flash-lite", "gemini-3.7-flash", "gemini-2.5-flash"]

    payload = {

        "contents": [{"parts": [{"text": f"{system}\n\n{prompt}".strip()}]}],

        "generationConfig": {"responseMimeType": "application/json", "temperature": temperature}

    }

    encoded_data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")



    for model_name in candidate_models:

        try:

            url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model_name}:generateContent?key={API_KEY}"

            req = urllib.request.Request(

                url,

                headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "x-goog-api-key": API_KEY},

                data=encoded_data

            )

            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:

                resp_json = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))

                raw_text = resp_json["candidates"][0]["content"]["parts"][0]["text"]

                m = re.search(r"(\{[\s\S]*\}|\[[\s\S]*\])", raw_text)

                if m:

                    return json.loads(m.group(0))

        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:

            print(f"   [LLM Note] {model_name} HTTP {e.code}: {e.reason}")

            continue

        except Exception as e:

            print(f"   [LLM Note] {model_name} connection error: {e}")

            continue



    # Fallback to OpenRouter if configured

    or_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")

    if or_key:

        try:

            req = urllib.request.Request(

                "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",

                headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {or_key}", "Content-Type": "application/json"},

                data=json.dumps({

                    "model": "google/gemma-2-9b-it:free",

                    "messages": [

                        {"role": "system", "content": system},

                        {"role": "user", "content": prompt}

                    ]

                }).encode("utf-8")

            )

            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:

                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))

                raw_text = data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

                m = re.search(r"(\{[\s\S]*\}|\[[\s\S]*\])", raw_text)

                if m:

                    return json.loads(m.group(0))

        except Exception as e:

            print(f"   [LLM Note] OpenRouter fallback error: {e}")



    return None





def get_recent_titles(limit: int = 50) -> list[str]:

    """Retrieves past generated/uploaded titles to eliminate near-duplicate suggestions."""

    if TITLES_LOG_FILE.exists():

        try:

            with open(TITLES_LOG_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:

                titles = json.load(f)

                if isinstance(titles, list):

                    return titles[-limit:]

        except Exception:

            pass

    return []





def record_recent_title(title: str):

    """Caches winning titles locally to maintain variety across videos."""

    titles = get_recent_titles(limit=100)

    if title not in titles:

        titles.append(title)

    try:

        with open(TITLES_LOG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:

            json.dump(titles[-50:], f, indent=2)

    except Exception:

        pass





def title_score(title: str, recent_titles: list[str], hook: str = "", verbose: bool = False) -> tuple[int, dict]:

    """

    Computes a transparent mathematical score (0-100) based on 5 strict criteria:

    1. Length (25-55 chars) [20 pts]

    2. Emotional or curiosity trigger word [20 pts]

    3. Low similarity to past 50 titles (<0.75 difflib ratio) [20 pts]

    4. Free of banned/unrealistic claims [20 pts]

    5. Alignment with thumbnail hook [20 pts]

    """

    clean_t = title.strip()

    checks = {}

    total = 0



    # 1. Length check

    t_len = len(clean_t)

    if 25 <= t_len <= 55:

        checks["Length (25-55 chars)"] = (True, f"{t_len} chars (+20)")

        total += 20

    elif 20 <= t_len < 25 or 55 < t_len <= 65:

        checks["Length (25-55 chars)"] = (False, f"{t_len} chars (suboptimal, +10)")

        total += 10

    else:

        checks["Length (25-55 chars)"] = (False, f"{t_len} chars (too short/long, +0)")



    # 2. Emotional/Curiosity keyword check

    t_words = set(re.findall(r"\b[a-zA-Z]+\b", clean_t.lower()))

    found_emo = t_words.intersection(EMOTIONAL_CURIOSITY_WORDS)

    if found_emo:

        checks["Curiosity/Emotion Hook"] = (True, f"Matched: {', '.join(list(found_emo)[:2])} (+20)")

        total += 20

    else:

        checks["Curiosity/Emotion Hook"] = (False, "No power/curiosity word detected (+0)")



    # 3. Near-duplicate check against recent titles

    max_sim = 0.0

    matched_prior = ""

    for past_t in recent_titles:

        sim = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, clean_t.lower(), past_t.lower()).ratio()

        if sim > max_sim:

            max_sim = sim

            matched_prior = past_t



    if max_sim < 0.75 or not recent_titles:

        checks["Title Uniqueness"] = (True, f"Max similarity {max_sim:.2f} (<0.75) (+20)")

        total += 20

    else:

        checks["Title Uniqueness"] = (False, f"Too similar ({max_sim:.2f}) to: '{matched_prior[:32]}...' (+0)")



    # 4. Prohibited/Banned claims check

    banned_m = BANNED_CLAIMS_PATTERN.search(clean_t)

    if not banned_m:

        checks["Safety & Truthfulness"] = (True, "Zero prohibited claims detected (+20)")

        total += 20

    else:

        checks["Safety & Truthfulness"] = (False, f"Banned claim detected: '{banned_m.group(0)}' (0 pts)")

        total = max(0, total - 25)



    # 5. Thumbnail Hook Match

    if hook:

        hook_tokens = set(re.findall(r"\b[a-zA-Z]{3,}\b", hook.lower()))

        hook_matches = t_words.intersection(hook_tokens)

        if hook_matches:

            checks["Thumbnail Hook Sync"] = (True, f"Aligned on: {', '.join(hook_matches)} (+20)")

            total += 20

        else:

            checks["Thumbnail Hook Sync"] = (False, "Does not align with thumbnail hook words (+5)")

            total += 5

    else:

        checks["Thumbnail Hook Sync"] = (True, "No hook constraints provided (+20)")

        total += 20



    final_score = max(0, min(100, total))



    if verbose:

        print(f"\n   --- Audit for: \"{clean_t}\" (Score: {final_score}/100) ---")

        for k, (ok, note) in checks.items():

            mark = "✓" if ok else "✗"

            print(f"      [{mark}] {k:26}: {note}")



    return final_score, checks





def log_upload_performance(video_id: str, title: str, hook: str, palette: str, layout: str, topic: str):

    """Logs uploaded Short parameters into performance_log.json for pattern learning."""

    record = {

        "video_id": video_id,

        "title": title,

        "hook": hook,

        "palette": palette or "Gold Classic",

        "layout": layout or "Adaptive",

        "post_time": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),

        "post_hour_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).hour,

        "topic": topic or "General"

    }

    history = []

    if PERFORMANCE_LOG_FILE.exists():

        try:

            with open(PERFORMANCE_LOG_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:

                history = json.load(f)

        except Exception:

            history = []



    history = [h for h in history if h.get("video_id") != video_id]

    history.append(record)

    try:

        with open(PERFORMANCE_LOG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:

            json.dump(history, f, indent=2)

    except Exception as e:

        print(f"   [Log Note] Could not write performance_log: {e}")





# --- B. CORE VIRALITY FUNCTIONS ---



def get_my_top_shorts(channel_name: str, max_results: int = 8) -> list[dict]:

    """

    Fetches user's last 30 uploads, filters for true Shorts (<=60s),

    calculates age in days, and ranks by views per day.

    """

    if channel_name in _CHANNEL_STATS_CACHE:

        return _CHANNEL_STATS_CACHE[channel_name]



    youtube = get_authenticated_youtube(channel_name)

    if not youtube:

        return []



    try:

        ch_res = youtube.channels().list(mine=True, part="contentDetails").execute()

        track_yt_quota(1)

        if not ch_res.get("items"):

            return []

        uploads_id = ch_res["items"][0]["contentDetails"]["relatedPlaylists"]["uploads"]



        pl_res = youtube.playlistItems().list(

            playlistId=uploads_id, part="contentDetails", maxResults=30

        ).execute()

        track_yt_quota(1)



        vid_ids = [item["contentDetails"]["videoId"] for item in pl_res.get("items", [])]

        if not vid_ids:

            return []



        v_res = youtube.videos().list(

            id=",".join(vid_ids),

            part="snippet,statistics,contentDetails"

        ).execute()

        track_yt_quota(1)



        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        shorts_list = []



        for v in v_res.get("items", []):

            duration_sec = parse_iso_duration(v.get("contentDetails", {}).get("duration", ""))

            if duration_sec <= 0 or duration_sec > 60:

                continue



            snip = v.get("snippet", {})

            stats = v.get("statistics", {})



            pub_str = snip.get("publishedAt", "")

            try:

                pub_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(pub_str.replace("Z", "+00:00"))

            except Exception:

                pub_dt = now



            age_days = max(0.1, (now - pub_dt).total_seconds() / 86400.0)

            views = int(stats.get("viewCount", 0))

            vpd = views / age_days



            shorts_list.append({

                "id": v["id"],

                "title": snip.get("title", ""),

                "views": views,

                "views_per_day": round(vpd, 1),

                "age_days": round(age_days, 1),

                "likes": int(stats.get("likeCount", 0)),

                "comments": int(stats.get("commentCount", 0)),

                "tags": snip.get("tags", []),

                "published_at": pub_str

            })



        shorts_list.sort(key=lambda x: x["views_per_day"], reverse=True)

        _CHANNEL_STATS_CACHE[channel_name] = shorts_list[:max_results]

        return _CHANNEL_STATS_CACHE[channel_name]

    except Exception as e:

        if "insufficientPermissions" in str(e):

            print("   ⚠️ [YouTube API] Existing token lacks read permissions. Click '+ Add Channel' in the sidebar to re-authenticate.")

        else:

            print(f"   [YouTube API] Note: {e}")

        return []





def scan_competitor_shorts(topic: str, limit: int = 6, channel_name: str = "") -> list[dict]:

    """

    Searches for competitor Shorts published in the last 45 days.

    Ranks by views-per-day velocity. Caches results locally for 12 hours.

    Falls back to yt-dlp if unauthenticated or quota is low.

    """

    clean_topic = topic.strip().lower()

    now_ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()



    # 1. Check 12-hour cache

    cache = {}

    if COMPETITOR_CACHE_FILE.exists():

        try:

            with open(COMPETITOR_CACHE_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:

                cache = json.load(f)

            if clean_topic in cache:

                cached_entry = cache[clean_topic]

                if (now_ts - cached_entry.get("timestamp", 0)) < 43200:

                    return cached_entry.get("items", [])[:limit]

        except Exception:

            cache = {}



    competitors = []

    youtube = get_authenticated_youtube(channel_name) if channel_name else None



    # 2. Query YouTube Data API

    if youtube:

        try:

            published_after = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=45)).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")

            search_res = youtube.search().list(

                q=f"{topic} #shorts",

                type="video",

                videoDuration="short",

                order="viewCount",

                publishedAfter=published_after,

                maxResults=15,

                part="snippet"

            ).execute()

            track_yt_quota(100)



            found_ids = [item["id"]["videoId"] for item in search_res.get("items", []) if "videoId" in item.get("id", {})]



            if found_ids:

                v_res = youtube.videos().list(

                    id=",".join(found_ids),

                    part="snippet,statistics,contentDetails"

                ).execute()

                track_yt_quota(1)



                now_dt = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

                for v in v_res.get("items", []):

                    dur_sec = parse_iso_duration(v.get("contentDetails", {}).get("duration", ""))

                    if dur_sec <= 0 or dur_sec > 60:

                        continue



                    stats = v.get("statistics", {})

                    snip = v.get("snippet", {})

                    pub_str = snip.get("publishedAt", "")

                    try:

                        pub_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(pub_str.replace("Z", "+00:00"))

                    except Exception:

                        pub_dt = now_dt



                    age_days = max(0.1, (now_dt - pub_dt).total_seconds() / 86400.0)

                    views = int(stats.get("viewCount", 0))

                    vpd = views / age_days



                    competitors.append({

                        "title": snip.get("title", ""),

                        "views": views,

                        "views_per_day": round(vpd, 1),

                        "age_days": round(age_days, 1),

                        "url": f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={v['id']}"

                    })

        except Exception as e:

            print(f"   [API Competitor Scan] Note ({e}). Falling back to yt-dlp...")



    # 3. Fallback to yt-dlp

    if not competitors:

        try:

            ydl_opts = {"quiet": True, "extract_flat": True, "skip_download": True, "socket_timeout": 3, "no_warnings": True}

            query = f"ytsearch3:{topic} #shorts"

            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:

                res = ydl.extract_info(query, download=False)

                for entry in res.get("entries", []):

                    if not entry:

                        continue

                    dur = entry.get("duration") or 0

                    if 0 < dur <= 60:

                        v_count = int(entry.get("view_count") or 0)

                        competitors.append({

                            "title": entry.get("title", ""),

                            "views": v_count,

                            "views_per_day": float(v_count),

                            "age_days": 1.0,

                            "url": f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={entry.get('id', '')}"

                        })

        except Exception as e:

            print(f"   [yt-dlp Competitor Scan] Note: {e}")



    competitors.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("views_per_day", 0), reverse=True)

    clean_results = competitors[:limit]



    # Cache for 12 hours

    if clean_results:
