        try:

            img_req = urllib.request.Request(candidate_u, headers=headers)

            with urllib.request.urlopen(img_req, timeout=12) as i_resp:

                data = i_resp.read()

                if len(data) > 5000:

                    final_img.write_bytes(data)

                    break

        except Exception:

            continue



    if final_img.exists() and final_img.stat().st_size > 5000:

        print(f"✅ [Pinterest Image] Saved to: Pinterest/Images/{final_img.name}")

        if sync_to_studio:

            shutil.copy(final_img, BG_DIR / final_img.name)

            shutil.copy(final_img, THUMB_DIR / final_img.name)

            print(f"🎨 Added to Studio Backgrounds & Thumbnails: {final_img.name}")

        return final_img



    print("❌ Failed to download image bytes from Pinterest.")

    return None



def run_cli_pinterest_downloader():

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)

    print("📌 PINTEREST IMAGE & VIDEO DOWNLOADER (AUTO-SORTED)")

    print(f"📂 Images Folder: {PIN_IMG_DIR.resolve()}")

    print(f"📂 Videos Folder: {PIN_VID_DIR.resolve()}")

    print("=" * 65)

    pin_url = input("Paste Pinterest Pin URL (pin.it or pinterest.com/pin/...): ").strip()

    if pin_url:

        download_pinterest_media(pin_url, sync_to_studio=True)





def download_audio_from_url():

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)

    print("🎵 DOWNLOAD HIGH-QUALITY MP3 AUDIO FROM YOUTUBE")

    print("=" * 60)

    url = input("Paste YouTube Video URL: ").strip()

    if not url:

        return



    print("\n📥 Downloading and extracting audio (MP3 192kbps)...")

    ydl_opts = {

        'format': 'bestaudio/best',

        'outtmpl': str(DOWNLOADS_DIR / '%(title)s.%(ext)s'),

        'postprocessors': [{'key': 'FFmpegExtractAudio', 'preferredcodec': 'mp3', 'preferredquality': '192'}],

        'overwrites': True,

        'quiet': False,

    }

    try:

        with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:

            info = ydl.extract_info(url, download=True)

            print(f"\n✅ Audio Downloaded: '{info.get('title')}.mp3'")

            print(f"📁 Saved to: {DOWNLOADS_DIR.resolve()}")

    except Exception as e:

        print(f"❌ Download failed: {e}")

def run_cli_bible(active_channel: str):

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)

    print("✝️ GENERATE FACELESS BIBLE VERSE & PRAYER SHORT")

    print("=" * 60)

    print("Select a Topic:")

    for k, v in CURATED_BIBLE_VAULT.items():

        print(f"  [{k}] {v['topic']}")

    print("  [8] Type a Custom Topic")



    choice = input("\nChoose (1-8): ").strip()

    if choice in CURATED_BIBLE_VAULT:

        topic = CURATED_BIBLE_VAULT[choice]["topic"]

    elif choice == "8":

        topic = input("Enter custom topic: ").strip()

    else:

        topic = "Debt & Financial Freedom"



    print(f"\n⚡ [1/3] Loading scripture & prayer for '{topic}'...")

    script = generate_bible_content(topic)

    print(f"   📖 Verse: {script.verse_citation} - \"{script.verse_text}\"")

    print(f"   🙏 Prayer: \"{script.prayer}\"")



    print("\n🎙️ [2/3] Generating solemn voiceover & word timings...")

    from voice_studio import build_spoken_voice_script

    voice_script = build_spoken_voice_script(script)

    temp_voice = BASE_DIR / "temp_voice.mp3"

    generate_voiceover(voice_script, temp_voice)



    print("\n⚡ [3/3] Rendering video (10s Short_Main video ➔ Jesus_Lord image)...")

    clean_name = clean_filename(f"Bible_{script.topic}_{script.verse_citation}")

    seq_dir = get_next_sequence_folder(clean_name)

    out_video = seq_dir / f"{clean_name}.mp4"

    out_thumb = seq_dir / f"{clean_name}_thumb.jpg"



    c_intro, c_bg, c_thumb = choose_media_in_cli(script.topic)

    success = render_faceless_bible_video(script, temp_voice, out_video, out_thumb, custom_intro=c_intro, custom_bg=c_bg, custom_thumb=c_thumb)

    if temp_voice.exists():

        temp_voice.unlink()



    if success:

        desc = f"""If you are dealing with {script.topic}, let this powerful scripture speak to your spirit.



📖 Scripture: {script.verse_citation}

"{script.verse_text}"



💡 Reflection:

{script.meaning}



🙏 Prayer:

{script.prayer}



Type "AMEN" in the comments if you receive this word today! 🙏



---

📖 Recommended Study Bible & Prayer Journal (Linked in channel bio):

Grab your copy to deepen your walk with God.



{' '.join(script.hashtags)} #Bible #Jesus #Prayer #Shorts #Faith



---

Disclaimer: As an Amazon Associate, I earn from qualifying purchases at no extra cost to you."""



        opt_title, opt_desc, opt_tags = optimize_with_virality_engine(script.topic, script.title, desc, script.hashtags + ["Bible", "Jesus", "Prayer"], active_channel)

        save_clip_metadata(out_video, opt_title, opt_desc, opt_tags)

        print("\n" + "=" * 60)

        print(f"✅ BIBLE SHORT READY: {out_video.name}")

        print(f"🖼️ THUMBNAIL READY:   {out_thumb.name}")

        print("=" * 60)



        target_ch = choose_upload_channel(active_channel)

        if target_ch:

            upload_video_to_youtube(target_ch, out_video)



def run_cli_podcast_scout(active_channel: str):

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)

    print("🎙️ AUTO-SCOUT VIRAL PODCAST (1M+ VIEWS)")

    print("=" * 60)

    print("Select Niche Category:")

    for k, v in CATEGORIES.items():

        print(f"  [{k}] {v['name']}")



    cat_choice = input("\nChoose Category (1-6): ").strip()

    cat_config = CATEGORIES.get(cat_choice, CATEGORIES["1"])



    target = find_viral_video(1_000_000, cat_config["queries"])

    if not target:

        print("⚠️ No new videos found with 1M+ views right now.")

        return



    print(f"\n🎯 Target Found: '{target['title']}' ({target['views']:,} views)")

    transcript = get_transcript(target["id"])

    if transcript:

        clips = analyze_transcript(transcript, cat_config["name"])

    else:

        clips = analyze_audio_fallback(target["url"], cat_config["name"])



    if not clips:

        print("⚠️ No suitable clip moments detected.")

        return



    for clip in clips:

        start_s = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.start_time)

        end_s = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.end_time)

        temp_slice = BASE_DIR / "temp_slice.mp4"



        try:

            download_slice(target["url"], start_s, end_s, temp_slice)

            seq_name = f"{clean_filename(clip.book_title)}_{clean_filename(clip.title)}"

            seq_dir = get_next_sequence_folder(seq_name)

            out_file = seq_dir / f"{seq_name}.mp4"

            if render_podcast_short(temp_slice, clip, out_file, cat_config["badge_tag"]):

                all_tags = list(set(clip.hashtags + cat_config["hashtags"]))

                desc = f"""{clip.hook_caption}\n\n📖 Featured Book: {clip.book_title} ({clip.book_author})\n👉 Grab your copy via the link in our channel bio!\n\n{' '.join(all_tags)}\n\n---\nDisclaimer: As an Amazon Associate, I earn from qualifying purchases at no extra cost to you."""

                opt_title, opt_desc, opt_tags = optimize_with_virality_engine(cat_config["name"], clip.title, desc, all_tags, active_channel)

                save_clip_metadata(out_file, opt_title, opt_desc, opt_tags)



                print(f"\n✅ SHORT CREATED: {out_file.name}")

                ask = input(f"🚀 Upload this short to '{active_channel}' now? (y/n): ").strip().lower()

                if ask == "y":

                    upload_video_to_youtube(active_channel, out_file)

        finally:

            if temp_slice.exists():

                temp_slice.unlink()



    history = set()

    if HISTORY_FILE.exists():

        try:

            with open(HISTORY_FILE, "r") as f:

                history = set(json.load(f))

        except Exception:

            pass

    history.add(target["id"])

    with open(HISTORY_FILE, "w") as f:

        json.dump(list(history), f, indent=2)



def run_cli_podcast_custom(active_channel: str):

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)

    print("🔗 CLIP A CUSTOM YOUTUBE VIDEO URL")

    print("=" * 60)

    url = input("Paste YouTube Video URL: ").strip()

    if not url:

        return



    m = re.search(r'(?:v=|\/)([0-9A-Za-z_-]{11})', url)

    if not m:

        print("❌ Invalid YouTube URL.")

        return



    v_id = m.group(1)



    print("\nHow would you like to clip this video?")

    print("  [1] Auto-detect best viral moment with AI (Even if subtitles are disabled!)")

    print("  [2] Enter my own exact timestamps (e.g. 00:05 to 00:45)")

    mode = input("Choose (1/2): ").strip()



    clips = []

    if mode == "2":

        start_t = input("Enter Start Time (e.g. 00:10): ").strip()

        end_t = input("Enter End Time   (e.g. 00:50): ").strip()

        title_t = input("Enter Title for this clip: ").strip() or "Inspiring Moment"

        clips.append(BookClip(

            title=title_t,

            start_time=start_t,

            end_time=end_t,

            book_title="Sacred Inspiration",

            book_author="Daily Wisdom",

            hook_caption="Watch this powerful moment.",

            hashtags=["#Shorts", "#Inspiration", "#Worship", "#Faith"]

        ))

    else:

        transcript = get_transcript(v_id)

        if transcript:

            clips = analyze_transcript(transcript, "Inspiration & Wisdom")

        else:

            clips = analyze_audio_fallback(url, "Inspiration & Wisdom")



    if not clips:

        print("⚠️ Could not detect clips automatically.")

        return



    for clip in clips:

        start_s = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.start_time)

        end_s = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.end_time)

        temp_slice = BASE_DIR / "temp_slice.mp4"



        try:

            download_slice(url, start_s, end_s, temp_slice)

            seq_name = f"{clean_filename(clip.book_title)}_{clean_filename(clip.title)}"

            seq_dir = get_next_sequence_folder(seq_name)

            out_file = seq_dir / f"{seq_name}.mp4"

            if render_podcast_short(temp_slice, clip, out_file, "SPECIAL HIGHLIGHT"):

                desc = f"""{clip.hook_caption}\n\n👉 Follow our channel for daily inspiration!\n\n{' '.join(clip.hashtags)}\n\n---\nDisclaimer: As an Amazon Associate, I earn from qualifying purchases at no extra cost to you."""

                opt_title, opt_desc, opt_tags = optimize_with_virality_engine(clip.title, clip.title, desc, clip.hashtags, active_channel)

                save_clip_metadata(out_file, opt_title, opt_desc, opt_tags)



                print(f"\n✅ SHORT CREATED: {out_file.name}")

                ask = input(f"🚀 Upload this short to '{active_channel}' now? (y/n): ").strip().lower()

                if ask == "y":

                    upload_video_to_youtube(active_channel, out_file)

        finally:

            if temp_slice.exists():

                temp_slice.unlink()



def run_cli_bulk_uploader(active_channel: str):

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)

    print("🚀 BATCH UPLOAD SAVED SHORTS TO YOUTUBE")

    print("=" * 60)



    mp4s = sorted(list(CLIPS_DIR.rglob("*.mp4")), key=os.path.getmtime, reverse=True)

    unuploaded = []



    for vid in mp4s:

        meta = load_clip_metadata(vid)

        if not meta.get("youtube_id"):

            unuploaded.append(vid)



    if not unuploaded:

        print("🎉 All shorts in 'clips/' folder are already uploaded!")

        return



    print(f"Found {len(unuploaded)} unuploaded Short(s):")

    for i, v in enumerate(unuploaded):

        print(f"  [{i+1}] {v.name}")



    target_ch = choose_upload_channel(active_channel)

    if target_ch:

        for v in unuploaded:

            upload_video_to_youtube(target_ch, v)

            time.sleep(2)



def run_cli_channel_switcher(current_active: str) -> str:

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)

    print("📺 YOUTUBE CHANNEL MANAGER")

    print("=" * 60)

    channels = load_channels()



    print("Connected Channels:")

    c_list = list(channels.keys())

    for i, ch in enumerate(c_list):

        mark = " (Active)" if ch == current_active else ""

        print(f"  [{i+1}] {ch}{mark}")



    print("  [+] Connect New YouTube Channel")

    print("  [b] Back")



    choice = input("\nChoose option: ").strip()

    if choice == "+":

        new_ch = connect_new_channel()

        return new_ch if new_ch else current_active

    elif choice.isdigit() and 1 <= int(choice) <= len(c_list):

        selected = c_list[int(choice) - 1]

        print(f"✅ Switched active channel to: '{selected}'")

        return selected



    return current_active



# --- 1-CLICK GIT & GITHUB AUTO-BACKUP ---

def save_everything_to_git() -> tuple[bool, str]:

    """Stages all safe project files, commits with a timestamp, and pushes to GitHub."""

    from datetime import datetime

    print("\n☁️ [Git Backup] Saving & pushing project to GitHub (leonshipley/DailyWisdom-Studio)...")

    try:

        # Ensure secrets stay untracked

        subprocess.run(

            ["git", "rm", "-r", "--cached", ".env", "client_secret*.json", "youtube_token.pickle", "channels/", "--ignore-unmatch"],

            cwd=str(BASE_DIR), stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL

        )

        subprocess.run(["git", "add", "."], cwd=str(BASE_DIR), check=True)



        status = subprocess.run(["git", "status", "--porcelain"], cwd=str(BASE_DIR), capture_output=True, text=True)

        if status.stdout.strip():

            ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

            subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-m", f"Studio Auto-Save: {ts}"], cwd=str(BASE_DIR), check=True)



        push_res = subprocess.run(["git", "push", "-u", "origin", "main"], cwd=str(BASE_DIR), capture_output=True, text=True)

        if push_res.returncode == 0:

            msg = "✅ Successfully saved & pushed everything to GitHub (leonshipley/DailyWisdom-Studio)!"

            print(msg)

            return True, msg

        else:

            err = push_res.stderr.strip() or push_res.stdout.strip()

            msg = f"⚠️ Committed locally, but push returned: {err}"

            print(msg)

            return False, msg

    except Exception as e:

        msg = f"❌ Git save error: {e}"

        print(msg)

        return False, msg





# --- 9. MAIN INTERACTIVE BACKEND MENU ---

def run_cli_voice_studio():

    """Interactive Terminal Voice Studio: Browse 4 accents, listen to any voice sample, and select."""

    from voice_studio import VOICE_CATALOG, DEFAULT_SAMPLE_TEXTS, load_active_voice, save_active_voice, synthesize_preview_sample, play_audio_on_windows

    cur = load_active_voice()

    accents = list(VOICE_CATALOG.keys())



    while True:

        print("\n" + "=" * 68)

        print("🎙️ PROFESSIONAL VOICE & ACCENT STUDIO (28 NEURAL VOICES)")

        print(f"🔊 Currently Active Voice: {cur.get('name')} ({cur.get('accent')})")

        print("=" * 68)

        for idx, acc in enumerate(accents, start=1):

            print(f"  [{idx}] {acc} ({len(VOICE_CATALOG[acc])} Voices)")

        print("  [b] Back to Main Menu")

        print("=" * 68)



        c_acc = input("Select Accent Category (1-4 or b): ").strip().lower()

        if c_acc in ("b", "back", "0", ""):

            break

        if not (c_acc.isdigit() and 1 <= int(c_acc) <= len(accents)):

            continue



        chosen_accent = accents[int(c_acc) - 1]

        v_list = VOICE_CATALOG[chosen_accent]

        sample_txt = DEFAULT_SAMPLE_TEXTS[chosen_accent]



        while True:

            print("\n" + "-" * 68)

            print(f"🎧 VOICES IN: {chosen_accent}")

            print("-" * 68)

            for v_i, v_obj in enumerate(v_list, start=1):

                mark = " ⭐ [ACTIVE]" if v_obj["id"] == cur.get("id") else ""

                print(f"  [{v_i}] {v_obj['name']} ({v_obj['gender']}){mark}")

                print(f"      Style: {v_obj['style']} | Rate: {v_obj['rate']} | Pitch: {v_obj['pitch']}")

            print("-" * 68)

            print("💡 Commands:")

            print(f"  • Type a number (1-{len(v_list)}) to SELECT that voice as Active")

            print(f"  • Type 'p1', 'p2', etc. to LISTEN / AUDITION that voice right now!")

            print("  • Type 'b' to go back to Accent Categories")



            cmd_in = input("\nYour choice (e.g. 'p1' to listen, '1' to select, 'b' back): ").strip().lower()

            if cmd_in in ("b", "back", ""):

                break

            if cmd_in.startswith("p") and cmd_in[1:].isdigit():

                p_idx = int(cmd_in[1:]) - 1

                if 0 <= p_idx < len(v_list):

                    target_v = v_list[p_idx]

                    print(f"🎧 Generating & playing sample for: {target_v['name']}...")

                    mp3_f = synthesize_preview_sample(target_v["voice"], target_v["rate"], target_v["pitch"], sample_txt)

                    play_audio_on_windows(mp3_f)

            elif cmd_in.isdigit() and 1 <= int(cmd_in) <= len(v_list):

                sel_v = dict(v_list[int(cmd_in) - 1])

                sel_v["accent"] = chosen_accent

                save_active_voice(sel_v)

                cur = sel_v

                print(f"\n✅ LOCKED IN ACTIVE VOICE: {sel_v['name']} ({chosen_accent})!")

                break

            break

        else:

            print("⚠️ Invalid choice. Enter 0 to 12.")



if __name__ == "__main__":

    main()


==========================================
=== FILE: pro_design_addon.py ===

import re
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont, ImageFilter, ImageEnhance

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

def clean_ascii(text: str) -> str:
    if not text:
        return ""
    t = (text.replace("’", "'").replace("‘", "'")
             .replace("“", '"').replace("”", '"')
             .replace("—", " - ").replace("…", "..."))
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", re.sub(r"[^\x20-\x7E]", "", t)).strip()

def get_black_font(size: int = 60) -> ImageFont.FreeTypeFont:
    """Finds Arial Black or Impact on Windows for ultra-thick thumbnail typography."""
    candidates = [
        Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/ariblk.ttf"),    # Arial Black (Thickest & cleanest)
        Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/impact.ttf"),    # Impact
        Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeuib.ttf"),  # Segoe UI Bold
        Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/arialbd.ttf"),   # Arial Bold
    ]
    for p in candidates:
        if p.exists():
            try:
                return ImageFont.truetype(str(p), max(12, int(size)))
            except Exception:
                continue
    return ImageFont.load_default()

def get_bold_font(size: int = 40) -> ImageFont.FreeTypeFont:
    candidates = [
        Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeuib.ttf"),
        Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/arialbd.ttf"),
        Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf")
    ]
    for p in candidates:
        if p.exists():
            try:
                return ImageFont.truetype(str(p), max(12, int(size)))
            except Exception:
                continue
    return ImageFont.load_default()

def split_hook(hook_str: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """Splits headline so Line 1 gets 3D Gold and Line 2 gets White."""
    words = clean_ascii(hook_str).upper().strip().split()
    if not words:
        return ("BREAK", "THE CHAINS")
    if len(words) == 1:
        return (words[0], "")
    if len(words) == 2:
        return (words[0], words[1])
    if len(words) == 3:
        return (words[0], f"{words[1]} {words[2]}")
    # 4+ words: e.g. DON'T / GIVE UP YET, BREAK / EVERY DEBT, PEACE / IN THE STORM
    if words[0] in ("BREAK", "DON'T", "DONT", "STOP", "NEVER", "GOD", "PRAY", "PEACE", "HEAL", "HOLD", "NO"):
        return (words[0], " ".join(words[1:]))
    mid = len(words) // 2
    return (" ".join(words[:mid]), " ".join(words[mid:]))

def render_3d_gold_text(canvas: Image.Image, text: str, font: ImageFont.FreeTypeFont, 
                        cx: int, cy: int, stroke_w: int = 14):
    """Draws true 3D chiseled metallic gold gradient text with multi-layer drop shadows."""
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), text, font=font, stroke_width=stroke_w)
    tw, th = bbox[2] - bbox[0], bbox[3] - bbox[1]
    tx = cx - tw // 2 - bbox[0]
    ty = cy - th // 2 - bbox[1]

    # 1. Multi-layer Dark 3D Drop Shadow
    for off in range(12, 3, -1):
        alpha = int(180 * (off / 12.0))
        draw.text((tx + off, ty + off + 2), text, font=font, 
                  fill=(0, 0, 0, alpha), stroke_width=stroke_w + 4, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, alpha))

    # 2. Thick Pitch-Black Outline
    draw.text((tx, ty), text, font=font, fill=(0, 0, 0, 255), 
              stroke_width=stroke_w, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 255))

    # 3. Create Metallic Gold Gradient (Light Yellow -> Rich Amber Gold -> Deep Bronze)
    grad_pad = 40
    gw, gh = tw + grad_pad * 2, th + grad_pad * 2
    grad_img = Image.new("RGBA", (gw, gh), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    g_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(grad_img)

    c_top = (255, 250, 185)   # Light glowing gold
    c_mid = (235, 185, 55)    # Rich metallic gold
    c_bot = (165, 105, 10)    # Deep bronze gold

    for y in range(gh):
        rat = y / max(1, gh - 1)
        if rat < 0.45:
            sr = rat / 0.45
            cr = int(c_top[0] * (1 - sr) + c_mid[0] * sr)
            cg = int(c_top[1] * (1 - sr) + c_mid[1] * sr)
            cb = int(c_top[2] * (1 - sr) + c_mid[2] * sr)
        else:
            sr = (rat - 0.45) / 0.55
            cr = int(c_mid[0] * (1 - sr) + c_bot[0] * sr)
            cg = int(c_mid[1] * (1 - sr) + c_bot[1] * sr)
            cb = int(c_mid[2] * (1 - sr) + c_bot[2] * sr)
        g_draw.line([(0, y), (gw, y)], fill=(cr, cg, cb, 255))

    # 4. Text Mask to cut out the gold gradient
    mask_img = Image.new("L", (gw, gh), 0)
    m_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(mask_img)
    m_draw.text((grad_pad - bbox[0], grad_pad - bbox[1]), text, font=font, fill=255)

    canvas.paste(grad_img, (tx - grad_pad, ty - grad_pad), mask=mask_img)

def render_pro_thumbnails(bg_image_path: Path, script: Any, thumb_path: Path, palette_name: str = "Gold Classic") -> Dict[str, Path]:
    """Creates thumbnails that match the reference design."""
    raw_img = Image.open(bg_image_path).convert("RGB")

    def _build(w: int, h: int, is_vertical: bool = True) -> Image.Image:
        # Aspect fill and center crop
        ir = raw_img.width / raw_img.height
        tr = w / h
        nw, nh = (int(h * ir), h) if ir > tr else (w, int(w / ir))
        im = raw_img.resize((nw, nh), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        im = im.crop(((nw - w) // 2, (nh - h) // 2, (nw - w) // 2 + w, (nh - h) // 2 + h))

        # Brightness & contrast boost for cinematic punch
        im = ImageEnhance.Contrast(im).enhance(1.15)
        im = ImageEnhance.Color(im).enhance(1.12).convert("RGBA")

        # HEAVY CINEMATIC DARK VIGNETTE (Crucial so text and stickers NEVER wash out!)
        vignette = Image.new("RGBA", (w, h), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        vd = ImageDraw.Draw(vignette)
        top_h = int(h * (0.24 if is_vertical else 0.30))
        bot_start = int(h * (0.46 if is_vertical else 0.42))

        for y in range(h):
            if y <= top_h:
                a = int(225 * ((1.0 - y / max(1, top_h)) ** 1.3))
            elif y >= bot_start:
                # Heavy dark shadow at the bottom for maximum text contrast
                a = int(245 * (((y - bot_start) / max(1, h - bot_start)) ** 1.05))
            else:
                a = 25
            vd.line([(0, y), (w, y)], fill=(5, 8, 16, min(245, a)))
        im = Image.alpha_composite(im, vignette)
        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(im)

        # 1. DOUBLE METALLIC GOLD BORDER WITH CORNER DIAMONDS
        gold_border = (225, 185, 65)
        ins1 = 36 if is_vertical else 20
        ins2 = ins1 + 14
        r1 = 44 if is_vertical else 24
        r2 = r1 - 10

        draw.rounded_rectangle([ins1, ins1, w - ins1, h - ins1], radius=r1, outline=gold_border, width=4)
        draw.rounded_rectangle([ins2, ins2, w - ins2, h - ins2], radius=r2, outline=(gold_border[0], gold_border[1], gold_border[2], 140), width=2)

        # Corner diamond accents
        for cx, cy in [(ins1 + 6, ins1 + 6), (w - ins1 - 6, ins1 + 6), (ins1 + 6, h - ins1 - 6), (w - ins1 - 6, h - ins1 - 6)]:
            draw.polygon([(cx, cy - 6), (cx + 6, cy), (cx, cy + 6), (cx - 6, cy)], fill=gold_border)

        # 2. TOP PILL BADGE (e.g. DEBT & FINANCIAL FREEDOM)
        topic_text = clean_ascii(getattr(script, "topic", "DAILY DEVOTIONAL")).upper()
        f_badge_sz = 34 if len(topic_text) <= 25 else max(24, int(760 / max(1, len(topic_text) * 0.62)))
        f_badge = get_black_font(f_badge_sz if is_vertical else 22)
        bb = draw.textbbox((0, 0), topic_text, font=f_badge)
        bw, bh = (bb[2] - bb[0]) + 70, (bb[3] - bb[1]) + 30
        bx = (w - bw) // 2
        by = 130 if is_vertical else 38

        # Shadow + dark pill + gold outline
        draw.rounded_rectangle([bx + 3, by + 5, bx + bw + 3, by + bh + 5], radius=bh // 2, fill=(0, 0, 0, 190))
        draw.rounded_rectangle([bx, by, bx + bw, by + bh], radius=bh // 2, fill=(9, 14, 25, 245), outline=gold_border, width=3)
        draw.text(((w - (bb[2] - bb[0])) // 2 - bb[0], by + (bh - (bb[3] - bb[1])) // 2 - bb[1]), 
                  topic_text, font=f_badge, fill=(255, 245, 185))

        # 3. LOWER-THIRD HOOK
        # Line 1: MASSIVE 3D GOLD GRADIENT
        # Line 2: MASSIVE PURE WHITE WITH DEEP BLACK STROKE
        raw_hook = getattr(script, "thumbnail_hook", "") or getattr(script, "title", "BREAK THE CHAINS")
        line1, line2 = split_hook(raw_hook)

        hook_y_center = int(h * 0.64) if is_vertical else int(h * 0.38)

        # Line 1 size (Arial Black / Impact)
        f_sz1 = 155 if len(line1) <= 6 else max(80, int(860 / (len(line1) * 0.58)))
        if not is_vertical: f_sz1 = int(f_sz1 * 0.60)
        f_line1 = get_black_font(f_sz1)
        render_3d_gold_text(im, line1, f_line1, w // 2, hook_y_center, stroke_w=16 if is_vertical else 9)

        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(im)

        # Line 2 (Pure White)
        if line2:
            f_sz2 = 115 if len(line2) <= 11 else max(62, int(860 / (len(line2) * 0.58)))
            if not is_vertical: f_sz2 = int(f_sz2 * 0.60)
            f_line2 = get_black_font(f_sz2)
            l2_bb = draw.textbbox((0, 0), line2, font=f_line2, stroke_width=14 if is_vertical else 8)
            l2_w, l2_h = l2_bb[2] - l2_bb[0], l2_bb[3] - l2_bb[1]
            l2_x = (w - l2_w) // 2 - l2_bb[0]
            l2_y = hook_y_center + (f_sz1 // 2) + 20 - l2_bb[1]

            # Deep 3D drop shadow
            for off in range(8, 2, -1):
                draw.text((l2_x + off, l2_y + off + 3), line2, font=f_line2, 
                          fill=(0, 0, 0, 180), stroke_width=16, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 180))
            # Text + black stroke
            draw.text((l2_x, l2_y), line2, font=f_line2, fill=(255, 255, 255), 
                      stroke_width=14 if is_vertical else 8, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 255))
            cur_bot_y = l2_y + l2_h + 18
        else:
            cur_bot_y = hook_y_center + (f_sz1 // 2) + 18

        # 4. SCRIPTURE REFERENCE (In Gold with Black Stroke)
        v_ref = clean_ascii(getattr(script, "verse_citation", "PROVERBS 10:22")).upper()
        f_ref_sz = 54 if is_vertical else 34
        f_ref = get_black_font(f_ref_sz)
        rb = draw.textbbox((0, 0), v_ref, font=f_ref, stroke_width=6)
        rw = rb[2] - rb[0]
        ry = min(h - (390 if is_vertical else 180), cur_bot_y + 14)

        # Shadow + text
        draw.text(((w - rw) // 2 - rb[0] + 4, ry - rb[1] + 4), v_ref, font=f_ref, fill=(0, 0, 0, 220), stroke_width=8, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 220))
        draw.text(((w - rw) // 2 - rb[0], ry - rb[1]), v_ref, font=f_ref, fill=(235, 185, 55), stroke_width=5, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 255))

        # 5. 3D GLOSSY METALLIC GOLD 'PRAY THIS NOW' BUTTON
        btn_w, btn_h = (440, 82) if is_vertical else (280, 52)
        bx1 = (w - btn_w) // 2
        by1 = min(h - (280 if is_vertical else 115), ry + 78)

        # Drop shadow
        draw.rounded_rectangle([bx1 + 4, by1 + 6, bx1 + btn_w + 4, by1 + btn_h + 6], radius=btn_h // 2, fill=(0, 0, 0, 200))

        # Gold gradient pill
        btn_img = Image.new("RGBA", (btn_w, btn_h), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        b_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(btn_img)
        for y_i in range(btn_h):
            r_i = y_i / max(1, btn_h - 1)
            cr = int(255 * (1 - r_i) + 215 * r_i)
            cg = int(240 * (1 - r_i) + 165 * r_i)
            cb = int(170 * (1 - r_i) + 40 * r_i)
            b_draw.line([(0, y_i), (btn_w, y_i)], fill=(cr, cg, cb, 255))

        btn_mask = Image.new("L", (btn_w, btn_h), 0)
        ImageDraw.Draw(btn_mask).rounded_rectangle([0, 0, btn_w, btn_h], radius=btn_h // 2, fill=255)
        im.paste(btn_img, (bx1, by1), mask=btn_mask)

        # Outer white/gold highlight border + Black text
        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
        draw.rounded_rectangle([bx1, by1, bx1 + btn_w, by1 + btn_h], radius=btn_h // 2, outline=(255, 255, 255, 220), width=3)
        cta_txt = "PRAY THIS NOW"
        f_cta = get_black_font(42 if is_vertical else 26)
        cb = draw.textbbox((0, 0), cta_txt, font=f_cta)
        cw, ch = cb[2] - cb[0], cb[3] - cb[1]
        draw.text((bx1 + (btn_w - cw) // 2 - cb[0], by1 + (btn_h - ch) // 2 - cb[1]), cta_txt, font=f_cta, fill=(15, 20, 28, 255))

        # 6. BOTTOM FLOATING ENGAGEMENT DOCK (3 STICKERS)
        if is_vertical:
            dock_y = h - 145
            f_stk = get_bold_font(21)

            # Sticker 1: Red SUBSCRIBE Pill
            s1_x, s1_w, s1_h = 75, 230, 48
            draw.rounded_rectangle([s1_x + 2, dock_y + 4, s1_x + s1_w + 2, dock_y + s1_h + 4], radius=s1_h // 2, fill=(0, 0, 0, 180))
            draw.rounded_rectangle([s1_x, dock_y, s1_x + s1_w, dock_y + s1_h], radius=s1_h // 2, fill=(220, 38, 38))
            draw.text((s1_x + 26, dock_y + 12), "SUBSCRIBE", font=f_stk, fill=(255, 255, 255))
            # Bell icon shape
            draw.polygon([(s1_x + 185, dock_y + 16), (s1_x + 195, dock_y + 28), (s1_x + 175, dock_y + 28)], fill=(255, 255, 255))
            draw.ellipse([s1_x + 182, dock_y + 28, s1_x + 188, dock_y + 34], fill=(255, 255, 255))

            # Sticker 2: LIKE IF YOU BELIEVE
            s2_x = s1_x + s1_w + 32
            # Gold thumb shape
            draw.rounded_rectangle([s2_x, dock_y + 16, s2_x + 10, dock_y + 32], radius=3, fill=(235, 185, 55))
            draw.polygon([(s2_x + 10, dock_y + 22), (s2_x + 24, dock_y + 15), (s2_x + 24, dock_y + 32), (s2_x + 10, dock_y + 32)], fill=(235, 185, 55))
            draw.text((s2_x + 32, dock_y + 12), "LIKE IF YOU BELIEVE", font=f_stk, fill=(255, 255, 255), stroke_width=3, stroke_fill=(0, 0, 0, 220))

            # Sticker 3: COMMENT AMEN Glass Pill
            s3_w, s3_h = 215, 48
            s3_x = w - s3_w - 75
            draw.rounded_rectangle([s3_x + 2, dock_y + 4, s3_x + s3_w + 2, dock_y + s3_h + 4], radius=s3_h // 2, fill=(0, 0, 0, 180))
            draw.rounded_rectangle([s3_x, dock_y, s3_x + s3_w, dock_y + s3_h], radius=s3_h // 2, fill=(9, 14, 25, 240), outline=gold_border, width=2)
            draw.text((s3_x + 25, dock_y + 12), "COMMENT AMEN", font=f_stk, fill=(255, 240, 175))

        return im.convert("RGB")

    # Generate 1080x1920 Master Vertical Thumbnail
    img_9x16 = _build(1080, 1920, is_vertical=True)
    img_9x16.save(str(thumb_path), "JPEG", quality=96)
    
    out_dir = thumb_path.parent
    try:
        plain_path = out_dir / 'raw_clean_background.jpg'
        raw_img.save(str(plain_path), 'JPEG', quality=98, subsampling=0)
        (out_dir / 'ai_thumbnail_prompt.txt').write_text(
            build_ai_thumbnail_prompt(
                getattr(script, 'topic', 'FAITH'),
                getattr(script, 'verse_citation', 'PSALM 34:18'),
                getattr(script, 'thumbnail_hook', '') or getattr(script, 'title', 'DON\'T GIVE UP YET')
            ),
            encoding='utf-8'
        )
    except Exception:
        pass
    p_png_9x16 = out_dir / "thumbnail_9x16.png"
    img_9x16.save(str(p_png_9x16), "PNG")

    # Generate 1280x720 Landscape Cover
    img_16x9 = _build(1280, 720, is_vertical=False)
    p_png_16x9 = out_dir / "thumbnail_16x9.png"
    img_16x9.save(str(p_png_16x9), "PNG")

    # Generate 3 Hook Variant Covers
    hook_variants = [
        getattr(script, "thumbnail_hook", "BREAK THE CHAINS"),
        f"GOD'S PROMISE FOR {clean_ascii(getattr(script, 'topic', 'YOU')).upper()[:16]}",
        f"HOLD ONTO {clean_ascii(getattr(script, 'verse_citation', 'THIS VERSE')).upper()}"
    ]
    for v_i, v_text in enumerate(hook_variants, start=1):
        orig_hk = getattr(script, "thumbnail_hook", "")
        script.thumbnail_hook = v_text
        var_im = _build(1080, 1920, is_vertical=True)
        var_im.save(str(out_dir / f"thumbnail_9x16_variant_{v_i}.png"), "PNG")
        script.thumbnail_hook = orig_hk

    return {"jpg": thumb_path, "9x16": p_png_9x16, "16x9": p_png_16x9}


def build_ai_thumbnail_prompt(topic: str, verse_ref: str, hook_text: str, scene_hint: str = "", is_vertical: bool = True) -> str:
    """
    Generates a topic-analyzed, ready-to-copy Master Thumbnail Prompt for external AI tools
    (Google AI Studio / Nano Banana 2.1 / Flow) matching the exact 3D Gold + 3 Stickers layout.
    """
    clean_t = clean_ascii(topic or "GOD'S PROMISE").upper()
    clean_v = clean_ascii(verse_ref or "PSALM 34:18").upper()
    line1, line2 = split_hook(hook_text or "DON'T GIVE UP YET")
    if not line2:
        line2 = "TRUST IN HIM"

    t_low = clean_t.lower()
    if any(w in t_low for w in ["debt", "money", "finan", "provid", "lack"]):
        scene_desc = "Jesus in a cream-white robe with a deep crimson sash, standing on a sunlit hillside with outstretched compassionate hands as warm golden heavenly light beams descend through parting clouds, symbolizing provision and freedom."
    elif any(w in t_low for w in ["anxiet", "storm", "fear", "worry", "panic", "overthink", "sleep", "night", "2 am", "3 am"]):
        scene_desc = "Jesus standing peacefully on a wooden boat calming a dramatic twilight sea, one hand raised gently as golden sunrise light breaks through dark storm clouds over calm water."
    elif any(w in t_low for w in ["heal", "sick", "pain", "body", "strength", "weary", "quit"]):
        scene_desc = "Jesus in a cream-white robe with a deep red sash on a grassy hillside at golden hour, one hand raised in blessing, a soft halo of sunlight behind His head, ordinary people sitting on the hill listening in peace."
    elif any(w in t_low for w in ["broken", "heart", "grief", "tears", "alone", "lonely", "forgiv", "marriag", "family"]):
        scene_desc = "Jesus with a warm, deeply compassionate expression in golden-hour light, gently comforting a weary traveler on an ancient stone path surrounded by olive trees at sunrise."
    else:
        scene_desc = scene_hint or "Jesus in a cream-white robe with a deep red sash standing on a grassy hillside at golden hour sunrise, one hand raised and open with a gentle compassionate expression and volumetric god rays."

    ar_txt = "vertical 9:16 (1080x1920)" if is_vertical else "horizontal 16:9 (1920x1080)"

    return (
        f"Create a premium YouTube Shorts thumbnail, {ar_txt}, for a Christian devotional video. "
        f"It must look like a top-tier professional Christian channel: photorealistic, cinematic, emotional.\n\n"
        f"SCENE (photorealistic, NOT cartoon, NOT painting-looking):\n"
        f"[{scene_desc}]\n"
        f"Cinematic photography look: 85mm lens, shallow depth of field, volumetric god rays, warm golden-hour color grading with teal-navy shadows, soft film grain, subtle lens flare, rich detail, high dynamic range. Realistic skin, hands and fabric. No distorted faces or fingers.\n\n"
        f"LIGHTING: strong warm rim light on the figure, bright glowing sky, gentle dark vignette at the top 15% and bottom 38% so the text pops with high contrast.\n\n"
        f"TYPOGRAPHY (spell EXACTLY, ALL CAPS, ultra-heavy bold sans-serif like Montserrat Black):\n"
        f"- Top badge pill with a thin gold outline and dark navy fill: \"{clean_t}\"\n"
        f"- Main headline in the lower third, two lines: \"{line1}\" in glowing 3D metallic gold gradient (bright yellow to deep amber) with a thick black outline, bevel, inner highlight and soft outer glow, then \"{line2}\" in pure white with a thick black outline and a soft shadow\n"
        f"- Verse reference under it in gold: \"{clean_v}\"\n"
        f"- A glossy gold rounded button: \"PRAY THIS NOW\"\n"
        f"- A small row at the bottom: a red \"SUBSCRIBE\" pill with a bell icon, a thumbs-up icon with \"LIKE IF YOU BELIEVE\", and \"COMMENT AMEN\"\n\n"
        f"LAYOUT: Jesus' face in the upper-middle with nothing covering it. The headline is large enough to read at thumbnail size and sits over a clean darkened area. A thin double gold border with rounded corners around the frame.\n\n"
        f"RULES: no other text, logos or watermarks. Every letter spelled correctly."
    )

=== FILE: renderer.py ===

# renderer.py
import subprocess
import re
from pathlib import Path
from analyzer import BookClip

BGM_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "bg_music.mp3"

def timestamp_to_seconds(ts: str) -> float:
    parts = [float(p) for p in ts.strip().split(":")]
    if len(parts) == 3:
        return parts[0] * 3600 + parts[1] * 60 + parts[2]
    elif len(parts) == 2:
        return parts[0] * 60 + parts[1]
    return parts[0]

def clean_filename(text: str) -> str:
    clean = re.sub(r'[\/*?:"<>|]', "", text)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", "_", clean).strip("_")[:45]

def render_short_with_badge(input_video: Path, clip: BookClip, output_path: Path):
    w, h = 1080, 1920
    start_sec = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.start_time)
    end_sec = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.end_time)
    duration = max(1.0, end_sec - start_sec)

    # Sanitize book title for FFmpeg drawtext
    clean_book = clip.book_title.replace(":", " -").replace("'", "")[:28]
    badge_text = f"Book: {clean_book} (Link in Bio)"

    print(f"\n⚡ [Renderer] Rendering: '{clip.title}' with badge [{badge_text}]...")

    filter_complex = (
        f"[0:v]setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,scale=-2:{h}:flags=lanczos,crop={w}:{h}:(in_w-{w})/2:0,"
        f"unsharp=5:5:0.85:5:5:0.0,eq=contrast=1.05:saturation=1.08[v_base];"
        # 1. Gold Progress Bar at bottom
        f"[v_base]drawbox=x=0:y=1902:w='min({w}, {w}*t/{duration})':h=14:color=0xFFCC00@1:t=fill[v_bar];"
        # 2. Sleek semi-transparent black pill box for the Book CTA
        f"[v_bar]drawbox=x=(in_w-850)/2:y=180:w=850:h=70:color=black@0.65:t=fill[v_box];"
        # 3. Dynamic Book Name Text inside the pill
        f"[v_box]drawtext=text='📖 {badge_text}':fontcolor=white:fontsize=32:x=(w-text_w)/2:y=198[vout];"
        # 4. Audio Ducking / Mixing
        f"[0:a]asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo[speech];"
        f"[1:a]volume=0.05,aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100:channel_layouts=stereo[bgm];"
        f"[speech][bgm]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:dropout_transition=2[aout]"
    )

    cmd = [
        "ffmpeg", "-y",
        "-ss", str(start_sec),
        "-t", str(duration),
        "-i", str(input_video),
        "-stream_loop", "-1",
        "-i", str(BGM_FILE),
        "-filter_complex", filter_complex,
        "-map", "[vout]",
        "-map", "[aout]",
        "-c:v", "libx264",
        "-preset", "veryfast",
        "-crf", "19",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-b:a", "192k",
        str(output_path)
    ]

    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    if res.returncode != 0:
        print(f"❌ FFmpeg Error: {res.stderr[-300:]}")
        return False
    return True

import os

import pickle

from yt_dlp.utils import download_range_func

from google import genai
from google.genai import types
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

# Google API client for YouTube Upload
try:
    from google_auth_oauthlib.flow import InstalledAppFlow

# CONFIGURATION

AUTO_UPLOAD_TO_YOUTUBE = True

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

client = genai.Client(api_key=API_KEY)

# --- 1. SCHEMAS ---
class BookClip(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(description="Catchy YouTube Short title under 60 chars")
    start_time: str = Field(description="Start timestamp HH:MM:SS or MM:SS")
    end_time: str = Field(description="End timestamp HH:MM:SS or MM:SS")
    book_title: str = Field(description="Exact book title discussed")
    book_author: str = Field(description="Author of the book")
    hook_caption: str = Field(description="1 punchy sentence summarizing the insight")
    hashtags: list[str] = Field(description="5 viral tags, e.g. ['#Wisdom', '#Books', '#Shorts']")

class AnalysisResponse(BaseModel):
    clips: list[BookClip]

# --- 2. UTILITIES ---
def clean_filename(text: str) -> str:
    clean = re.sub(r'[\/*?:"<>|]', "", text)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", "_", clean).strip("_")[:35]

def timestamp_to_seconds(ts: str) -> float:
    parts = [float(p) for p in ts.strip().split(":")]
    if len(parts) == 3:
        return parts[0] * 3600 + parts[1] * 60 + parts[2]
    elif len(parts) == 2:
        return parts[0] * 60 + parts[1]
    return parts[0]

def ensure_bgm():
    if not BGM_FILE.exists():
        print("\n[Audio] Downloading ambient track...")
        url = "https://ia801503.us.archive.org/15/items/ambient-relaxing-music/ambient-calm.mp3"
        try:
            urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, BGM_FILE)
        except Exception:
            cmd = [
                "ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi",
                "-i", "sine=frequency=110:sample_rate=44100",
                "-t", "120", "-af", "volume=0.03,lowpass=f=300",
                str(BGM_FILE)
            ]
            subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)

# --- 3. SCOUT WITH 1,000,000+ VIEWS ---
SEARCH_QUERIES = [
    "ytsearch15:books that changed my life podcast",
    "ytsearch15:Ryan Holiday favorite books",
    "ytsearch15:Chris Williamson best books ever",
    "ytsearch15:Alex Hormozi reading list recommended",
    "ytsearch15:Steven Bartlett best books diary of a ceo",
]

def load_history() -> set:
    if HISTORY_FILE.exists():
        try:
            with open(HISTORY_FILE, "r") as f:
                return set(json.load(f))
        except Exception:
            return set()
    return set()

def save_to_history(video_id: str):
    history = load_history()
    history.add(video_id)
    with open(HISTORY_FILE, "w") as f:
        json.dump(list(history), f, indent=2)

def find_viral_video() -> dict | None:
    history = load_history()
    ydl_opts = {"quiet": True, "extract_flat": True, "skip_download": True}

    print(f"\n🔍 [Scout] Scanning top influencer channels for 1M+ view videos...")
    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
        for query in SEARCH_QUERIES:
            try:
                res = ydl.extract_info(query, download=False)
                for entry in res.get("entries", []):
                    if not entry:
                        continue
                    v_id = entry.get("id")
                    duration = entry.get("duration", 0)
                    view_count = entry.get("view_count") or 0

                    if v_id in history or duration < 180 or duration > 3600:
                        continue

                    if view_count == 0:
                        try:
                            info = ydl.extract_info(f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={v_id}", download=False)
                            view_count = info.get("view_count") or 0
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                    if view_count >= MIN_VIEW_COUNT:
                        print(f"🎯 VIRAL TARGET FOUND: '{entry.get('title')}' ({view_count:,} views)")
                        return {
                            "id": v_id,
                            "url": f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={v_id}",
                            "title": entry.get("title")
                        }
            except Exception:
                continue
    print("⚠️ No new videos found.")
    return None

# --- 4. LIGHTNING TRANSCRIPT FETCH (MULTI-VERSION COMPATIBLE) ---
def get_video_transcript(video_id: str) -> str | None:
    print("⚡ [Fast-Track] Pulling timestamped transcript (0 MB video download)...")
    data = None

    # 1. Try modern API (v1.0+: .fetch())
    try:
        api = YouTubeTranscriptApi()
        if hasattr(api, "fetch"):
            fetched = api.fetch(video_id, languages=["en", "en-US", "en-GB"])
            if hasattr(fetched, "to_raw_data"):
                data = fetched.to_raw_data()
            elif isinstance(fetched, list):
                data = fetched
            else:
                data = list(fetched)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2. Try legacy API (get_transcript)
    if not data:
        try:
            if hasattr(YouTubeTranscriptApi, "get_transcript"):
                data = YouTubeTranscriptApi.get_transcript(video_id, languages=["en", "en-US", "en-GB"])
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not data:
        print("⚠️ Could not extract captions for this video.")
        return None

    formatted = []
    for line in data:
        text = line.get("text", "") if isinstance(line, dict) else getattr(line, "text", "")
        start = line.get("start", 0) if isinstance(line, dict) else getattr(line, "start", 0)
        sec = int(start)
        m, s = divmod(sec, 60)
        h, m = divmod(m, 60)
        ts = f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}" if h > 0 else f"{m:02d}:{s:02d}"
        formatted.append(f"[{ts}] {text}")

    print("✅ Transcript loaded in under 1 second!")
    return "\n".join(formatted)

# --- 5. GEMINI TEXT BRAIN (2 SECONDS) ---
def analyze_transcript_for_books(transcript_text: str, max_clips: int = 2) -> list[BookClip]:
    print("🧠 [Brain] Gemini analyzing text transcript in 2 seconds...")

    prompt = f"""
    You are an elite short-form video editor for a wisdom/book channel.
    Here is the exact timestamped transcript of a podcast.
    Analyze this transcript and extract up to {max_clips} viral moments where a specific book, philosophy, or mental model is explained.

    Rules:
    - Clip length: strictly between 30 and 55 seconds.
    - Identify the exact BOOK TITLE and AUTHOR being discussed.
    - Use the timestamps from the transcript (HH:MM:SS or MM:SS format).
    - Start right at the punchy hook; stop cleanly after the lesson is complete.

    TRANSCRIPT:
    {transcript_text[:120000]}
    """

    candidate_models = ["gemini-3.8-flash", "gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-2.5-flash-lite"]
    response = None

    for model_name in candidate_models:
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=model_name,
                contents=prompt,
                config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
                    response_mime_type="application/json",
                    response_schema=AnalysisResponse,
                ),
            )
            break
        except Exception:
            continue

    if not response:
        return []

    data = json.loads(response.text)
    return [BookClip(**item) for item in data.get("clips", [])]

# --- 6. TARGETED 45-SECOND SLICE DOWNLOAD (3 SECONDS) ---
def download_clip_slice(url: str, start_sec: float, end_sec: float, output_path: Path):
    pad_start = max(0.0, start_sec - 1.5)
    pad_end = end_sec + 1.5
    duration = pad_end - pad_start

    print(f"⚡ [Fast-Track] Downloading ONLY the {round(duration)}s slice (~20 MB)...")

    ydl_opts = {
        "format": "bestvideo[height<=1080][ext=mp4]+bestaudio[ext=m4a]/best[height<=1080]/best",
        "download_ranges": download_range_func(None, [(pad_start, pad_end)]),
        "force_keyframes_at_cuts": True,
        "outtmpl": str(output_path),
        "merge_output_format": "mp4",
        "overwrites": True,
        "quiet": True,
    }

    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
        ydl.download([url])

# --- 7. DESIGNER RENDER ENGINE ---
def render_designer_short(raw_slice: Path, clip: BookClip, output_path: Path) -> bool:
    w, h = 1080, 1920
    start_sec = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.start_time)
    end_sec = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.end_time)
    duration = max(1.0, end_sec - start_sec)

    clean_book = re.sub(r'[\':,"]', '', clip.book_title).upper()[:28]

    font_file = "C\\:/Windows/Fonts/arialbd.ttf"
    if not Path("C:/Windows/Fonts/arialbd.ttf").exists():
        font_file = "C\\:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf"

    print(f"⚡ [Render] Stamping Designer Gold Badge on: '{clip.title}'...")

    # Fast relative seek inside the pre-trimmed 45-second slice
    offset_start = 1.5 if start_sec >= 1.5 else start_sec

    filter_complex = (
        f"[0:v]setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,scale=-2:{h}:flags=lanczos,crop={w}:{h}:(in_w-{w})/2:0,"
        f"unsharp=5:5:0.85:5:5:0.0,eq=contrast=1.06:saturation=1.10[v_base];"
        f"[v_base]drawbox=x=110:y=220:w=860:h=140:color=0x0D0D12@0.88:t=fill[card1];"
        f"[card1]drawbox=x=110:y=220:w=860:h=140:color=0xD4AF37@0.85:t=3[card2];"
        f"[card2]drawbox=x=110:y=220:w=12:h=140:color=0xD4AF37@1:t=fill[card3];"
        f"[card3]drawtext=fontfile='{font_file}':text='FEATURED BOOK':fontcolor=0xD4AF37:fontsize=22:x=145:y=240[txt1];"
        f"[txt1]drawtext=fontfile='{font_file}':text='{clean_book}':fontcolor=white:fontsize=34:x=145:y=274[txt2];"
        f"[txt2]drawtext=fontfile='{font_file}':text='Link in Channel Bio ↗':fontcolor=0xBBBBCC:fontsize=22:x=145:y=322[txt3];"
        f"[txt3]drawbox=x=0:y=1902:w='min({w}, {w}*t/{duration})':h=16:color=0xD4AF37@1:t=fill[vout];"
        f"[0:a]asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100[speech];"
        f"[1:a]volume=0.05,aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=44100[bgm];"
        f"[speech][bgm]amix=inputs=2:duration=first[aout]"
    )

    cmd = [
        "ffmpeg", "-y",
        "-ss", str(offset_start),
        "-t", str(duration),
        "-i", str(raw_slice),
        "-stream_loop", "-1",
        "-i", str(BGM_FILE),
        "-filter_complex", filter_complex,
        "-map", "[vout]",
        "-map", "[aout]",
        "-c:v", "libx264",
        "-preset", "veryfast",
        "-crf", "18",
        "-c:a", "aac",
        "-b:a", "192k",
        str(output_path)
    ]

    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    return res.returncode == 0

# --- 8. YOUTUBE UPLOADER ---
def upload_to_youtube(video_path: Path, title: str, description: str, tags: list[str]) -> str | None:
    if not YOUTUBE_API_AVAILABLE or not CLIENT_SECRET_FILE.exists():
        return None

    scopes = ["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.upload"]
    credentials = None

    if TOKEN_PICKLE.exists():
        with open(TOKEN_PICKLE, "rb") as token:
            credentials = pickle.load(token)

    if not credentials or not credentials.valid:
        if credentials and credentials.expired and credentials.refresh_token:
            credentials.refresh(Request())
        else:
            flow = InstalledAppFlow.from_client_secrets_file(str(CLIENT_SECRET_FILE), scopes)
            credentials = flow.run_local_server(port=0)
        with open(TOKEN_PICKLE, "wb") as token:
            pickle.dump(credentials, token)

    youtube = build("youtube", "v3", credentials=credentials)

    body = {
        "snippet": {
            "title": title[:100],
            "description": description,
            "tags": tags,
            "categoryId": "27"
        },
        "status": {
            "privacyStatus": UPLOAD_PRIVACY,
            "selfDeclaredMadeForKids": False
        }
    }

    media = MediaFileUpload(str(video_path), chunksize=-1, resumable=True, mimetype="video/mp4")
    request = youtube.videos().insert(part="snippet,status", body=body, media_body=media)

    print(f"\n🚀 [YouTube] Publishing '{title}' to @TheDailyWisdom_Hub...")
    response = None
    while response is None:
        status, response = request.next_chunk()
        if status:
            print(f"   Upload progress: {int(status.progress() * 100)}%")

    video_id = response.get("id")
    url = f"https://youtube.com/shorts/{video_id}"
    print(f"🎉 Live on YouTube! URL: {url}")
    return url

# --- 9. ULTRA-FAST PIPELINE ---
def main():
    start_total_time = time.time()
    ensure_bgm()

    target = find_viral_video()
    if not target:
        return

    # Step 1: Pull transcript without downloading video
    transcript = get_video_transcript(target["id"])
    if not transcript:
        save_to_history(target["id"])
        return

    # Step 2: Gemini analyzes text in 2 seconds
    clips = analyze_transcript_for_books(transcript, max_clips=2)
    if not clips:
        print("⚠️ No clear book recommendations found in transcript.")
        save_to_history(target["id"])
        return

    # Step 3: Download ONLY the 45-second chunk & render immediately
    for clip in clips:
        start_s = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.start_time)
        end_s = timestamp_to_seconds(clip.end_time)

        temp_slice = BASE_DIR / "temp_slice.mp4"
        try:
            download_clip_slice(target["url"], start_s, end_s, temp_slice)

            filename = f"{clean_filename(clip.book_title)}_{clean_filename(clip.title)}.mp4"
            out_file = CLIPS_DIR / filename

            success = render_designer_short(temp_slice, clip, out_file)
            if success:
                desc = f"""{clip.hook_caption}

📖 Featured Book: {clip.book_title} ({clip.book_author})
👉 Grab your copy via the link in our channel bio!

{' '.join(clip.hashtags)} #Shorts

---
Disclaimer: As an Amazon Associate, I earn from qualifying purchases at no extra cost to you."""

                print("\n" + "=" * 55)
                print(f"✅ SHORT COMPLETED IN RECORD TIME: {out_file.name}")
                print(f"📌 TITLE: {clip.title}")
                print(f"📖 BOOK: {clip.book_title} ({clip.book_author})")
                print("=" * 55)

                if AUTO_UPLOAD_TO_YOUTUBE:
                    upload_to_youtube(out_file, clip.title, desc, clip.hashtags)
        finally:
            if temp_slice.exists():
                temp_slice.unlink()

    save_to_history(target["id"])
    total_time = round(time.time() - start_total_time, 1)
    print(f"\n⚡ ENTIRE RUN FINISHED IN JUST {total_time} SECONDS!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== FILE: scout.py ===

# scout.py
import json
from pathlib import Path
import yt_dlp

HISTORY_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "processed_history.json"

# Top channels & keywords known for viral book discussions
SEARCH_QUERIES = [
    "ytsearch5:Ryan Holiday favorite life changing books",
    "ytsearch5:Ali Abdaal books that changed my life",
    "ytsearch5:Chris Williamson best books modern wisdom",
    "ytsearch5:Alex Hormozi books you must read",
    "ytsearch5:Steven Bartlett diary of a ceo best books",
]

def load_history() -> set:
    if HISTORY_FILE.exists():
        try:
            with open(HISTORY_FILE, "r") as f:
                return set(json.load(f))
        except Exception:
            return set()
    return set()

def save_to_history(video_id: str):
    history = load_history()
    history.add(video_id)
    with open(HISTORY_FILE, "w") as f:
        json.dump(list(history), f, indent=2)

def find_next_video() -> dict | None:
    """Searches for the first high-quality, unprocessed video."""
    history = load_history()

    ydl_opts = {
        "quiet": True,
        "extract_flat": True,
        "skip_download": True,
    }

    print("\n🔍 [Scout] Scanning top influencer channels for book videos...")

    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
        for query in SEARCH_QUERIES:
            try:
                res = ydl.extract_info(query, download=False)
                entries = res.get("entries", [])
                for entry in entries:
                    if not entry:
                        continue
                    v_id = entry.get("id")
                    title = entry.get("title", "")
                    duration = entry.get("duration", 0)

                    # Criteria: Not already processed & between 4 to 45 mins
                    if v_id not in history and 240 <= duration <= 2700:
                        print(f" Target Found: '{title}' ({entry.get('url')})")
                        return {
                            "id": v_id,
                            "url": f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={v_id}",
                            "title": title
                        }
            except Exception as e:
                print(f" Warning on query '{query}': {e}")
                continue

    print("⚠️ No new candidate videos found. Try updating search queries.")
    return None

def download_candidate_video(url: str, output_path: Path) -> str:
    print(f"\n📥 [Downloader] Downloading master video...")
    ydl_opts = {
        "format": "bestvideo[height<=1080][ext=mp4]+bestaudio[ext=m4a]/best[height<=1080]/best",
        "outtmpl": str(output_path),
        "merge_output_format": "mp4",
        "overwrites": True,
        "quiet": True,
    }
    with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
        info = ydl.extract_info(url, download=True)
        return info.get("title", "Master_Video")

=== FILE: sticker_studio.py ===

import os
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
STICKERS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "custom_media" / "stickers"
STICKERS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def ensure_default_stickers():
    """Generates clean high-res transparent stickers & animated GIFs if empty."""
    # 1. Red Subscribe + Bell Badge
    sub_png = STICKERS_DIR / "subscribe_bell_badge.png"
    if not sub_png.exists():
        im = Image.new("RGBA", (360, 80), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
        d.rounded_rectangle([4, 6, 356, 76], radius=38, fill=(0, 0, 0, 160))
        d.rounded_rectangle([0, 0, 350, 70], radius=35, fill=(220, 38, 38))
        d.text((45, 18), "SUBSCRIBE", fill=(255, 255, 255))
        # Bell icon
        d.polygon([(265, 20), (280, 42), (250, 42)], fill=(255, 255, 255))
        d.ellipse([260, 42, 270, 52], fill=(255, 255, 255))
        im.save(str(sub_png), "PNG")

    # 2. Golden Cross Badge
    cross_png = STICKERS_DIR / "golden_cross_badge.png"
    if not cross_png.exists():
        im = Image.new("RGBA", (140, 140), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
        d.rounded_rectangle([0, 0, 140, 140], radius=28, fill=(9, 14, 25, 230), outline=(225, 185, 55), width=3)
        # Gold cross
        d.rounded_rectangle([58, 22, 82, 118], radius=6, fill=(235, 185, 55))
        d.rounded_rectangle([32, 45, 108, 69], radius=6, fill=(235, 185, 55))
        im.save(str(cross_png), "PNG")

    # 3. Live Animated Pulsing Subscribe Bell GIF
    live_gif = STICKERS_DIR / "live_subscribe_pulse.gif"
    if not live_gif.exists():
        frames = []
        for i in range(12):
            scale = 1.0 + 0.08 * (1.0 if i < 6 else (11 - i) / 6.0)
            fw, fh = int(320 * scale), int(70 * scale)
            f_im = Image.new("RGBA", (360, 90), (0, 0, 0, 0))
            d = ImageDraw.Draw(f_im)
            bx = (360 - fw) // 2
            by = (90 - fh) // 2
            d.rounded_rectangle([bx, by, bx + fw, by + fh], radius=fh // 2, fill=(220, 38, 38))
            d.text((bx + 40, by + int(16 * scale)), "SUBSCRIBE 🔔", fill=(255, 255, 255))
            frames.append(f_im)
        frames[0].save(
            str(live_gif),
            save_all=True,
            append_images=frames[1:],
            duration=80,
            loop=0,
            transparency=0,
            disposal=2
        )

def get_sticker_inventory() -> dict:
    """Returns a dictionary of label -> Path for all available stickers & GIFs."""
    ensure_default_stickers()
    items = {
        "None (No Sticker)": None
    }
    for f in sorted(STICKERS_DIR.iterdir()):
        if f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() in (".png", ".webp", ".gif", ".jpg"):
            is_gif = f.suffix.lower() == ".gif"
            lbl = f"{'✨ [LIVE GIF]' if is_gif else '🖼️ [STICKER]'} {f.stem.replace('_', ' ').title()}"
            items[lbl] = f
    return items

def build_ffmpeg_sticker_filter(
    sticker_path: Path,
    video_dur: float,
    position: str = "Bottom Dock (Centered)",
    timing: str = "Throughout Entire Video"
) -> tuple:
    """Builds the FFmpeg input args and overlay filter for static or animated stickers."""
    if not sticker_path or not sticker_path.exists():
        return [], ""

    is_gif = sticker_path.suffix.lower() == ".gif"
    inputs = ["-ignore_loop", "0", "-i", str(sticker_path.absolute())] if is_gif else ["-i", str(sticker_path.absolute())]

    # Coordinate mapping for 1080x1920
    if "Bottom Right" in position:
        x_expr = "W-w-70"
        y_expr = "H-h-160"
        scale_w = 300
    elif "Top Right" in position:
        x_expr = "W-w-70"
        y_expr = "200"
        scale_w = 160
    else:  # Bottom Dock (Centered)
        x_expr = "(W-w)/2"
        y_expr = "H-h-130"
        scale_w = 380

    # Timing filter
    if "Last 6s" in timing:
        enable_expr = f":enable='gte(t,{max(0.0, video_dur - 6.0):.2f})'"
    elif "First 5s" in timing:
        enable_expr = ":enable='lte(t,5.0)'"
    else:
        enable_expr = ""

    filter_str = f"scale={scale_w}:-1[stk_scaled];[vout][stk_scaled]overlay=x={x_expr}:y={y_expr}{enable_expr}[vout_stk]"
    return inputs, filter_str

=== FILE: viral_researcher.py ===

import os
import re
import json
import collections
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from dotenv import load_dotenv

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
load_dotenv(BASE_DIR / ".env")

# Instant In-Memory Cache (0.001s instant re-renders)
_VIRAL_RESEARCH_CACHE = {}

def clean_ascii(text: str) -> str:
    if not text:
        return ""
    t = (text.replace("’", "'").replace("‘", "'")
             .replace("“", '"').replace("”", '"')
             .replace("—", " - ").replace("…", "..."))
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", re.sub(r"[^\x20-\x7E]", "", t)).strip()

def extract_clean_json(text: str) -> dict | None:
    if not text:
        return None
    start = text.find('{')
    end = text.rfind('}')
    if start != -1 and end != -1 and end > start:
        try:
            return json.loads(text[start:end+1])
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None

def call_gemini_fast(prompt: str, system: str = "") -> dict | None:
    """Ultra-fast direct Gemini call (Bypasses slow models.list). Responds in ~1.5s."""
    key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
    if not key:
        return None

    full_prompt = f"{system}\n\n{prompt}".strip() if system else prompt

    try:
        from google import genai
        from google.genai import types
        client = genai.Client(api_key=key)

        # Direct call to the fastest models (No slow network scanning)
        for model_id in ["gemini-3.7-flash", "gemini-3.6-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"]:
            try:
                res = client.models.generate_content(
                    model=model_id,
                    contents=full_prompt,
                    config=types.GenerateContentConfig(response_mime_type="application/json", temperature=0.7)
                )
                if res and res.text:
                    parsed = extract_clean_json(res.text)
                    if parsed:
                        return parsed
            except Exception:
                continue
    except Exception:
        pass

    return None

HIGH_VOLUME_SEO_POOL = [
    "Christian Motivation", "Bible Verse Today", "Daily Prayer", "Faith Over Fear",
    "Jesus Christ", "Powerful Prayer", "Spiritual Breakthrough", "Gods Promise",
    "Morning Prayer", "Scripture For Sleep", "Holy Spirit", "Christian Faith",
    "Miracle Prayer", "Word Of God", "Christian Shorts", "Jesus Loves You",
    "Bible Study", "Biblical Wisdom", "Peace In The Storm", "God Will Provide"
]

def pack_tags_to_limit(topic: str, ai_tags: list, competitor_keywords: list, max_chars: int = 380) -> list:
    combined = []
    seen = set()
    if isinstance(ai_tags, str):
        ai_tags = [x.strip() for x in re.split(r'[,;]', ai_tags) if x.strip()]
    elif not isinstance(ai_tags, list):
        ai_tags = []
    if isinstance(competitor_keywords, str):
        competitor_keywords = [x.strip() for x in re.split(r'[,;]', competitor_keywords) if x.strip()]
    elif not isinstance(competitor_keywords, list):
        competitor_keywords = []
    topic_clean = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9\s]", "", str(topic)).strip()
    priority_tags = [
        topic_clean,
        f"{topic_clean} Prayer",
        f"{topic_clean} Bible Verse",
        f"God on {topic_clean}",
        f"Overcome {topic_clean}"
    ] + competitor_keywords + ai_tags + HIGH_VOLUME_SEO_POOL

    total_len = 0
    final_tags = []
    for raw_tag in priority_tags:
        t = str(raw_tag).replace("#", "").strip().title()
        t_clean = re.sub(r"[<>,#\"'\\`\r\n\t&!?:;]", "", t)
        if len(t_clean) >= 3 and t_clean.lower() not in seen:
            tag_cost = len(t_clean) + 1
            if total_len + tag_cost <= max_chars:
                final_tags.append(t_clean)
                seen.add(t_clean.lower())
                total_len += tag_cost
    return final_tags

TOPIC_HASHTAG_MAP = {
    "debt": ["#DontGiveUp", "#DebtFree", "#BreakEveryDebt", "#FinancialBreakthrough", "#GodWillProvide", "#MiraclePrayer"],
